该论文针对传统强化学习在无人机导航中依赖人工奖励函数和手动调参的问题，提出AgenticRL框架，利用多模态GPT代理自主生成任务特定奖励函数、通过PPO训练策略，并借助诊断反馈循环优化奖励。系统结合真实图像与自然语言任务描述，在推理时自动识别场景并选择合适策略，实现自适应部署。在包括门框穿越、避障、越墙着陆等多任务测试中，该框架相较初始奖励策略性能提升71%，实测成功率91%，仿真到现实迁移准确率94%，展现出良好的泛化能力与自主性。
Using Reward Uncertainty to Induce Diverse Behaviour in Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Anthony GX-Chen, Ankit Anand, Gheorghe Comanici, Zaheer Abbas, Eser Aygün, David Smalling, Shibl Mourad, Doina Precup, André Barreto, Mark Rowland
First: 2026-06-02T17:50:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:50:14+00:00
Comments: Core contributors: Anthony GX-Chen, Ankit Anand, Gheorghe Comanici, André Barreto, Mark Rowland
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classical reinforcement learning (RL) typically seeks a deterministic policy that maximizes the expected sum of a scalar reward. Yet, modern applications such as language model fine-tuning or scientific discovery demand diversity. Existing remedies such as entropy regularization or diversity bonuses often require fragile trade-offs that sacrifice performance for stochasticity or rely on heuristic metrics that can misalign policy rankings. We argue that diversity is more naturally understood as the rational response to uncertainty in the reward. When the reward function is not perfectly known--as is the case with ambiguous preferences or imperfect reward models--committing to a single action can be sub-optimal. Building on this, we propose a fundamental reformulation of the RL objective by replacing the scalar reward with a distribution over reward functions, and applying a non-linear objective over sets of actions. The result is a framework in which calibrated behavioural diversity emerges naturally, remains controllable through the reward function distribution, and is obtained without sacrificing expected reward. Focusing on the contextual bandit setting, we derive a principled gradient estimator for this objective and prove that our formulation naturally generalizes both vanilla policy gradient and more recently developed action-set approaches. Our empirical results demonstrate that this framework offers a robust and theoretically grounded alternative for complex RL tasks where the traditional formulation of the problem fails to induce the desired breadth of agent behaviour.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用奖励不确定性诱导强化学习中的多样化行为
经典强化学习（RL）通常追求最大化期望标量奖励总和的确定性策略。然而，现代应用如语言模型微调或科学发现则需要多样性。现有方法如熵正则化或多样性奖励通常需要脆弱的权衡，牺牲性能换取随机性，或依赖可能与策略排序不一致的启发式指标。我们认为，多样性更自然地理解为对奖励不确定性的理性响应。当奖励函数并非完全已知——如偏好模糊或奖励模型不完美时，坚持单一动作可能非最优。基于此，我们提出对RL目标的根本性重构：用奖励函数分布替代标量奖励，并在动作集合上应用非线性目标。结果是一个框架，其中校准后的行为多样性自然涌现，可通过奖励函数分布控制，且不牺牲期望奖励。聚焦上下文多臂老虎机设置，我们推导出该目标的原理性梯度估计器，并证明该框架自然推广了基础策略梯度及近期发展的动作集合方法。实验证明，该框架为传统问题设定无法诱导所需行为广度的复杂RL任务提供了一种稳健且理论基础坚实的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for diverse agent behavior in modern RL applications like language modeling and scientific discovery, this work proposes treating diversity as a rational response to reward uncertainty rather than imposing it via ad hoc regularization. The method reformulates the RL objective by replacing scalar rewards with a distribution over reward functions and optimizing a non-linear objective over action sets, enabling naturally calibrated diversity without sacrificing expected reward. Experiments in contextual bandits show the framework generalizes vanilla policy gradient and action-set methods, while empirically producing more diverse and robust behavior compared to traditional approaches.
为满足语言模型微调和科学发现等现代RL任务对行为多样性的需求，本文提出将多样性视为对奖励不确定性的理性响应，而非依赖熵正则化或启发式奖励增益。方法通过将标量奖励替换为奖励函数分布，并在动作集合上优化非线性目标，使多样性自然涌现且可控，同时不牺牲期望奖励。在上下文多臂老虎机实验中，该框架有效推广了标准策略梯度和动作集方法，实现了高表现且多样化的策略行为，且无性能损失。
Position: Adversarial ML for LLMs Is Not Making Any Progress
Authors: Javier Rando, Jie Zhang, Nicholas Carlini, Florian Tramèr
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-02-04T12:17:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:48:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026 Position Paper Track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the past decade, considerable research effort has been devoted to securing machine learning (ML) models that operate in adversarial settings. Yet, progress has been slow even for simple "toy" problems (e.g., robustness to small adversarial perturbations) and is often hindered by non-rigorous evaluations. Today, adversarial ML research has shifted towards studying larger, general-purpose language models. In this position paper, we argue that the situation is now even worse: in the era of LLMs, the field of adversarial ML studies problems that are (1) less clearly defined, (2) harder to solve, and (3) even more challenging to evaluate. As a result, we caution that yet another decade of work on adversarial ML may be failing to produce meaningful progress.
中文标题/摘要
标题：立场：针对大语言模型的对抗性机器学习研究毫无进展
过去十年，大量研究致力于在对抗环境下运行的机器学习模型的安全性。然而，即使对于简单的“玩具”问题（如对微小对抗扰动的鲁棒性），进展也十分缓慢，且常因非严谨的评估而受阻。如今，对抗性机器学习研究已转向研究更大、更通用的语言模型。本文立场认为，当前情况更糟：在大语言模型时代，该领域研究的问题（1）定义更模糊，（2）更难解决，（3）评估也更困难。因此，我们警告，再过一个十年，对抗性机器学习研究可能仍无法取得实质性进展。
Summary / 总结
This position paper argues that adversarial machine learning research on large language models has not made meaningful progress, despite decades of effort. The authors contend that problems in this domain are now less clearly defined, harder to solve, and even more difficult to evaluate than in earlier, simpler settings. They highlight that the shift toward LLMs has exacerbated these challenges, and warn that continued investment may yield little advancement without fundamental methodological changes.
本文指出，针对大语言模型的对抗机器学习研究在过去十年中并未取得实质性进展。作者认为，当前研究的问题定义更模糊、解决难度更大、评估也更困难，且依赖非严谨的基准测试。他们警告，若不进行根本性的方法论调整，未来十年的努力可能收效甚微。
Efficient ASR Training with Conversations that Never Happened
Authors: Máté Gedeon, Péter Mihajlik
First: 2026-06-02T17:46:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:46:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conversational ASR for lower-resource languages and niche domains is limited by the scarcity of domain-matched multi-speaker training data. We propose an augmentation pipeline that generates scenario-level dialogues with participant metadata, maps speaker attributes to TTS voice profiles, and assembles synthesized utterances into speaker-aware simulated conversations. We evaluated five LLM families under single-generator, fixed-budget mixture, and scale-up settings using the same FastConformer-Large training recipe for each one. We ran comprehensive evaluations on the Hungarian BEA-Dialogue benchmark corpus, with the method itself being applicable to any language given the resources for each component. The results show that synthetic conversations consistently improve speech recognition performance, but generator choice and data composition strongly affect the gains. Our largest training configuration, using only 67 hours of real conversations and 636 hours of simulated data, achieves better performance on the evaluation benchmark than a zero-shot model trained on 2700 hours of Hungarian speech. These findings indicate that LLM-generated conversational data synthesized with TTS is a practical complement to real conversational corpora for speech model training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用从未发生过的对话高效训练ASR
低资源语言和特定领域的对话式ASR受限于缺乏匹配领域的多说话人训练数据。我们提出一种增强流程，生成包含参与者元数据的场景级对话，将说话人属性映射到TTS语音配置，并将合成语句组装成具有说话人感知的模拟对话。我们使用相同的FastConformer-Large训练方案，在单生成器、固定预算混合和扩展规模设置下评估了五类LLM。我们在匈牙利BEA-Dialogue基准语料库上进行了全面评估，该方法在具备各组件资源的前提下可适用于任何语言。结果表明，合成对话持续提升语音识别性能，但生成器选择和数据组成显著影响增益。我们最大的训练配置仅使用67小时真实对话和636小时模拟数据，在评估基准上表现优于在2700小时匈牙利语音上训练的零样本模型。这些发现表明，由LLM生成并经TTS合成的对话数据是真实对话语料的有效补充，可用于语音模型训练。
Summary / 总结
To address the shortage of domain-specific multi-speaker data for conversational ASR in low-resource languages, the paper introduces a synthetic data augmentation pipeline that generates simulated dialogues using LLMs, maps speaker attributes to TTS voices, and constructs speaker-aware conversations. Evaluated across five LLM families under varying training configurations on the Hungarian BEA-Dialogue corpus, the method consistently improved ASR performance, with the largest setup—using only 67 hours of real data and 636 hours of synthetic data—outperforming a zero-shot model trained on 2700 hours of real speech. The results demonstrate that LLM-generated, TTS-synthesized conversations can effectively supplement real corpora, especially when real data is scarce.
为解决低资源语言中对话式ASR缺乏领域匹配多说话人数据的问题，该论文提出一种合成数据增强流程，利用LLM生成场景级对话，将说话人属性映射到TTS语音，并构建说话人感知的模拟对话。在五类LLM家族下进行多组实验，结果表明合成对话能持续提升ASR性能。尤其在仅用67小时真实对话和636小时合成数据训练的配置中，模型表现优于使用2700小时真实匈牙利语数据训练的零样本模型，证明LLM生成的合成对话可有效补充真实语料用于训练。
CoMPAS3D: A Dataset and Benchmark for Interactive Motion
Authors: Bermet Burkanova, Yasaman Etesam, Payam Jome Yazdian, Trinity Evans, Chuxuan Zhang, Zoe Stanley, Paige Tuttösí, Angelica Lim
First: 2025-07-25T21:33:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:45:14+00:00
Comments: https://rosielab.github.io/compas3d
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Socially interactive humanoid robots must engage with humans through their bodies, adapting in real time to a partner's movement, intent, and abilities. This requires models that understand not just how bodies move, but what movement means in a shared social context. Yet evaluation frameworks for interactive motion generation do not measure whether generated follower motion is legible within a shared movement vocabulary, nor whether it is appropriate to the partner's proficiency level. This gap has two causes: existing frameworks rely on kinematic metrics such as FID and beat alignment that cannot measure either property, and existing datasets lack the move annotations and proficiency variation needed. Salsa is well-suited as an evaluation domain: improvised, dyadic, and governed by a move vocabulary and judging criteria covering timing, musicality, technique, difficulty, partnering, and originality. We present CoMPAS3D, a motion capture dataset of improvised partner salsa paired with an evaluation framework covering kinematic quality, two objective metrics (move legibility and proficiency appropriateness), and six competition-based subjective dimensions. The dataset includes 3 hours of improvisation by 18 dancers spanning beginner, intermediate, and professional levels, with over 2,800 expert-annotated segments covering move types, errors, and stylistic elements. We define three benchmarks: move classification (analogous to transcription), proficiency estimation (fluency assessment), and follower generation (dialogue response). Fine-tuned vision-language models perform strongly on objective metrics applied to ground-truth motion sequences. Applied to Duolando and InterGen, the metrics reveal failures that kinematic metrics miss. Human evaluations confirm the gap between generated and ground-truth motion. CoMPAS3D, annotations, benchmark code, and baseline results are publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoMPAS3D：用于交互式运动的基准数据集
社交互动型人形机器人必须通过身体与人类互动，实时适应伙伴的运动、意图和能力。这需要理解身体运动及其在共享社交语境中含义的模型。然而，现有交互式运动生成评估框架无法衡量生成的跟随运动是否符合共享运动词汇，或是否适合伙伴的熟练程度。这一差距源于两点：现有框架依赖如FID和节拍对齐等运动学指标，无法衡量上述属性；现有数据集缺乏所需的动作标注和熟练度变化。萨尔萨舞是理想的评估领域：即兴、双人互动，且受动作词汇和评判标准约束，涵盖节奏、音乐性、技巧、难度、搭档配合和原创性。我们提出CoMPAS3D，包含即兴搭档萨尔萨舞的动作捕捉数据集及评估框架，涵盖运动学质量、两项客观指标（动作可读性与熟练度适配性）和六项基于竞赛的主观维度。数据集包含18名舞者（初学者、中级、专业级）3小时即兴表演，超过2800个专家标注片段，涵盖动作类型、错误及风格元素。我们定义三个基准：动作分类（类比转录）、熟练度估计（流畅性评估）和跟随生成（对话响应）。微调的视觉-语言模型在应用于真实运动序列的客观指标上表现优异。应用于Duolando和InterGen时，这些指标揭示了运动学指标无法捕捉的失败。人工评估证实生成运动与真实运动之间存在差距。CoMPAS3D、标注、基准代码及基线结果已公开。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of evaluation frameworks for interactive motion that assess both legibility within a shared movement vocabulary and appropriateness to a partner’s proficiency, the authors introduce CoMPAS3D: a motion capture dataset of improvised partner salsa with expert annotations and a multi-dimensional benchmark. The method combines kinematic metrics with two objective measures—move legibility and proficiency appropriateness—and six subjective competition-based dimensions. Evaluated on three benchmarks—move classification, proficiency estimation, and follower generation—the dataset reveals that fine-tuned vision-language models perform well on objective metrics but fail on subjective and contextual dimensions, as shown by human evaluations comparing generated motion to ground-truth. The results highlight critical gaps in current motion generation systems that kinematic metrics alone cannot capture.
为填补现有交互式运动评估框架无法衡量动作可理解性与技能适配性的空白，作者构建了CoMPAS3D数据集，包含18名舞者在3小时即兴萨尔萨舞中的动作捕捉数据，并配套全面评估体系。该方法结合运动学指标与两个客观指标（动作可理解性、技能适配性）及六个主观竞赛维度。数据集包含超过2800个专家标注片段，覆盖动作类型、错误与风格元素。实验表明，微调的视觉-语言模型在客观指标上表现良好，但在主观维度上与真实人类动作存在明显差距，凸显仅依赖运动学指标的评估体系不足。人工评估进一步证实，生成动作在语境适配与表现力上远不及真实人类互动。
Back into Plato's Cave: Examining Cross-modal Representational Convergence at Scale
Authors: A. Sophia Koepke, Daniil Zverev, Shiry Ginosar, Alexei A. Efros
First: 2026-04-20T17:56:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:45:12+00:00
Comments: Project page: http://akoepke.github.io/cave_umwelten/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests that neural networks trained on different modalities (e.g., text and images) align and eventually converge toward the same representation of reality. If true, this has significant implications for whether modality choice matters at all. We show that the experimental evidence for this hypothesis is fragile and depends critically on the evaluation regime. Alignment is measured using mutual nearest neighbors on small datasets ($\approx$1K samples) and degrades substantially as the dataset is scaled to millions of samples. The same behavior is observed beyond text-image, for text-audio and text-video alignment. The alignment that remains between model representations reflects coarse semantic overlap rather than consistent fine-grained structure. Moreover, the evaluations in Huh et al. are done in a one-to-one image-caption setting, a constraint that breaks down in realistic many-to-many settings and further reduces measured alignment. We also find that the reported trend of stronger language models increasingly aligning with vision does not appear to hold for newer models. Overall, our findings suggest that the current evidence for cross-modal representational convergence is considerably weaker than subsequent works have taken it to be. Models trained on different modalities may learn equally rich representations of the world, just not the same one.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重返柏拉图的洞穴：大规模考察跨模态表征收敛性
柏拉图表征假说认为，不同模态（如文本与图像）训练的神经网络会趋于对齐并最终收敛至对现实的相同表征。若成立，这将影响模态选择是否重要的判断。我们发现该假说的实证证据脆弱，高度依赖评估环境。在小数据集（约1K样本）上，通过互近邻测量对齐度，但当数据集扩展至百万样本时，对齐度显著下降。此现象不仅存在于文本-图像对齐，也出现在文本-音频和文本-视频对齐中。剩余的表征对齐仅反映粗粒度语义重叠，而非一致的细粒度结构。此外，Huh等人的评估在一对一图像-标题设置下进行，而在现实中的多对多场景中该约束失效，进一步降低测量到的对齐度。我们还发现，报告中语言模型越强越趋向视觉表征的趋势，对新模型不成立。总体而言，我们的研究结果表明，当前跨模态表征收敛性的证据远弱于后续研究所认为的那样。不同模态训练的模型可能学习到同样丰富的世界表征，但并非同一表征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the Platonic Representation Hypothesis that neural networks across modalities converge toward shared representations of reality, this study investigates whether such alignment holds at scale. Using mutual nearest neighbors as a metric, it finds that alignment degrades significantly when scaling from small (≈1K) to large (millions) datasets, and this pattern persists across text-image, text-audio, and text-video pairs. The remaining alignment reflects only coarse semantic overlap, not fine-grained structural consistency, and is further weakened in many-to-many settings compared to the one-to-one evaluations used in prior work. Crucially, the trend of increasing alignment between language and vision models with model size does not hold for newer architectures, suggesting cross-modal convergence is less robust than previously assumed.
基于‘柏拉图洞穴’假设——即不同模态的神经网络会收敛到对现实世界的共同表征——本研究检验了该假设在大规模数据下的有效性。通过互近邻方法评估对齐度，发现从千级到百万级数据规模时，跨模态对齐显著下降，且该现象在图文、文音、文视三类模态中均存在。剩余对齐仅反映粗粒度语义重叠，而非细粒度结构一致性，且在多对多场景下比一对一评估更弱。更重要的是，语言模型越强越趋向视觉表征的趋势在新模型中不成立，表明跨模态表征收敛性远不如前人所认为的那样稳固。
VLESA: Vision-Language Embodied Safety Agent for Human Activity Monitoring
Authors: Hanjiang Hu, Yiyuan Pan, Jiaxing Li, Xusheng Luo, Alexander Robey, Na Li, Yebin Wang, Changliu Liu
First: 2026-06-02T17:42:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:42:17+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 5 tables, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As AI systems increasingly assist humans in physical tasks, ensuring safety becomes paramount -- physical actions carry immediate and irreversible consequences that digital errors do not. We introduce the Vision-Language Embodied Safety Agent (VLESA), a framework that monitors human activities from egocentric video and triggers real-time safety interventions when dangerous actions are predicted. VLESA addresses intent-dependent safety where identical actions can be safe or dangerous depending on context. A dataset pairing egocentric frames with goal-conditioned safety annotations is introduced, enabling a goal-conditioned safety Q-filter trained via GRPO that evaluates actions with respect to inferred intent without retraining. On top of that, an intent-action prediction agent is proposed to jointly infer goals and predict future actions from video. On the ASIMOV-2.0 benchmark, VLESA achieves higher intervention accuracy at the exact ground-truth frame compared to baselines, while the GRPO-trained Q-filter improves action safety by over 41 percentage points through goal-conditioned constrained decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/HanjiangHu/VLESA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VLESA：面向人类活动监控的视觉-语言具身安全代理
随着AI系统越来越多地协助人类执行物理任务，确保安全变得至关重要——物理行为具有即时且不可逆的后果，而数字错误则没有。我们提出了视觉-语言具身安全代理（VLESA），该框架通过第一人称视频监控人类活动，并在预测到危险行为时触发实时安全干预。VLESA解决了依赖意图的安全问题，即相同的行为在不同情境下可能安全或危险。我们引入了一个配对第一人称视频帧与目标条件安全标注的数据集，支持通过GRPO训练的目标条件安全Q滤波器，该滤波器可基于推断出的意图评估动作，无需重新训练。此外，我们提出了一种意图-动作预测代理，可联合推断目标并从视频中预测未来动作。在ASIMOV-2.0基准测试中，VLESA在精确的地面真实帧上比基线方法具有更高的干预准确率，而通过目标条件约束解码训练的Q滤波器使动作安全性提升了41个百分点以上。代码见https://github.com/HanjiangHu/VLESA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ensure physical safety in human-AI collaboration, VLESA introduces a vision-language embodied agent that monitors egocentric video to detect dangerous human activities and trigger real-time interventions. It employs a goal-conditioned safety Q-filter trained via GRPO to evaluate actions based on inferred intent without retraining, and a joint intent-action prediction agent to forecast future actions from video. On the ASIMOV-2.0 benchmark, VLESA achieves higher intervention accuracy at ground-truth frames than baselines, and the GRPO-based Q-filter improves action safety by over 41 percentage points through constrained decoding conditioned on inferred goals.
随着AI系统越来越多地协助人类执行物理任务，确保安全变得至关重要，因为物理动作的后果是即时且不可逆的。VLESA（视觉-语言具身安全代理）通过第一人称视频监控人类活动，并在预测到危险动作时触发实时安全干预。它通过引入基于目标的安全Q滤波器（使用GRPO训练），在不重新训练的情况下根据推断意图评估动作，同时提出一个意图-动作预测代理，联合推断目标并预测未来动作。在ASIMOV-2.0基准测试中，VLESA在真实帧上的干预准确率高于基线方法，且GRPO训练的Q滤波器通过目标条件约束解码使动作安全性提升超过41个百分点。
Demo2Tutorial: From Human Experience to Multimodal Software Tutorials
Authors: Zechen Bai, Zhiheng Chen, Yiqi Lin, Kevin Qinghong Lin, Difei Gao, Xiangwu Guo, Xin Wang, Mike Zheng Shou
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-06-02T17:39:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:39:07+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Human experience in digital environments offers a vast, underexplored resource of authentic, untrimmed interactions that contain rich procedural knowledge. We introduce Demo2Tutorial, a framework that transforms this experience captured via screen recordings and interaction logs into structured, multimodal software tutorials for teaching both humans and agents. Demo2Tutorial first collects human experience via a dedicated recorder, then parses raw experience using a multimodal Action Parser to reconstruct perception, action, and intent. A Step Planner then abstracts these steps into hierarchical task graphs representing goals and steps. Finally, a Tutorial Composer transforms the parsed experience into structured, reusable image-text instructions. We evaluate the tutorial generation quality on a new benchmark derived from official software documentation. We further demonstrate that this distilled representation benefits (i) human learning, by automatically generating multimodal tutorials, and (ii) agent learning, by improving downstream GUI-agent planning and generalization. Experiments show Demo2Tutorial produces high-quality tutorials that surpass human-authored ones and significantly outperform baseline methods, while enabling both faster human task completion and improved GUI agent planning, demonstrating that structured tutorials distilled from human experience can serve as effective knowledge representations for advancing both human learning and agent capabilities. Code and data will be available at https://github.com/showlab/Demo2Tutorial.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Demo2Tutorial：从人类经验到多模态软件教程
数字环境中的用户体验蕴含着大量未被充分探索的、真实且未经剪辑的交互数据，其中包含丰富的程序性知识。我们提出了Demo2Tutorial框架，该框架可将通过屏幕录制和交互日志捕获的用户体验转化为结构化、多模态的软件教程，用于教学人类与智能体。Demo2Tutorial首先通过专用录制器收集人类经验，然后利用多模态动作解析器解析原始经验，重建感知、动作与意图；随后，步骤规划器将这些步骤抽象为表示目标与步骤的层次化任务图；最后，教程编排器将解析后的经验转化为结构化、可复用的图文指令。我们在基于官方软件文档构建的新基准上评估教程生成质量，并进一步证明该提炼表示在（i）人类学习中，通过自动生成多模态教程提升学习效果；（ii）智能体学习中，通过提升下游GUI智能体规划与泛化能力。实验表明，Demo2Tutorial生成的教程质量优于人工编写教程，显著优于基线方法，同时加速人类任务完成并提升GUI智能体规划能力，证明从人类经验提炼的结构化教程可作为有效知识表示，推动人类学习与智能体能力的发展。代码与数据将在https://github.com/showlab/Demo2Tutorial公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the untapped procedural knowledge in human digital interactions, Demo2Tutorial transforms raw screen recordings and interaction logs into structured, multimodal software tutorials. It first captures human experience via a dedicated recorder, then uses a multimodal Action Parser to reconstruct perception, action, and intent, followed by a Step Planner that abstracts these into hierarchical task graphs, and finally a Tutorial Composer that generates reusable image-text instructions. Evaluated on a new benchmark derived from official documentation, the framework produces tutorials that surpass human-authored ones and outperform baselines, accelerating human task completion and improving GUI agent planning, demonstrating the efficacy of human experience distilled into structured knowledge for both human and agent learning.
为挖掘人类数字交互中蕴含的丰富程序性知识，Demo2Tutorial将屏幕录制和交互日志转化为结构化、多模态的软件教程。其方法包括：首先采集人类操作经验，再通过多模态动作解析器重建感知、动作与意图，接着用步骤规划器将其抽象为层次化任务图，最后由教程编排器生成可复用的图文指令。在基于官方文档构建的新基准上评估，该框架生成的教程质量优于人工编写版本，显著超越基线方法，同时提升人类任务完成效率与GUI代理的规划能力与泛化表现。
Preference-Calibrated Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Zeyi Liu, Guangyao Liu, Yinuo Qu, Yuquan Xue, Bofang Jia, Chunhua Yang, Weihua Gui, Keke Huang, Ziwei Wang
First: 2026-06-02T17:38:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:38:25+00:00
Comments: Submitted to CoRL2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) improves sample efficiency in real-robot manipulation through online human intervention. However, successful trajectories may include suboptimal actions that deviate from the desired task-execution path and force human intervention. Existing HIL-RL methods typically apply the consistent credit assignment principle to all transitions, uniformly propagating discounted terminal rewards through suboptimal segments, ignoring the actual contribution of each transition to task success. This overestimates Q-values for critic learning and indirectly misguides actor updates toward suboptimal behavior patterns. To this end, we propose PACT, a Preference-calibrated Actor-Critic Training framework that leverages the implicit preference signals induced by intervention to perform credit reassignment on identified suboptimal segments while directly guiding policy training for unbiased critic-actor learning. Specifically, we first design a progress model that learns from human demonstration and identifies suboptimal segments for credit correction. Then, from the human action and resampled policy action at the intervention state, we build preference pairs to define a counterfactual advantage that penalizes Bellman targets of the identified suboptimal segment, enabling directional credit calibration. Moreover, we directly align the policy with human corrective actions in the bounded mean space, providing an additional signal beyond critic-guided updates. Across five real-robot manipulation tasks, PACT improves the average success rate by 24.5% and achieves 1.3 times faster convergence, thereby improving both RL sample efficiency and performance. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/HILRL-A1X-BC05.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于偏好的人机协同强化学习用于机器人操作
人机协同强化学习（HIL-RL）通过在线人类干预提升真实机器人操作的样本效率。然而，成功轨迹可能包含偏离期望任务路径的次优动作，从而引发人类干预。现有HIL-RL方法通常对所有状态转移应用一致的信用分配原则，将折扣后的终端奖励均匀传播至次优段落，忽略各转移对任务成功的实际贡献，导致Q值高估，间接引导策略更新趋向次优行为模式。为此，我们提出PACT（偏好校准的演员-评论家训练框架），利用干预所隐含的偏好信号，对识别出的次优段落进行信用重分配，并直接引导策略训练，实现无偏的评论家-演员学习。具体而言，我们首先设计一个进度模型，从人类演示中学习并识别次优段落以进行信用校正；然后，从干预状态下的人类动作与重采样策略动作构建偏好对，定义反事实优势，惩罚已识别次优段落的贝尔曼目标，实现定向信用校准。此外，我们直接在有界均值空间中对齐策略与人类修正动作，提供超越评论家引导更新的额外信号。在五个真实机器人操作任务中，PACT平均成功率提升24.5%，收敛速度提升1.3倍，从而同时提升强化学习的样本效率与性能。代码见https://anonymous.4open.science/r/HILRL-A1X-BC05。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of suboptimal actions in human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning that unnecessarily trigger human intervention, the authors propose PACT, a preference-calibrated actor-critic framework that recalibrates credit assignment by leveraging implicit human preference signals. The method first identifies suboptimal segments using a progress model trained on human demonstrations, then constructs preference pairs from human and resampled policy actions to define a counterfactual advantage that adjusts Bellman targets for those segments. Additionally, it directly aligns the policy with human corrective actions in a bounded mean space to guide learning. Experimental results across five real-robot manipulation tasks show that PACT improves average success rate by 24.5% and achieves 1.3x faster convergence compared to baseline methods, enhancing both sample efficiency and performance.
为解决人类干预频繁且常因次优动作触发的问题，作者提出PACT框架，通过利用人类干预隐含的偏好信号对信用分配进行校准。该方法首先通过人类演示训练进度模型以识别次优片段，再基于人类动作与重采样策略动作构建偏好对，定义反事实优势以调整次优片段的贝尔曼目标。同时，它在有界均值空间中直接对齐策略与人类修正动作，提供额外引导。在五个真实机器人操作任务中，PACT使平均成功率提升24.5%，收敛速度提升1.3倍，显著提高样本效率与性能。
A Pocket Offline Model for Simultaneous Speech Translation as CUNI Submission to IWSLT 2026
Authors: Aziz Sharipov Ortega, Dominik Macháček
First: 2026-06-02T17:37:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:37:11+00:00
Comments: IWSLT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We implement simultaneous translation capability with the offline direct speech-to-text translation model Canary, using the state-of-the-art policy AlignAtt, and submit it to IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Shared task for Czech to English and English to German and Italian. The strengths of our system are: (1) high translation quality, outperforming similarly sized baselines both in low- and high-latency regimes in computationally unaware simulations; (2) low computational requirements, as the model has only 1B parameters; (3) multilinguality -- support of 25 source and 25 target languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：作为IWSLT 2026提交的便携式离线模型，用于同步语音翻译
我们使用最新的策略AlignAtt，基于离线直接语音转文本翻译模型Canary，实现了同步翻译功能，并提交至IWSLT 2026同步语音翻译共享任务（捷克语至英语、英语至德语和意大利语）。 本系统的优势包括：(1) 高翻译质量，在计算感知不敏感的低延迟和高延迟模拟中均优于同等规模的基线模型；(2) 低计算需求，模型仅含10亿参数；(3) 多语言支持——支持25种源语言和25种目标语言。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, high-quality simultaneous speech translation in real-world settings, this work adapts the offline direct speech-to-text model Canary to support simultaneous translation using the AlignAtt policy, submitted to the IWSLT 2026 shared task for Czech-to-English, English-to-German, and English-to-Italian. The method leverages a compact 1-billion-parameter model to balance performance and computational efficiency. Experimental results show the system achieves superior translation quality compared to similarly sized baselines across both low- and high-latency conditions in simulations, while maintaining low resource demands and supporting 25 source and 25 target languages.
为满足现实场景中高效高质量同声传译的需求，本研究将离线直接语音转文本模型Canary适配为支持同声传译的系统，采用AlignAtt策略并提交至IWSLT 2026共享任务（捷克语-英语、英语-德语、英语-意大利语）。该方法基于仅10亿参数的轻量模型，在保证低计算开销的同时实现优异翻译质量，仿真测试中在低延迟与高延迟环境下均优于同等规模基线系统，并支持25种源语言与25种目标语言。
MLSkip: Data Skipping for ML Filters via Lightweight Metadata
Authors: Mihail Stoian, Mark Gerarts, Pascal Ginter, Andreas Zimmerer, Jan Van den Bussche, Andreas Kipf
First: 2026-06-02T17:36:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:36:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Database vendors recently released AI functions that can be used in filter predicates. As such functions often rely on costly, black-box ML models, they unveil new data management challenges. Concretely, traditional data skipping techniques for integer and string data fail to be applicable to the new filter type. Indeed, there is no known mechanism for pruning non-qualifying row groups, e.g., when reading files from blob storage. In this work, we initiate the study of data skipping techniques for ML filters. We make the case that Parquet's default min-max metadata is enough to enable pruning. To this end, we draw connections to two lines of research: (i) the recently proposed query language for ML models and (ii) neural network verification. Our preliminary results on ReLU architectures show that on tables from TPC-H and TPC-DS, the average pruning effectiveness for filters of selectivity below 0.1% amounts to 27.4%. Finally, inspired by research on spatial joins, we propose an enhanced metadata structure: a size-bounded 2D convex hull that verification tools can make better use of, increasing the pruning effectiveness to 38.31%, while occupying at most 45 bytes per row group and column pair. We observe an end-to-end speedup of 1.07$\times$ over PyTorch in DuckDB.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MLSkip：通过轻量级元数据实现机器学习过滤器的数据跳过
数据库供应商最近发布了可在过滤谓词中使用的AI函数。由于这些函数通常依赖昂贵且黑盒的机器学习模型，它们带来了新的数据管理挑战。具体而言，传统的针对整数和字符串数据的数据跳过技术无法适用于新类型的过滤器。事实上，目前尚无已知机制用于剪枝不符合条件的行组，例如从Blob存储中读取文件时。 本研究首次探讨了机器学习过滤器的数据跳过技术。我们论证了Parquet默认的最小-最大元数据足以支持剪枝。为此，我们联系了两个研究方向：(i) 最近提出的用于机器学习模型的查询语言，(ii) 神经网络验证。 我们在ReLU架构上的初步结果表明，在TPC-H和TPC-DS表上，选择性低于0.1%的过滤器平均剪枝效果为27.4%。最后，受空间连接研究的启发，我们提出了一种增强的元数据结构：一个大小受限的二维凸包，验证工具可更有效地利用该结构，使剪枝效果提升至38.31%，同时每行组和列对最多占用45字节。我们在DuckDB上观察到相对于PyTorch的端到端加速比为1.07倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rise of AI functions in databases that use costly ML models in filter predicates, this work addresses the lack of effective data skipping for such filters. It proposes leveraging Parquet’s min-max metadata to enable pruning, drawing on insights from ML query languages and neural network verification. Experimental results on ReLU-based models over TPC-H and TPC-DS datasets show 27.4% average pruning effectiveness for filters with selectivity below 0.1%, which improves to 38.31% using a size-bounded 2D convex hull metadata structure, while keeping overhead minimal at 45 bytes per row group and column pair, yielding a 1.07x end-to-end speedup over PyTorch in DuckDB.
为应对数据库中AI函数在过滤条件中使用昂贵黑盒ML模型带来的新数据管理挑战，本文首次研究面向ML过滤器的数据跳过技术。通过结合Parquet默认的min-max元数据、ML查询语言及神经网络验证研究，提出基于ReLU架构的剪枝策略。在TPC-H和TPC-DS数据集上，对选择率低于0.1%的过滤器，平均剪枝效果达27.4%，采用改进的二维凸包元数据结构后提升至38.31%，每行组每列元数据开销不超过45字节，最终在DuckDB中实现比PyTorch快1.07倍的端到端性能。
PointAction: 3D Points as Universal Action Representations for Robot Control
Authors: Mutian Tong, Han Jiang, Qiao Feng, Lingjie Liu, Jiatao Gu
First: 2026-06-02T17:30:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:30:50+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://oriontmt.github.io/pointaction/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video-Action Models (VAMs) leverage the broad visual dynamics captured by pre-trained video diffusion models, offering a promising path toward generalizable robot manipulation. However, RGB-only video rollouts are not directly actionable: they leave metric 3D motion, contact geometry, and fine-grained spatial constraints under-specified, making action grounding ambiguous. Meanwhile, scaling action supervision across diverse tasks and embodiments remains costly. We present PointAction, a framework that bridges video predictions to robot actions through explicit point-based 4D modeling. PointAction fine-tunes a foundation video generation model to jointly predict future RGB frames and dynamic 3D pointmaps, producing temporally consistent 3D motion of task-relevant scene geometry. These point dynamics serve as a structured, embodiment-agnostic action interface, which a diffusion-based action decoder maps to executable robot actions. By using metric 3D point dynamics as the interface between video prediction and control, PointAction reduces the ambiguity of RGB-only action grounding and supports transfer across tasks and embodiments with limited action supervision. Experiments show that PointAction achieves state-of-the-art 4D generation quality on robot scenes, outperforms existing baselines in simulation, and generalizes to two real robot arms unseen during pretraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PointAction：用于机器人控制的3D点作为通用动作表示
视频动作模型（VAMs）利用预训练视频扩散模型捕捉的广泛视觉动态，为通用机器人操作提供了一条有前景的路径。然而，仅RGB视频的 rollout 并非直接可执行：它们未明确指定度量3D运动、接触几何和细粒度空间约束，导致动作定位模糊。同时，跨多样任务和具身形态扩展动作监督仍成本高昂。我们提出 PointAction，通过显式的点基4D建模，将视频预测与机器人动作连接起来。PointAction 微调基础视频生成模型，联合预测未来RGB帧和动态3D点图，生成任务相关场景几何的时序一致3D运动。这些点动态作为结构化、具身无关的动作接口，由基于扩散的动作解码器映射为可执行机器人动作。通过将度量3D点动态作为视频预测与控制之间的接口，PointAction 减少了仅RGB动作定位的模糊性，并支持在有限动作监督下跨任务和具身形态的迁移。实验表明，PointAction 在机器人场景中实现了最先进的4D生成质量，在仿真中优于现有基线，并能泛化到预训练期间未见过的两个真实机器人臂。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to bridge video prediction with actionable robot control, PointAction introduces a method that fine-tunes a video diffusion model to jointly predict RGB frames and 3D pointmaps over time, enabling temporally consistent 3D motion of scene geometry. This point-based 4D representation serves as a structured, embodiment-agnostic action interface, decoded by a diffusion-based action decoder into executable robot commands. Experiments demonstrate that PointAction achieves state-of-the-art 4D generation quality in simulated robot scenes, outperforms existing baselines, and generalizes effectively to real robot arms not seen during pretraining.
为解决仅用RGB视频预测导致的机器人动作模糊问题，PointAction提出通过显式建模4D点动态来连接视频预测与机器人控制。该方法微调视频扩散模型，联合预测未来RGB帧与动态3D点图，再通过扩散式动作解码器转化为可执行动作。实验表明，PointAction在机器人场景中实现领先的4D生成质量，优于现有基线方法，并能在预训练未见的两个真实机械臂上有效泛化，仅需少量动作监督。
SEAOTTER: Sensor Embedded Autoencoding with One-Time Transcode for Efficient Reconstruction
Authors: Dan Jacobellis, Neeraja J. Yadwadkar
First: 2026-06-02T17:28:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:28:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In robotics systems, vast amounts of visual data are easily captured at high resolution using low-cost, low-power hardware. Yet, limited bandwidth and on-device compute resources prevent full utilization when transmitted via conventional codecs like JPEG/MPEG. Newer codecs, like AV1/AVIF, improve the rate-distortion trade-off, but demand far more resources for encoding, impractical without custom ASICs. Recent asymmetric autoencoders deliver high quality under extreme power and bandwidth constraints, but add prohibitive decoding cost and use bespoke formats that ignore decades of infrastructure built around standards like JPEG. To address these limitations, we introduce a compression framework for cloud robotics based on a Sensor Embedded Autoencoder paired with a One-Time Transcode for Efficient Reconstruction (SEAOTTER). Because the sensor, cloud, and consumer stages face very different power and bandwidth budgets, SEAOTTER combines the compactness of a learned latent with the broad usability of a standard JPEG file. Since naive transcoding degrades performance, we propose a learnable JPEG color and quantization transform that enables increased accuracy for global, dense, and vision-language-based perception. Using SEAOTTER, we train both general-purpose and task-aware transcoding pipelines for a pre-trained, frozen encoder. At a compression ratio of 200:1 and compared to AVIF, we observe 7 times faster encoding, 3.5 times faster decoding, and +8% ImageNet top-1 accuracy, while retaining compatibility with JPEG infrastructure. Our code is available at https://github.com/UT-SysML/seaotter .
中文标题/摘要
标题：SEAOTTER：基于单次转码的传感器嵌入式自编码器高效重建框架
在机器人系统中，低成本、低功耗硬件可轻松捕获大量高分辨率视觉数据。然而，受限于带宽和设备端计算资源，传统编解码器（如JPEG/MPEG）无法充分利用这些数据。新编解码器（如AV1/AVIF）虽优化了码率-失真权衡，但编码需求过高，需专用ASIC才可实现。近期的非对称自编码器在极端功耗和带宽约束下可实现高质量重建，但解码成本高昂且使用专用格式，与JPEG等标准构建的基础设施不兼容。为解决上述问题，我们提出基于传感器嵌入式自编码器与单次转码的云机器人压缩框架（SEAOTTER）。由于传感器、云端和终端阶段面临截然不同的功耗与带宽预算，SEAOTTER结合了学习潜空间的紧凑性与标准JPEG文件的广泛可用性。因直接转码会降低性能，我们提出可学习的JPEG色彩与量化变换，以提升全局、稠密及视觉-语言感知的准确性。使用SEAOTTER，我们训练了通用型与任务感知型转码流水线，针对预训练并冻结的编码器。在200:1压缩比下，相比AVIF，我们观察到编码速度提升7倍、解码速度提升3.5倍、ImageNet top-1准确率提升8%，同时保持与JPEG基础设施兼容性。代码开源于https://github.com/UT-SysML/seaotter。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional codecs like JPEG/MPEG and the resource demands of newer codecs such as AV1/AVIF in robotics systems with constrained bandwidth and compute, the authors propose SEAOTTER: a compression framework combining a sensor-embedded autoencoder with a one-time learnable transcode to JPEG. This approach leverages a pre-trained, frozen encoder to generate compact latent representations, then applies a learnable JPEG color and quantization transform to preserve perceptual quality while maintaining compatibility with existing JPEG infrastructure. Experiments show SEAOTTER achieves 200:1 compression with 7x faster encoding, 3.5x faster decoding, and +8% ImageNet top-1 accuracy compared to AVIF, without requiring custom hardware.
为解决机器人系统中传统压缩编码效率低、现代编码器资源需求高的问题，SEAOTTER提出一种传感器嵌入式自编码器与一次性转码相结合的框架，以在极低带宽和计算资源下实现高效重建。该方法利用预训练冻结编码器生成紧凑的潜在表示，并通过可学习的JPEG色彩与量化变换，在保持与现有JPEG基础设施兼容的前提下提升感知质量。实验表明，在200:1压缩比下，SEAOTTER相较AVIF编码速度提升7倍、解码速度提升3.5倍，同时ImageNet top-1准确率提升8%。
FlashbackCL: Mitigating Temporal Forgetting in Federated Learning
Authors: Mubarak A. Ojewale, Adriana E. Chis, Jorge M. Cortes-Mendoza, Bernardo Pulido-Gaytan, Horacio Gonzalez-Velez
First: 2026-06-02T17:28:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:28:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated Learning (FL) of foundation and edge models increasingly targets deployments where client data distributions drift over time, yet existing forgetting-mitigation methods assume each client's distribution is stationary. Flashback, the strongest recent FL method against cross-client (spatial) forgetting, uses monotonically accumulating per-class label counts as a knowledge proxy; this proxy becomes miscalibrated under temporal distribution shift and anchors the global model to an outdated class balance. We formalise temporal forgetting in FL with a per-phase metric isolated from protocol-level fluctuations and propose Flashback Continual Learning (FlashbackCL), a drop-in extension of Flashback with (i) temporally-decayed label counts; (ii) a device-aware replay buffer with Class-Balanced Reservoir Sampling (CBRS); and (iii) server-side active coreset curation on the public distillation set. The results show that FlashbackCL achieves 6.9% to 10.0% relative improvement relative to Flashback, on CIFAR-10 with 50 clients and three controlled temporal shift modes, while simultaneously reducing temporal forgetting by up to 68%. A 5-variant ablation identifies CBRS replay as the critical component. FlashbackCL also improves Flashback by 3.5 points on stationary CIFAR-100, suggesting that class-balanced replay regularises spatial heterogeneity as well as temporal shift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlashbackCL：缓解联邦学习中的时间遗忘
联邦学习（FL）中基础和边缘模型日益面向客户端数据分布随时间漂移的部署场景，但现有遗忘缓解方法假设每个客户端的分布是稳定的。Flashback 是近期针对跨客户端（空间）遗忘最强的 FL 方法，它使用按类别单调累积的标签计数作为知识代理；然而在时间分布漂移下，该代理失准并使全局模型锚定在过时的类别平衡上。我们形式化了 FL 中的时间遗忘，并提出了一种独立于协议级波动的每阶段度量，进而提出 Flashback 连续学习（FlashbackCL），作为 Flashback 的即插即用扩展，包含：（i）时衰减的标签计数；（ii）设备感知的重放缓冲区，采用类别平衡水库采样（CBRS）；（iii）服务器端在公共蒸馏集上的主动核心集筛选。实验表明，在 CIFAR-10 上使用 50 个客户端和三种可控时间漂移模式下，FlashbackCL 相对于 Flashback 实现 6.9% 至 10.0% 的相对提升，同时将时间遗忘降低高达 68%。5 种变体消融实验表明 CBRS 重放是关键组件。此外，FlashbackCL 在静态 CIFAR-100 上使 Flashback 提升 3.5 分，表明类别平衡重放可同时缓解空间异质性与时间漂移。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to address temporal forgetting in federated learning where client data distributions evolve over time, FlashbackCL extends the Flashback method by introducing temporally-decayed label counts, a device-aware replay buffer using Class-Balanced Reservoir Sampling, and server-side active coreset selection. Experiments on CIFAR-10 with 50 clients under three temporal shift scenarios show FlashbackCL improves Flashback by 6.9–10.0% relative performance while reducing temporal forgetting by up to 68%, with ablation confirming CBRS as critical. The method also enhances Flashback by 3.5 points on stationary CIFAR-100, indicating class-balanced replay helps mitigate both temporal and spatial heterogeneity.
为应对联邦学习中客户端数据随时间漂移导致的遗忘问题，本文提出FlashbackCL方法，在Flashback基础上引入时衰减标签计数、设备感知的类平衡回放缓冲区及服务器端主动核心集选择。在CIFAR-10上50客户端三类时间漂移场景下，FlashbackCL相较Flashback提升6.9%至10.0%，同时将时间遗忘降低68%，消融实验表明类平衡回放是关键组件；在静态CIFAR-100上亦提升3.5分，表明类平衡回放可同时缓解时空异质性。
q0: Primitives for Hyper-Epoch Pretraining
Authors: Bishwas Mandal, Shmuel Berman, Akshay Vegesna, Samip Dahal
First: 2026-06-02T17:27:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:27:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-epoch training is becoming the standard now that compute is growing faster than the supply of high-quality text. But pretraining a single model saturates within a few passes, long before the compute budget is exhausted. We argue this calls for a conceptual shift from training a single model toward exploring a population of models and aggregating their predictions. We introduce hyper-epoch pretraining (q0), which turns a multi-epoch budget into a population of diverse models whose combined predictions reach a lower validation loss than a single refined model. q0 reduces to three core primitives. A cyclic schedule with anti-correlated learning rate and weight decay collects diverse models from a few parallel trajectories. Chain distillation trains each model against its predecessor so that model quality compounds across the population. A learned prior, fit on a held out set, selects and weights members for any inference budget. On a 1.8B-parameter model trained on 100M FineWeb tokens, q0 matches a strong 256-epoch ensemble baseline using only ${\sim}56$ epochs (${\sim}4.6\times$ fewer), or ${\sim}67$ epochs (${\sim}3.8\times$ fewer) when matched to the baseline's ensemble size, and continues to improve beyond it. These gains reach cumulative ${\sim}12.9\times$ data efficiency under the Slowrun setting and transfer to downstream benchmarks. Crucially, the optimal allocation shifts with the budget, so we give prescriptive recipes for how to spend a given epoch budget to maximize generalization, from a single epoch up to the largest budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：q0：超周期预训练的基本方法
随着计算能力增长快于高质量文本供应，多周期训练正成为标准。但单个模型在几轮训练后即趋于饱和，远早于计算预算耗尽。我们主张应从训练单个模型转向探索模型群体并聚合其预测。我们引入超周期预训练（q0），将多周期预算转化为一组多样模型，其联合预测的验证损失低于单个优化模型。q0包含三个核心要素：带反相关学习率与权重衰减的循环调度，从少数并行轨迹中收集多样模型；链式蒸馏，使每个模型针对其前驱模型训练，从而在群体中累积模型质量；基于保留数据集学习的先验，用于选择并加权任何推理预算下的模型成员。在1.8B参数模型上，使用1亿个FineWeb token训练，q0仅用约56个周期（约4.6倍更少）或约67个周期（约3.8倍更少）即可匹配256周期集成基线，且在基线集成规模下仍持续提升。这些收益在Slowrun设置下累计达约12.9倍的数据效率，并可迁移到下游基准测试。关键在于，最优分配随预算变化，我们提供从单周期到最大预算的指导性方案，以最大化泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
As compute grows faster than high-quality text supply, multi-epoch training is now standard, yet single-model pretraining saturates early. The paper proposes hyper-epoch pretraining (q0), a method that transforms a multi-epoch budget into a population of diverse models whose combined predictions outperform a single refined model. q0 uses three core primitives: a cyclic schedule with anti-correlated learning rate and weight decay to generate diverse models, chain distillation to compound quality across the population, and a learned prior to select and weight models for any inference budget. On a 1.8B model trained on 100M tokens, q0 achieves validation loss comparable to a 256-epoch ensemble using only ~56 epochs (~4.6x fewer), or ~67 epochs (~3.8x fewer) when matched to ensemble size, and continues improving beyond it. It also shows ~12.9x data efficiency under Slowrun and generalizes to downstream tasks, with optimal model allocation depending on budget size.
鉴于单模型多轮预训练在计算资源充足时性能提前饱和，本文提出超周期预训练（q0）方法，通过构建模型群体并聚合其预测以提升泛化能力。该方法包含三个核心组件：使用反相关学习率与权重衰减的循环调度生成多样模型，链式蒸馏使模型质量在群体中累积，以及基于保留数据集学习的先验选择与加权模型。在1.8B参数模型上，q0仅用约56个周期（约4.6倍更少）即可匹配256周期集成基线，或用约67个周期（约3.8倍更少）匹配集成规模，且性能持续优于基线。在Slowrun设置下，其数据效率提升约12.9倍，并在下游任务中有效迁移，且最优模型分配策略随预算规模动态调整。
Entropy Is Not Enough: Unlocking Effective Reinforcement Learning for Visual Reasoning via Vision-Anchored Token Selection
Authors: Senjie Jin, Peixin Wang, Boyang Liu, Xiaoran Fan, Shuo Li, Zhiheng Xi, Jiazheng Zhang, Yuhao Zhou, Tao Gui, Qi Zhang, Xuanjing Huang
First: 2026-06-02T17:26:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:26:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While token-level entropy is commonly recognized as effective for credit assignment in text-only reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), it remains unclear whether this mechanism still holds in visual reasoning. Our controlled study shows that this mechanism collapses in visual reasoning due to the omission of vision-sensitive tokens with naturally low entropy. Although existing multimodal RL methods increasingly acknowledge the importance of visual perception, they struggle to satisfy the inherent demand for interleaving precise perceptual grounding with semantic reasoning, either lacking systematic visual measurements or overlooking that token entropy primarily drives semantic exploration. To address this, we introduce VEPO (Vision-Entropy token-selection for Policy Optimization), an effective RL framework explicitly integrating visual sensitivity with token entropy via a principled multiplicative coupling, where VEPO redirects gradient credit toward tokens which are simultaneously visually grounded and highly informative. Extensive experiments demonstrate VEPO's leading performance, significantly outperforming the entropy-only baseline by 2.28 points at 7B-scale and 3.15 points at 3B-scale. Ablations further substantiate the soundness of our method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：熵不足：通过视觉锚定的令牌选择解锁视觉推理的有效强化学习
尽管令牌级熵在仅文本的强化学习中常被用于可验证奖励的信用分配，但其在视觉推理中的有效性尚不明确。我们的控制研究表明，由于忽略了自然熵值较低的视觉敏感令牌，该机制在视觉推理中失效。尽管现有多模态强化学习方法日益重视视觉感知，但它们难以满足精确感知锚定与语义推理交织的内在需求，要么缺乏系统的视觉度量，要么忽视令牌熵主要驱动语义探索的本质。为此，我们提出VEPO（用于策略优化的视觉-熵令牌选择），通过原理性的乘法耦合显式整合视觉敏感性与令牌熵，使梯度信用向同时具备视觉锚定与高信息量的令牌倾斜。大量实验表明，VEPO表现领先，在7B规模下比仅熵基线高出2.28分，在3B规模下高出3.15分。消融实验进一步验证了方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that token-level entropy, effective in text-only reinforcement learning, fails in visual reasoning due to neglecting visually sensitive tokens with low entropy, the authors propose VEPO, a reinforcement learning framework that couples visual sensitivity with token entropy multiplicatively to guide gradient credit toward tokens that are both visually grounded and semantically informative. Experiments show VEPO significantly outperforms entropy-only baselines, achieving 2.28 and 3.15 point improvements at 7B and 3B scale respectively, with ablation studies confirming the method's effectiveness.
鉴于文本强化学习中常用的词级熵在视觉推理中失效——因其忽略视觉敏感且熵值低的词，作者提出VEPO框架，通过乘法耦合视觉敏感性与词熵，引导梯度优先流向视觉锚定且语义信息丰富的词。实验表明，VEPO在7B和3B规模下分别比熵基方法高出2.28和3.15分，验证了其在视觉推理任务中的有效性。
Correcting Neural Operator Spectral Bias via Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Sparse Observations
Authors: Niccolò Perrone, Fanny Lehmann, Stefania Fresca, Filippo Gatti
First: 2026-06-02T17:26:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:26:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Neural operator surrogates (NO) approximate PDE solutions orders of magnitude faster than numerical solvers, but suffer from spectral bias: high-frequency content is systematically attenuated, limiting reliability where fine-scale structure matters. Sparse sensor measurements of the field are often available too, offering pointwise accuracy without spectral distortion but covering only a small fraction of the domain. We address this by treating NO predictions as auxiliary observations in a diffusion posterior sampling framework. Our method, FreqNO-DPS (https://github.com/niccoloperrone/FreqNO-DPS), combines an unconditional score-based diffusion prior, trained on high-fidelity simulations, with diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) conditioned on sparse observations and guided by a frozen neural operator. Naive integration reintroduces the surrogate's spectral bias; we resolve this with a closed-form, spectrally shaped guidance score that weights the surrogate by its frequency-dependent accuracy and needs no denoiser backpropagation. A distribution-free analysis bounds the approximation error across the frequency-diffusion-time plane and shows the guidance's frequency dependence is preserved regardless of distributional assumptions. On 3D elastic wavefield prediction at 5% and 2% sensor coverage, the method reaches near-zero spectral bias across all bands, where both the surrogate and sensor-only DPS show systematic high-frequency attenuation. Isotropic guidance, the natural baseline, improves pointwise accuracy but carries the bias into the posterior nearly intact, confirming that frequency-dependent calibration is essential, not merely beneficial. The framework needs only paired surrogate/reference data and exploits no problem-specific structure beyond the residual's approximate spectral diagonality, verifiable for new surrogates via the coherence diagnostic we provide.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过扩散后验采样与稀疏观测纠正神经算子的频谱偏差
神经算子代理（NO）比数值求解器快几个数量级地近似偏微分方程解，但存在频谱偏差：高频成分系统性衰减，限制了在细尺度结构重要的场景下的可靠性。稀疏传感器测量通常可用，提供点精度无频谱失真但仅覆盖域的小部分。我们通过将NO预测视为扩散后验采样框架中的辅助观测来解决此问题。我们的方法FreqNO-DPS（https://github.com/niccoloperrone/FreqNO-DPS）结合了基于高保真模拟训练的无条件分数扩散先验，以及以冻结神经算子引导、条件于稀疏观测的扩散后验采样（DPS）。直接积分会重新引入代理的频谱偏差；我们通过闭式、频谱形状引导分数解决此问题，该分数按频率依赖精度加权代理，无需去噪反向传播。分布无关分析在频谱-扩散-时间平面内界定了近似误差，并表明引导的频率依赖性在不依赖分布假设下仍被保留。在3D弹性波场预测中，5%和2%传感器覆盖率下，该方法在所有频段实现近零频谱偏差，而代理和仅传感器DPS均存在系统性高频衰减。各向同性引导（自然基线）虽提升点精度，但几乎完整保留偏差至后验，证实频率依赖校准至关重要而非仅有益。该框架仅需配对代理/参考数据，且仅利用残差近似频谱对角性这一通用结构，新代理可通过我们提供的相干性诊断验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spectral bias in neural operator surrogates that suppress high-frequency content, this work proposes FreqNO-DPS, a diffusion posterior sampling method that leverages sparse sensor observations to correct this bias. The approach combines a score-based diffusion prior trained on high-fidelity data with a frequency-dependent guidance score that weights the neural operator’s predictions by their frequency-specific accuracy, avoiding denoiser backpropagation. Experimental results on 3D elastic wavefield prediction at 5% and 2% sensor coverage show that FreqNO-DPS achieves near-zero spectral bias across all frequency bands, outperforming both the original neural operator and isotropic diffusion posterior sampling, which preserves bias despite improving pointwise accuracy. The method requires only paired surrogate/reference data and relies on a coherence diagnostic to verify spectral diagonality, making it broadly applicable.
为解决神经算子代理模型在高频成分上存在系统性衰减（频谱偏置）的问题，本文提出FreqNO-DPS方法，利用稀疏场测量数据作为辅助观测，通过扩散后验采样进行校正。该方法结合基于高保真模拟训练的无条件分数扩散先验，以及由冻结神经算子生成的频谱依赖引导分数，该分数按频率加权预测结果，无需去噪反向传播。在3D弹性波场预测任务中，5%和2%传感器覆盖率下，该方法在所有频段实现近零频谱偏置，显著优于原始代理模型和无频谱依赖的等向引导扩散后验采样（后者虽提升点精度但保留偏置）。方法仅需代理模型与参考数据配对，且通过提供的相干性诊断可验证新代理模型的频谱对角性，实现通用性。
Quadratic integrate-and-fire neurons exhibit less fragmented loss landscapes and outperform leaky integrate-and-fire neurons in spike-based gradient descent
Authors: Carlo Wenig, Raoul-Martin Memmesheimer, Christian Klos
First: 2026-06-02T17:26:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:26:12+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 5 figures (main part)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ability to train spiking neural networks is essential for modeling biological neural networks as well as for neuromorphic computing. However, for the extensively used leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) neurons, arbitrarily small parameter changes can induce spike (dis)appearances that disrupt subsequent activity, leading to unstable neural representations and permanently silent neurons during exact spike-based gradient descent. Recent work shows that a class of neuron models, which includes the quadratic integrate-and-fire (QIF) neuron, avoids these discontinuities and enables continuous and even smooth spike-based gradient descent. However, it remains unclear whether these advantages translate into practice. Here, we demonstrate that they do so via a controlled comparison between networks of LIF and QIF neurons on the popular Spiking Heidelberg Digits dataset. Specifically, in a first step, we perform a thorough hyperparameter search to optimize both models, revealing a clear performance advantage of QIF neurons. In a second step, we visualize the loss and gradient landscapes. Consistent with their inferior performance, we find that the loss landscapes of LIF neurons, which are discontinuous, appear more fragmented and the related gradients more erratic. An analysis of the landscapes of single samples indicates that these features arise from changes in the temporal order of spikes, which often cause disruptive spike (dis)appearances. Overall, our results advocate replacing LIF neurons with neuron models exhibiting continuous spiking dynamics, such as QIF neurons, for gradient descent training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：二次积分发放神经元表现出更少的损失景观碎片化，并在基于尖峰的梯度下降中优于漏积分发放神经元
训练脉冲神经网络的能力对于模拟生物神经网络以及神经形态计算至关重要。然而，对于广泛使用的漏积分发放（LIF）神经元，任意微小的参数变化都可能引发尖峰（消失或出现），从而破坏后续活动，导致神经表示不稳定，并在精确的基于尖峰的梯度下降中产生永久沉默的神经元。近期研究表明，一类神经元模型（包括二次积分发放（QIF）神经元）可避免这些不连续性，从而实现连续甚至平滑的基于尖峰的梯度下降。然而，这些优势是否在实践中体现尚不明确。本文通过在流行的Spiking Heidelberg Digits数据集上对LIF与QIF神经元网络进行受控比较，证明了这些优势确实存在。首先，我们进行了全面的超参数搜索以优化两种模型，结果明确显示QIF神经元性能更优。其次，我们可视化了损失与梯度景观。与性能较差相一致，我们发现LIF神经元的损失景观不连续、更碎片化，相关梯度更不稳定。对单样本景观的分析表明，这些特征源于尖峰时间顺序的变化，常导致尖峰（消失或出现）的干扰。总体而言，我们的结果建议在梯度下降训练中，用具有连续尖峰动态的神经元模型（如QIF神经元）替代LIF神经元。

Motivated by the instability of leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) neurons during spike-based gradient descent due to discontinuous spike generation, this study investigates whether quadratic integrate-and-fire (QIF) neurons offer practical advantages. Through controlled experiments on the Spiking Heidelberg Digits dataset, the authors optimized both neuron types and found QIF neurons consistently outperformed LIF neurons. Visual analysis of loss and gradient landscapes confirmed that LIF networks exhibited fragmented, discontinuous landscapes with erratic gradients, while QIF networks showed smoother, more continuous behavior. These differences were traced to spike timing disruptions in LIF models, which caused spike disappearances or appearances, undermining training stability.
为了解决漏积分发放（LIF）神经元在基于尖峰的梯度下降中因尖峰突变导致训练不稳定的问题，本研究探讨了二次积分发放（QIF）神经元是否具有实际优势。在Spiking Heidelberg Digits数据集上进行对比实验并优化超参数后，QIF神经元表现明显优于LIF神经元。损失与梯度景观可视化显示，LIF网络存在碎片化、不连续的景观和剧烈波动的梯度，源于尖峰时序变化引发的尖峰消失或出现，而QIF网络则呈现更平滑连续的景观，支持其在梯度下降训练中更稳定。
PINNfluence: Interpreting PINNs through Influence Functions
Authors: Aleksander Krasowski, Jonas R. Naujoks, Moritz Weckbecker, Galip Ü. Yolcu, Thomas Wiegand, Sebastian Lapuschkin, Wojciech Samek, René P. Klausen
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2024-09-13T16:23:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:25:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful deep learning approach for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) in the physical sciences, yet their behavior remains largely opaque and is typically understood through failure mode analyses rather than explicit interpretability. To address this issue, we introduce PINNfluence, a training data attribution framework for interpreting PINNs based on influence functions. By extending influence functions to composite physics-informed training objectives, we enable fine-grained attribution between predictions, loss components, and training data points. Through benchmark experiments across various PDEs, we demonstrate that influence patterns provide granular diagnostics that distinguish structural characteristics across well-trained and poorly-trained PINNs. PINNfluence thus opens a new avenue for understanding and improving the reliability of PINNs through the lens of their data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PINNfluence：通过影响函数解释物理信息神经网络
物理信息神经网络（PINNs）已成为解决物理科学中偏微分方程（PDEs）的强大深度学习方法，但其行为仍缺乏透明性，通常通过故障模式分析而非明确可解释性来理解。为解决此问题，我们提出了PINNfluence，一种基于影响函数的训练数据归因框架，用于解释PINNs。通过将影响函数扩展至复合物理信息训练目标，我们实现了预测、损失分量与训练数据点之间的细粒度归因。通过在各类PDE上的基准实验，我们证明影响模式提供了区分训练良好与训练不佳的PINNs结构特征的精细诊断。因此，PINNfluence为通过数据视角理解并提升PINNs的可靠性开辟了新途径。

为了解决物理信息神经网络（PINN）缺乏可解释性的问题，本文提出PINNfluence框架，利用影响函数将训练数据对预测和损失项的贡献进行归因。通过将影响函数扩展至复合物理约束目标，该方法实现了对单个数据点如何影响模型行为的细粒度诊断。在多个偏微分方程上的实验表明，影响模式能区分训练良好与训练不佳的PINN的结构差异，为诊断和提升PINN可靠性提供了新途径。
Neural Attention Search Linear: Towards Adaptive Token-Level Hybrid Attention Models
Authors: Difan Deng, Andreas Bentzen Winje, Lukas Fehring, Marius Lindauer
First: 2026-02-03T16:02:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:25:15+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The quadratic computational complexity of softmax transformers has become a bottleneck in long-context scenarios. In contrast, linear attention model families provide a promising direction towards a more efficient sequential model. These linear attention models compress past KV values into a single hidden state, thereby efficiently reducing complexity during both training and inference. However, their expressivity remains limited by the size of their hidden state. Previous work proposed interleaving softmax and linear attention layers to reduce computational complexity while preserving expressivity. Nevertheless, the efficiency of these models remains bottlenecked by their softmax attention layers. In this paper, we propose Neural Attention Search Linear (NAtS-L), a framework that applies both linear attention and softmax attention operations within the same layer on different tokens. NAtS-L automatically determines whether a token can be handled by a linear attention model, i.e., tokens that have only short-term impact and can be encoded into fixed-size hidden states, or require softmax attention, i.e., tokens that contain information related to long-term retrieval and need to be preserved for future queries. By searching for optimal Gated DeltaNet and softmax attention combinations across tokens, we show that NAtS-L provides a strong yet efficient token-level hybrid architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经注意力搜索线性：迈向自适应的词级混合注意力模型
softmax变换器的二次计算复杂度已成为长上下文场景的瓶颈。相比之下，线性注意力模型家族为更高效的序列模型提供了有前景的方向。这些线性注意力模型将过去的KV值压缩为单个隐藏状态，从而在训练和推理过程中高效降低复杂度。然而，其表达能力受限于隐藏状态的大小。先前工作提出交错softmax与线性注意力层，以降低计算复杂度同时保持表达能力。但这些模型的效率仍受限于其softmax注意力层。本文提出神经注意力搜索线性（NAtS-L），一种在同一层内对不同词元同时应用线性注意力和softmax注意力操作的框架。NAtS-L自动判断词元是否可由线性注意力模型处理（即仅具有短期影响、可编码为固定大小隐藏状态的词元），或需softmax注意力（即包含与长期检索相关的信息、需保留以供未来查询的词元）。通过搜索词元间的最优Gated DeltaNet与softmax注意力组合，我们表明NAtS-L提供了一种强大且高效的词级混合架构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of softmax transformers in long-context tasks and the limited expressivity of linear attention models, this paper introduces Neural Attention Search Linear (NAtS-L), a token-level hybrid attention framework that dynamically assigns either linear or softmax attention to each token based on its context dependency. NAtS-L uses a search mechanism to optimize combinations of Gated DeltaNet and softmax attention per token, automatically identifying short-term tokens suitable for linear attention and long-term tokens requiring softmax attention. Experimental results demonstrate that NAtS-L achieves strong performance while significantly reducing computational complexity compared to pure softmax or linear attention models, offering a more efficient and expressive alternative for long-context processing.
为了解决softmax变换器在长上下文场景中的计算瓶颈以及线性注意力模型表达能力受限的问题，本文提出Neural Attention Search Linear（NAtS-L），一种在token级别动态分配线性或softmax注意力的混合架构。NAtS-L在单层内搜索最优的Gated DeltaNet与softmax注意力组合，自动判断哪些token可被压缩为固定大小隐藏状态（使用线性注意力），哪些需保留长程依赖（使用softmax）。实验表明，NAtS-L在保持高性能的同时显著降低计算复杂度，有效平衡了效率与表达能力。
Multi-Robot Bearing-only Pose Estimation via Angle Rigidity
Authors: J. Francisco Presenza, Leonardo J. Colombo, Ignacio Mas, Juan I. Giribet
First: 2026-06-02T17:22:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:22:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This letter proposes a novel distributed bearing-based pose estimator for time-varying multi-robot systems. The method uses angles computed from body-frame bearings to estimate the robots' positions in $\mathbb{R}^3$ without knowledge of their orientations. The orientations in $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ are recovered from the estimated positions, the bearings, and the bearing derivatives. The proposed observer only requires the (directed) sensing topology to be \textit{angle-rigid}, a weaker condition than the commonly used ones like bearing rigidity. Local uniform exponential stability of the proposed observer is established under the assumption of persistently exciting motions for a subset of robots. Simulations are presented and discussed to evaluate the scheme's effectiveness and practicality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过角度刚性实现多机器人仅测角姿态估计
本文提出了一种针对时变多机器人系统的新型分布式仅测角姿态估计器。该方法利用从机体坐标系测得的角度，无需已知机器人姿态即可估计其在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中的位置。机器人在 $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ 中的姿态由估计位置、测角值及其导数恢复。所提出的观测器仅需感知拓扑满足“角度刚性”条件，该条件弱于常用的测角刚性条件。在部分机器人运动持续激励的假设下，证明了该观测器的局部一致指数稳定性。通过仿真评估了该方案的有效性与实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for distributed pose estimation in multi-robot systems without orientation measurements, this paper introduces a bearing-only estimator that leverages angle rigidity in the sensing topology to estimate 3D positions. The method computes angles from body-frame bearings and recovers orientations in SO(3) using estimated positions and bearing derivatives. Under the assumption of persistently exciting motion for a subset of robots, the observer is proven locally uniformly exponentially stable, and simulations confirm its effectiveness and practicality in real-world scenarios.
为解决多机器人系统中无需姿态信息即可实现分布式位姿估计的需求，本文提出一种基于角度刚性的仅测角定位方法。该方法利用机器人间测得的体坐标系方向角，结合角度刚性传感拓扑结构，直接估计机器人在三维空间中的位置，再结合位置、测角及其导数恢复其姿态。在部分机器人运动持续激励的假设下，该观测器被证明具有局部一致指数稳定性，仿真结果验证了其在动态环境中的有效性和实用性。
Neural Network Verification using Partial Multi-Neuron Relaxation
Authors: Ido Shmuel, Guy Katz
First: 2026-05-28T16:15:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:20:50+00:00
Comments: To appear in SAIV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The increasing integration of deep neural networks in critical systems has spawned a theoretical and practical interest in formally guaranteeing safety properties about their behavior. To achieve this, contemporary verification algorithms rely on computing linear relaxations for a network's non-linear activation functions. Existing approaches for linear relaxations typically fall into one of two categories: single-neuron relaxation, in which each activation neuron is bounded in terms of its sources; and multi-neuron relaxation, in which linear bounds involving multiple activation neurons and their sources are calculated. However, existing methods might fail to balance tightness and scalability, as single-neuron bounds might not derive sufficiently tight bounds necessary for verification to complete, whereas generating multi-neuron relaxation for all activation neurons is computationally expensive. In this paper, we present a middle-ground approach featuring partial multi-neuron relaxation, in which we generate multi-neuron bounds for only a small, heuristically selected subset of neurons. To achieve this, we build upon existing branching heuristics for selecting neurons and for optimizing bounding hyper-planes for multi-neuron bounds. We integrated our proposed method within the Marabou verifier, and obtained favorable results in comparison to existing bound tightening methods. Our experiments showcase the potential of our technique for neural network verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用部分多神经元松弛的神经网络验证
深度神经网络在关键系统中的日益集成，激发了对其行为安全属性进行形式化保证的理论与实践兴趣。为实现这一目标，当前验证算法依赖于计算网络非线性激活函数的线性松弛。现有线性松弛方法通常分为两类：单神经元松弛，即每个激活神经元根据其输入源进行界限约束；以及多神经元松弛，即计算涉及多个激活神经元及其输入源的线性界限。然而，现有方法可能难以平衡紧致性与可扩展性：单神经元界限可能无法生成足够紧的界限以完成验证，而为所有激活神经元生成多神经元松弛则计算成本高昂。本文提出一种折中方法——部分多神经元松弛，仅对一小部分通过启发式选择的神经元生成多神经元界限。为此，我们基于现有分支启发式方法，用于选择神经元和优化多神经元界限的超平面。我们将所提方法集成到Marabou验证器中，并在与现有界限紧化方法的比较中获得良好结果。实验展示了该技术在神经网络验证中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to formally verify safety properties of neural networks in critical systems, this paper introduces partial multi-neuron relaxation—a hybrid approach that selectively applies multi-neuron linear bounds to a small subset of neurons, balancing tightness and scalability. Building on existing branching heuristics and hyper-plane optimization techniques, the method integrates with the Marabou verifier and demonstrates improved performance over single-neuron and full multi-neuron relaxation methods in experimental evaluations, achieving tighter bounds while maintaining computational feasibility.
为满足在关键系统中验证神经网络安全属性的需求，本文提出部分多神经元松弛方法，该方法仅对一小部分神经元应用多神经元线性界限，以在紧致性与可扩展性间取得平衡。该方法基于现有分支启发式和超平面优化技术，生成比单神经元方法更紧的界限，同时避免全量多神经元松弛的计算开销。实验结果表明，该方法在Marabou验证器中优于现有界限紧致方法，显著提升了神经网络验证的效率与效果。
Value-Aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction for Reasoning Models
Authors: Ting-Yun Chang, Harvey Yiyun Fu, Deqing Fu, Chenghao Yang, Jesse Thomason, Robin Jia
First: 2026-06-02T17:16:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:16:33+00:00
Comments: Codes: https://github.com/terarachang/VaSE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reasoning models improve accuracy through extended chains of thought, but their long outputs create a memory and compute bottleneck. KV cache eviction methods reduce this cost by evicting unimportant key-value pairs from the cache, yet they often yield worse accuracy than selection-based sparse attention alternatives, which keep the full KV cache. We identify key factors crucial to KV cache eviction accuracy. First, a small fraction of value states have abnormally large magnitudes, and evicting them causes catastrophic failure where models enter repetitive reasoning loops. Second, introducing stochasticity during eviction improves accuracy by increasing cache diversity. Based on these findings, we propose Value-aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction (VaSE), a training-free recipe that protects large-magnitude value states and promotes diverse eviction decisions. Across six reasoning tasks, Qwen3 models using VaSE with 4x KV cache compression yield higher average accuracies than SOTA selection method at the same sparsity, while outperforming the strongest eviction method by more than 4%. Overall, VaSE bridges the gap between efficiency and accuracy, supporting FlashAttention2 and enabling a static memory footprint for reasoning models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向推理模型的价值感知随机KV缓存淘汰
推理模型通过扩展思维链提升准确性，但其长输出造成内存与计算瓶颈。KV缓存淘汰方法通过淘汰不重要的键值对降低该成本，但通常比基于选择的稀疏注意力方法（保留完整KV缓存）准确率更低。我们识别出影响KV缓存淘汰准确率的关键因素：首先，少量值状态具有异常大的幅值，淘汰它们会导致模型陷入重复推理循环；其次，淘汰过程中引入随机性可提升准确率，通过增加缓存多样性。基于此，我们提出价值感知随机KV缓存淘汰（VaSE），一种无需训练的方案，保护幅值大的值状态并促进多样化的淘汰决策。在六个推理任务中，使用VaSE实现4倍KV缓存压缩的Qwen3模型，在相同稀疏度下平均准确率高于当前最优选择方法，且比最强淘汰方法高出4%以上。总体而言，VaSE弥合了效率与准确率之间的差距，支持FlashAttention2，并为推理模型提供静态内存占用。
Summary / 总结
To address the memory and compute bottlenecks caused by long reasoning chains in large language models, this work investigates KV cache eviction strategies that balance efficiency and accuracy. It identifies two critical factors: the presence of high-magnitude value states whose eviction leads to catastrophic accuracy drops, and the benefit of stochastic eviction for increasing cache diversity. Based on these insights, it proposes Value-aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction (VaSE), a training-free method that protects high-magnitude values and introduces randomness in eviction decisions. Experimental results across six reasoning tasks show that VaSE achieves higher accuracy than state-of-the-art selection-based methods under the same sparsity level, and outperforms the best eviction method by over 4%, while being compatible with FlashAttention2 and enabling static memory usage.
为解决推理模型因长输出导致的内存与计算瓶颈，该论文研究了KV缓存淘汰策略在效率与准确率间的权衡。研究发现，淘汰异常大的值状态会导致模型陷入重复推理循环，而引入随机性可提升准确率，因能增加缓存多样性。提出的值感知随机KV缓存淘汰（VaSE）方法无需训练，通过保护高幅值值状态并促进多样化淘汰决策，在六个推理任务中，4倍缓存压缩下平均准确率优于当前最优选择方法，且比最强淘汰方法高出4%以上。
FFR: Forward-Forward Learning for Regression
Authors: Xinyang Liu, Xuanyu Liang, Shiqi Ding, Boyang Li, Zhiqiang Que, Jiayang Li, Guosheng Hu
First: 2026-06-02T17:15:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:15:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm offers a computationally efficient and biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation (BP) by training neural networks through purely local, layer-wise optimization. However, FF is inherently designed for classification via contrastive positive-negative sample pairs, and extending it to regression poses fundamental challenges: continuous target space lack natural "opposites" for contrastive learning, and the standard goodness function carries no information about target magnitude or ordering. We propose FFR (Forward-Forward for Regression), to our knowledge, the first framework to extend FF to real-world regression and demonstrate competitive performance across diverse real-world datasets. FFR introduces three key innovations: (1) an ordinal competitive goodness function that replaces contrastive pairs with competitive learning between partitioned neuron groups under distance-aware ordinal supervision; (2) a stratified ladder architecture where shallow layers learn coarse ordinal discrimination and deeper layers refine into fine-grained regression, with multi-scale feature aggregation for inter-layer collaboration; and (3) hierarchical prediction with uncertainty estimation, where multi-scale predictors jointly provide robust predictions and prediction confidence as a free-lunch. Extensive experimental results show FFR recovers on average 98.6% of BP's accuracy across five real-world regression benchmarks while reducing peak training memory to only 27% of BP's at depth 8 and 8% at depth 32, with per-iteration time around 72% of BP's, and substantially outperforms all BP-free competitors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FFR：面向回归的前向-前向学习
前向-前向（FF）算法通过纯局部、逐层优化训练神经网络，为反向传播（BP）提供了一种计算高效且符合生物可塑性的替代方案。然而，FF本质上为分类任务设计，依赖对比正负样本对；将其扩展至回归任务面临根本性挑战：连续目标空间缺乏自然的“对立”样本用于对比学习，且标准的优劣函数不包含目标量级或排序信息。我们提出FFR（面向回归的前向-前向），据我们所知，这是首个将FF扩展至真实世界回归任务并展现跨多类真实数据集竞争力的框架。FFR引入三项关键创新：（1）基于距离感知序数监督的序数竞争优劣函数，用分区神经元组间的竞争学习替代对比样本对；（2）分层梯度架构，浅层学习粗粒度序数判别，深层细化为细粒度回归，并通过多尺度特征聚合实现层间协作；（3）分层预测与不确定性估计，多尺度预测器联合提供稳健预测及置信度作为“免费午餐”。大量实验表明，FFR在五个真实世界回归基准上平均恢复98.6%的BP精度，同时在深度8时训练峰值内存仅为BP的27%，深度32时降至8%，每迭代时间约为BP的72%，显著优于所有无BP的竞品。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for biologically plausible and computationally efficient alternatives to backpropagation, the paper introduces FFR, the first framework to extend the Forward-Forward algorithm to regression tasks. FFR addresses the core challenge of regression by replacing contrastive learning with an ordinal competitive goodness function, using distance-aware supervision between neuron groups. It employs a stratified ladder architecture that progressively refines ordinal discrimination into fine-grained regression through multi-scale feature aggregation, and incorporates hierarchical prediction with uncertainty estimation for robustness. Experiments show FFR achieves 98.6% of backpropagation’s accuracy on five real-world regression benchmarks while reducing peak training memory by 73% at depth 32 and cutting per-iteration time to 72% of BP’s, outperforming all BP-free competitors.
为解决回归任务中前向-前向算法缺乏对比学习机制的问题，FFR提出三项创新：基于距离感知的序数竞争损失函数、分层梯度架构与多尺度特征聚合、以及带不确定性估计的分层预测。该框架通过序数监督替代对比学习，实现连续目标空间的有效建模。实验表明，FFR在五个真实回归数据集上平均恢复98.6%的反向传播精度，深度32时训练内存仅为反向传播的8%，每轮迭代时间约为72%，显著优于所有无反向传播的竞品方法。
T2AV-Compass: Towards Unified Evaluation for Text-to-Audio-Video Generation
Authors: Zhe Cao, Tao Wang, Jiaming Wang, Yanghai Wang, Yuanxing Zhang, Jialu Chen, Miao Deng, Jiahao Wang, Yubin Guo, Chenxi Liao, Yize Zhang, Zhaoxiang Zhang, Jiaheng Liu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-12-24T10:30:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:15:46+00:00
Comments: 41 pages, 13 figures, 12 tables. Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) generation aims to synthesize temporally coherent video and semantically synchronized audio from natural language, yet its evaluation remains fragmented, often relying on unimodal metrics or narrowly scoped benchmarks that fail to capture cross-modal alignment, instruction following, and perceptual realism under complex prompts. To address this limitation, we present T2AV-Compass, a unified benchmark for comprehensive evaluation of T2AV systems, consisting of 500 diverse and complex prompts constructed via a taxonomy-driven pipeline to ensure semantic richness and physical plausibility. Besides, T2AV-Compass introduces a dual-level evaluation framework that integrates objective signal-level metrics for video quality, audio quality, and cross-modal alignment with a subjective MLLM-as-a-Judge protocol for instruction following and realism assessment. Extensive evaluation of 11 representative T2AVsystems reveals that even the strongest models fall substantially short of human-level realism and cross-modal consistency, with persistent failures in audio realism, fine-grained synchronization, instruction following, etc. These results indicate significant improvement room for future models and highlight the value of T2AV-Compass as a challenging and diagnostic testbed for advancing text-to-audio-video generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：T2AV-Compass：迈向文本到音频视频生成的统一评估
文本到音频视频（T2AV）生成旨在从自然语言合成时序一致的视频和语义同步的音频，但其评估仍碎片化，常依赖单模态指标或范围狭窄的基准，难以捕捉跨模态对齐、指令遵循及复杂提示下的感知真实感。为解决此问题，我们提出T2AV-Compass，一个用于全面评估T2AV系统的统一基准，包含500个通过分类学驱动流程构建的多样且复杂的提示，确保语义丰富性和物理合理性。此外，T2AV-Compass引入双层级评估框架，结合视频质量、音频质量和跨模态对齐的客观信号级指标，以及主观MLLM作为裁判的指令遵循与真实感评估协议。对11个代表性T2AV系统的广泛评估表明，即使最强模型在人类级真实感和跨模态一致性方面仍显著不足，存在音频真实感、细粒度同步、指令遵循等方面的持续失败。这些结果表明未来模型仍有显著改进空间，并凸显T2AV-Compass作为推动文本到音频视频生成发展的挑战性与诊断性测试平台的价值。
Summary / 总结

The evaluation framework combines objective metrics for video and audio quality and cross-modal alignment with a subjective MLLM-as-a-Judge protocol to assess instruction following and perceptual realism. Experimental results across 11 state-of-the-art T2AV models show that even top-performing systems significantly lag behind human-level performance in realism, synchronization, and alignment, underscoring the need for more robust evaluation and highlighting T2AV-Compass as a critical diagnostic tool.
为解决当前文本到音频视频生成系统评估碎片化、不全面的问题，本文提出T2AV-Compass统一评测基准，包含通过分类学驱动流程构建的500个语义丰富且物理上合理的复杂提示。该基准采用双层评估框架，结合客观指标评估视频、音频质量和跨模态对齐，同时引入基于MLLM的主观评判协议评估指令遵循与感知真实感。在11个代表性模型上的实验表明，即便最强模型在音频真实度、细粒度同步性及指令遵循等方面仍显著落后于人类水平，凸显模型仍有巨大提升空间，同时证明T2AV-Compass作为推动该领域发展的关键诊断测试平台具有重要价值。
DiffUNet^2: Bidirectional Prediction, Probabilistic Generation and Collaborative Visual Discovery for Scientific Data
Authors: Mengdi Chu, Jiaxin Yang, Angus G. Forbes, Nathan Debardeleben, Earl Lawrence, Ayan Biswas, Han-Wei Shen
First: 2026-06-02T17:15:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:15:01+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 20 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling temporal evolution is important to analyzing and reasoning about scientific phenomena, yet most machine learning methods provide deterministic forward predictions that overlook multiple plausible outcomes and rarely support backward reasoning, limiting their usefulness in practical scientific workflows. We present a framework that integrates diffusion-based generative modeling with interactive visual analytics for scientific exploration. We introduce DiffUNet^2, a conditional diffusion model that enables bidirectional, any-to-any generation across time and captures distributions of plausible system evolutions. Built upon the model, our interactive system supports branching timeline exploration, user-guided state editing, and probability-space navigation, enabling scientists to actively explore alternative hypotheses rather than passively observe predictions. We evaluate the model on 5 datasets across different scientific domains to validate its predictive accuracy and probability-space ensemble quality. In collaboration with domain experts, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in supporting practical scientific temporal data analysis workflows. By integrating modeling and visual interaction, our approach enables scientists to interactively explore system dynamics, transforming generative models into tools for hypothesis-driven scientific analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiffUNet^2：双向预测、概率生成与协作视觉发现用于科学数据
建模时间演化对于分析和推理科学现象至关重要，但大多数机器学习方法仅提供确定性的前向预测，忽视多种可能结果，且很少支持反向推理，限制了其在实际科学工作流中的实用性。我们提出一个框架，将基于扩散的生成建模与交互式视觉分析相结合，用于科学探索。我们引入DiffUNet^2，一种条件扩散模型，支持跨时间的双向、任意到任意生成，并捕捉系统演化可能性分布。基于该模型，我们的交互式系统支持分支时间线探索、用户引导状态编辑和概率空间导航，使科学家能够主动探索替代假设，而非被动观察预测。我们在五个不同科学领域的数据集上评估该模型，验证其预测准确性和概率空间集成质量。通过与领域专家合作，我们展示了该方法在支持实际科学时序数据分析工作流中的有效性。通过整合建模与视觉交互，我们的方法使科学家能够交互式探索系统动态，将生成模型转化为基于假设驱动的科学分析工具。
