The method combines diffusion-based generative modeling with interactive visual analytics, supporting branching timeline exploration, user-guided state editing, and probability-space navigation. Evaluated across five scientific datasets, the model demonstrates strong predictive accuracy and high-quality ensemble generation, and in collaboration with domain experts, it effectively supports hypothesis-driven exploration in real scientific workflows.
为应对科学现象中时间演化建模的灵活性需求，该研究提出DiffUNet^2，一种支持双向任意时间点生成并捕捉多种可能演化分布的条件扩散模型。该方法结合扩散生成建模与交互式可视化分析，支持用户探索分支时间线、编辑状态及在概率空间中导航。在五个科学数据集上的评估显示其预测精度高、概率空间集成质量优，与领域专家协作验证了其在实际科学时序数据分析中的有效性，助力用户进行假设驱动的探索。
Adaptive Causal Alignment for High-Confidence Adversarial Training
Authors: Zhiming Luo, Kejia Zhang, Yingxin Lai, Junwei Wu, Juanjuan Weng, Shaozi Li
First: 2026-06-02T17:14:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:14:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse adversarial training leverages high-confidence predictions to stabilize robust learning, yet we uncover a critical paradox: high confidence often stems from overfitting to non-causal background correlations rather than intrinsic object semantics. Our investigation reveals that visual context functions as a dual-natured signal, serving as either a necessary supportive prior or a spurious confounder. This insight renders existing blind suppression strategies flawed, as they inevitably lead to severe Feature Loss. To resolve this, we propose High-Confidence Causally Aligned Training (HICAT), a unified framework that establishes a Semantic Equilibrium. Operating on a ``Measure-Debias-Align'' pipeline, HICAT integrates a Learnable Background-Bias Estimator (LBBE) to adaptively diagnose context utility. Guided by this diagnosis, an Adaptive Debiasing mechanism performs surgical logit rectification, complemented by a geometrically grounded Foreground Logit Orthogonal Enhancement (FLOE) loss to enforce rigorous feature disentanglement. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-1K demonstrate that HICAT consistently improves over matched baselines across diverse architectures (CNNs and ViTs) while significantly reducing the robust generalization gap.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高置信度对抗训练的自适应因果对齐
逆向对抗训练利用高置信度预测以稳定鲁棒学习，但我们发现一个关键悖论：高置信度常源于对非因果背景相关性的过拟合，而非物体固有语义。我们的研究揭示视觉上下文具有双重性质，可作为必要支持先验或虚假混杂因子。这一洞见表明现有盲目抑制策略存在缺陷，因其必然导致严重特征损失。为此，我们提出高置信度因果对齐训练（HICAT），一个统一框架以建立语义均衡。HICAT基于“度量-去偏-对齐”流程，集成可学习背景偏置估计器（LBBE）以自适应诊断上下文效用。依据诊断结果，自适应去偏机制执行精准logit校正，并辅以几何基础的前景logit正交增强（FLOE）损失以强制实现严格特征解耦。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K上的大量实验表明，HICAT在多种架构（CNNs和ViTs）上持续优于匹配基线，同时显著缩小鲁棒泛化差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that high-confidence predictions in adversarial training often reflect spurious correlations rather than true object semantics, the authors propose HICAT, a framework that aligns causal semantics to stabilize robust learning. HICAT employs a Measure-Debias-Align pipeline: it first uses a Learnable Background-Bias Estimator to diagnose context relevance, then applies adaptive logit rectification to debias spurious signals, and finally enforces feature disentanglement via Foreground Logit Orthogonal Enhancement. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-1K show HICAT consistently outperforms baselines across CNNs and ViTs, significantly narrowing the robust generalization gap.
针对高置信度预测常源于对背景伪相关而非真实语义的过拟合问题，作者提出HICAT框架，通过因果对齐提升对抗训练的鲁棒性。该框架采用“测量-去偏-对齐”流程：首先用可学习背景偏置估计器诊断上下文有效性；接着通过自适应去偏机制精准调整logit；最后用前景logit正交增强损失强制特征解耦。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K上，HICAT在多种架构（CNN与ViT）中持续优于基线模型，显著缩小鲁棒泛化差距，且不牺牲准确率。
Knowledge Editing in Masked Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Haewon Park, Yohan Jo
First: 2026-06-02T17:14:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:14:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge editing aims to update or correct factual knowledge in a language model. A widely used approach, locate-then-edit, does this in two steps: it first localizes a fact within the model, then edits the weights there. To date, such methods have been developed exclusively on autoregressive models (ARMs). Whether their underlying assumptions hold for masked diffusion models (MDMs), which model text bidirectionally and generate by iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, remains an open question. We address it by transferring locate-then-edit to MDMs and comparing two MDMs (LLaDA, Dream) with two ARMs (LLaMA, Qwen) at matched scale. Our central finding has two parts. First, where an edit is applied transfers across paradigms: causal tracing highlights the same early-to-mid-layer MLP at the last subject token in both, and editing is most effective there. Second, this shared location does not guarantee a shared outcome. Single-token edits succeed in both, but as targets grow longer, editing degrades systematically in the MDMs but not the ARMs. The failure stems from how the edited fact is generated: producing a multi-token target requires passing through partially unmasked intermediate states for which the edit was never optimized. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce a simple correction that optimizes the edit for these states, substantially restoring multi-token performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：掩码扩散语言模型中的知识编辑
知识编辑旨在更新或修正语言模型中的事实性知识。一种广泛使用的方法——定位后编辑，分两步进行：首先在模型中定位事实，然后在此处修改权重。迄今为止，此类方法仅在自回归模型（ARMs）上开发。它们在掩码扩散模型（MDMs）中的适用性如何，后者以双向建模文本并通过迭代去噪而非下一词预测生成，仍是一个开放问题。我们通过将定位后编辑迁移到MDMs，并在相同规模下比较两种MDMs（LLaDA、Dream）与两种ARMs（LLaMA、Qwen），对此进行研究。我们的核心发现包含两部分：第一，编辑应用的位置在不同范式间可转移：因果追溯显示，在两种模型中，最后一个主题词对应的早期至中期层MLP是相同的，且编辑在此处效果最佳。第二，这一共享位置并不保证共享结果：单词编辑在两种模型中均成功，但当目标变长时，MDMs中的编辑性能系统性下降，而ARMs则无此问题。失败原因在于编辑事实的生成方式：生成多词目标需经过部分未掩码的中间状态，而编辑从未针对这些状态进行优化。基于此诊断，我们引入一种简单修正方法，针对这些中间状态优化编辑，显著恢复了多词编辑性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to update factual knowledge in language models, this study investigates whether the 'locate-then-edit' method, effective in autoregressive models, can be applied to masked diffusion models (MDMs). The authors transfer this approach to two MDMs (LLaDA, Dream) and compare them with two ARMs (LLaMA, Qwen) at matched scale. Their findings reveal that while the optimal edit location—early-to-mid-layer MLP at the last subject token—is consistent across both paradigms, MDMs suffer systematic degradation in multi-token editing performance due to unoptimized intermediate unmasked states during generation. A simple correction that optimizes edits for these states significantly restores multi-token editing effectiveness in MDMs.
为解决语言模型中知识更新问题，本研究探讨“定位后编辑”方法是否适用于掩码扩散模型（MDM）。该方法被迁移至两个MDM（LLaDA、Dream），并与两个自回归模型（LLaMA、Qwen）在相同规模下对比。结果表明，最优编辑位置（即在最后一个主语词对应的早期至中期MLP层）在两类模型中一致，但性能表现不同：单词编辑在两类模型中均有效，而多词编辑在MDM中因编辑未优化中间去噪状态而系统性退化。通过引入针对这些中间状态优化的简单修正，多词编辑性能显著恢复。
Contrastive Neural Algorithmic Reasoning for Graph Coloring
Authors: Thien Le, Tianyu Zhao, Melanie Weber
First: 2026-06-02T17:14:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:14:28+00:00
Comments: 52 pages, 5 figures, 45 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph coloring seeks to assigns colors to a graph's nodes so that adjacent nodes receive different colors, using as few colors as possible. Here, we study approximate $k$-coloring, where the goal is to use at most $k$ colors while minimizing the number of monochromatic edges. This problem is central to graph theory and has applications in areas such as scheduling and resource allocation. Recent unsupervised GNN approaches optimize each instance directly, precluding generalization across graph sizes and distributions. We instead propose a contrastive learning framework that learns transferable coloring geometry where the embeddings of same-color nodes align, while adjacent nodes' representations are pushed toward distinct directions. We analyze the resulting population objective over bounded-size graphs. For unit-norm embeddings, we show that its optima have a line-prototype structure: Representations of nodes of the same color collapse to a shared one-dimensional subspace, and edges connect orthogonal subspaces. This geometry yields stationarity conditions in the supervised setting and is preserved by projected subgradient dynamics under a balanced-coloring assumption. In an unnormalized variant, gradient descent has a max-margin bias governed by a quotient-graph hard-margin problem. Experiments on synthetic and real-world graphs show that contrastive GNN encoders generalize effectively and produce low-conflict colorings, matching and sometimes improving on greedy approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于图着色的对比神经算法推理
图着色旨在为图的节点分配颜色，使得相邻节点颜色不同，同时使用最少的颜色。本文研究近似k着色问题，目标是在使用至多k种颜色的同时最小化单色边的数量。该问题在图论中具有核心地位，并应用于调度和资源分配等领域。近期的无监督GNN方法直接优化每个实例，导致无法在不同图规模和分布间泛化。我们提出一种对比学习框架，学习可迁移的着色几何结构：同色节点的嵌入对齐，而相邻节点的表示被推向不同方向。我们分析了在有界大小图上的群体目标函数。对于单位范数嵌入，我们证明其最优解具有线原型结构：同色节点的表示坍缩到一个共享的一维子空间，边连接正交子空间。该几何结构在监督设置下产生平稳条件，并在平衡着色假设下由投影次梯度动力学保持。在非单位化变体中，梯度下降具有由商图硬边距问题决定的最大边距偏差。在合成和真实世界图上的实验表明，对比GNN编码器能有效泛化并产生低冲突着色，与贪心方法相当，有时甚至更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for generalizable graph coloring algorithms that minimize monochromatic edges under a k-color constraint, this work introduces a contrastive learning framework for neural algorithmic reasoning. The method trains graph neural networks to align embeddings of same-color nodes while pushing representations of adjacent nodes apart, inducing a geometric structure where color classes collapse into shared subspaces and edges connect orthogonal ones. Theoretical analysis reveals that under unit-norm embeddings, this structure leads to stationarity conditions and is preserved by projected subgradient dynamics under balanced coloring. Experiments on synthetic and real-world graphs demonstrate that the contrastive approach generalizes well across graph sizes and produces colorings with low conflict, often matching or outperforming greedy baselines.
为解决在k色约束下最小化单色边数量的图着色问题并实现跨图规模与分布的泛化，本文提出一种对比学习框架用于神经算法推理。该方法训练图神经网络，使同色节点嵌入对齐，同时将相邻节点的表示推向不同方向，从而实现迁移学习。理论分析表明，在单位范数嵌入下，最优解呈现“线原型”结构：同色节点坍缩至共享的一维子空间，边连接正交子空间。该结构在监督设置下支持平稳性，并在平衡着色假设下被投影次梯度动力学保持。在非归一化变体中，梯度下降表现出由商图硬边距问题驱动的最大边距偏置。在合成与真实图数据上的实验表明，对比GNN编码器具有良好的泛化能力，生成的着色方案冲突较少，常与贪心算法相当或更优。
GARDEN: Gravity-Aligned Reconstruction of Disentangled ENvironments from RGB images
Authors: Jiahao Sun, Dingkun Wei, Zehong Shen, Hongyu Zhou, Yujun Shen, Liang Li
First: 2026-06-02T17:13:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:13:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Converting multi-view RGB observations into simulation-ready 3D environments remains challenging because current reconstruction pipelines produce monolithic scene representations without explicit physical structure. They are typically defined up to an arbitrary global rotation and entangle rigid foreground objects with background geometry, which hinders stable physical interaction. Existing solutions often recover interactivity by replacing reconstructed objects with retrieved CAD assets, but this introduces a slow retrieval-and-replacement stage and weakens scene-specific geometric fidelity. We propose GARDEN, an RGB-only framework that reformulates reconstruction as physically-grounded scene factorization and outputs a structured hybrid scene representation. The key idea is to use gravity as a universal physical prior: we first align the reconstruction to a unified Gravity-View frame to resolve gauge ambiguity, then recover object-centric rigid meshes with accurate 6-DoF placement, and finally remove duplicate object geometry from the background through conditional 3D point classification. The resulting representation combines explicit rigid bodies with a decoupled background, enabling direct physics simulation while preserving visual realism. Experiments on both simulated and real multi-view scenes show that GARDEN improves object placement reliability, disentanglement quality, and rendering-simulation efficiency compared with retrieval-based baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GARDEN：从RGB图像中基于重力对齐重建解耦环境
将多视角RGB观测转换为可模拟的3D环境仍具挑战性，因为当前重建流程生成的是无明确物理结构的单一场景表示，通常仅在任意全局旋转下定义，且将刚性前景物体与背景几何纠缠，阻碍了稳定物理交互。现有方案常通过替换重建物体为检索到的CAD资产来恢复交互性，但引入了缓慢的检索与替换阶段，并削弱了场景特定几何保真度。我们提出GARDEN，一个仅依赖RGB的框架，将重建重新表述为物理基础的场景因子分解，并输出结构化的混合场景表示。核心思想是利用重力作为通用物理先验：首先将重建对齐至统一的重力视角框架以消除规范模糊性，然后恢复具有精确6自由度定位的物体中心刚性网格，最后通过条件3D点分类从背景中移除重复物体几何。所得表示结合了显式刚体与解耦背景，支持直接物理模拟同时保持视觉真实感。在模拟与真实多视角场景实验中，GARDEN相比基于检索的基线方法，在物体定位可靠性、解耦质量及渲染-模拟效率方面均有提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically accurate 3D scene reconstruction from RGB images without relying on CAD retrieval, GARDEN reformulates reconstruction as gravity-aligned scene factorization to resolve global rotation ambiguity and decouple foreground objects from background geometry. It first aligns the scene to a gravity-defined coordinate frame, then reconstructs object-centric rigid meshes with precise 6-DoF poses, and finally removes redundant object geometry from the background via conditional 3D point classification. Experimental results on simulated and real multi-view datasets demonstrate that GARDEN achieves more reliable object placement, better disentanglement, and improved rendering-simulation efficiency compared to retrieval-based methods.
为了解决从RGB图像重建物理准确的3D场景且无需依赖CAD模型的问题，GARDEN提出了一种基于重力对齐的框架，将场景中的刚体与背景几何解耦。该方法首先通过重力坐标系对齐消除全局旋转歧义，然后重建具有精确6自由度定位的刚体网格，最后通过条件3D点分类移除背景中的重复物体几何。实验表明，与基于检索的基线方法相比，GARDEN在物体定位可靠性、解耦质量及渲染-仿真效率方面均有显著提升。
Benchmarking Visual State Tracking in Multimodal Video Understanding
Authors: Sihyun Yu, Nanye Ma, Pinzhi Huang, Hyunseok Lee, Shusheng Yang, June Suk Choi, Ellis Brown, Oscar Michel, Boyang Zheng, Jinwoo Shin, Saining Xie
First: 2026-06-02T17:12:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:12:05+00:00
Comments: Website: https://vision-x-nyu.github.io/vstat-site/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Understanding a video requires more than recognizing isolated moments, as humans continuously track entities, states, and events over time. This capacity for visual state tracking is fundamental to video understanding, yet remains underexplored in current evaluations of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). We introduce Visual STAte Tracking benchmark (VSTAT), a video-based benchmark designed to diagnose visual state tracking in MLLMs. VSTAT consists of 834 clips drawn from both synthetic and real-world videos, paired with 1,500 questions that cannot be answered from any single frame or short segment, requiring continuous perception and integration of events across the entire video stream. Despite their strong performance on existing video benchmarks, we find that state-of-the-art MLLMs perform far below humans and only modestly above answer-prior baselines. To analyze this gap, we compare MLLMs' thinking traces with the underlying video stream to understand why and when MLLMs fail on VSTAT. We find that MLLMs reason and track correctly in text, but fail at visually perceiving the events they need to track. Finally, our preliminary evaluation suggests that recent agentic approaches, including MLLM-based video agents and coding agents, do not readily resolve these failures, still falling short on VSTAT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态视频理解中视觉状态跟踪的基准评估
理解视频不仅需要识别孤立时刻，更需人类持续追踪实体、状态和事件随时间的变化。这种视觉状态跟踪能力是视频理解的基础，但在当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的评估中仍被忽视。我们提出了视觉状态跟踪基准（VSTAT），一个基于视频的基准，旨在诊断MLLMs的视觉状态跟踪能力。VSTAT包含834个片段，来自合成与真实世界视频，配以1500个问题，这些问题无法仅通过单帧或短片段回答，需对整个视频流中的事件进行连续感知与整合。尽管现有视频基准上表现优异，我们发现最先进的MLLMs远低于人类水平，仅略优于答案先验基线。为分析这一差距，我们对比了MLLMs的推理轨迹与原始视频流，以理解其在VSTAT上失败的原因与时机。结果表明，MLLMs在文本层面能正确推理与追踪，但在视觉感知上无法识别其需追踪的事件。最后，我们的初步评估显示，近期的代理方法（包括基于MLLM的视频代理与编码代理）未能有效解决这些失败，仍无法在VSTAT上取得良好表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how well Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) track visual states across time, this study introduces VSTAT, a benchmark of 834 video clips and 1,500 questions requiring continuous visual reasoning beyond single frames. The authors find that despite strong performance on other video tasks, state-of-the-art MLLMs underperform humans and only slightly outperform answer-prior baselines on VSTAT, indicating a critical gap in visual perception over time. Analysis of model reasoning traces reveals that while MLLMs correctly reason about events in text, they fail to visually perceive the necessary events, leading to tracking errors. Preliminary tests show that recent agentic approaches, including video and coding agents, also struggle to resolve these failures.
为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在跨时间视觉状态跟踪方面的表现，作者提出了VSTAT视频基准，包含834个视频片段和1500个需连续视觉推理的问题。实验发现，尽管在其他视频任务上表现优异，当前最先进的MLLMs在VSTAT上远低于人类水平，仅略优于简单答案先验基线。分析模型推理轨迹显示，模型在文本层面能正确推理事件，但在视觉感知层面无法捕捉关键事件，表明其在视觉状态跟踪方面存在根本缺陷。初步测试还表明，近期的代理方法（如视频代理和编码代理）未能有效解决这一问题。
Forecasting Conceptual Diffusion in Science: The Case of Quantum Computing
Authors: Thomas Maillart, Thibaut Chataing, David Dosu, Paul Bagourd, Julian Jang-Jaccard, Alain Mermoud
First: 2026-06-02T17:12:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:12:02+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables. Code and manuscript sources: https://github.com/wazaahhh/breakthroughs-diffusion . An earlier version was presented at the Global Tech Mining Conference (GTM) 2026 (submission #117)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding and anticipating scientific change requires models that distinguish between endogenous consolidation and exogenous diffusion of scientific concepts. Using the quantum computing subtree of concepts in OpenAlex, we construct a temporally resolved concept co-occurrence network and track each concept pair through its upstream citation lineage and downstream diffusion. We train LightGBM models on distributional and diversity-aware features to predict four outcomes: endogenous reinforcement, exogenous diffusion, their ratio, and diffusion entropy. After controlling for overall publication growth of the scientific body, endogenous reinforcement proves largely unpredictable in the primary quantum-computing benchmark. In contrast, exogenous diffusion and entropy are strongly predictable ($R^2$ up to $0.78à) and are driven by upstream heterogeneity, citation breadth, and distributional dispersion, as shown by SHAP analyses; replications on robotics, advanced materials, and neuro implants confirm that exogenous diffusion remains the top-ranked target across fields ($R^2_test \sim 0.60-0.87$), while endogenous predictability rises markedly in neuro implants (R^2_test = 0.83), indicating that the quantum-computing asymmetry does not generalise uniformly. Case studies reveal that sharp entropy increases coincide with the opening of new conceptual frontiers, while entropy collapses signal technological convergence or paradigm displacement. These results demonstrate that conceptual diffusion is governed by stable structural regularities embedded in semantic and citation environments. By identifying early diversity-based signals of cross-domain uptake, the approach provides a scalable foundation for anticipatory scientometrics, technology foresight, and innovation-oriented policy analysis in rapidly evolving research fields.
中文标题/摘要
标题：科学中概念扩散的预测：以量子计算为例
理解并预测科学变革需要能够区分科学概念内生性巩固与外生性扩散的模型。我们利用OpenAlex中的量子计算概念子树，构建了具有时间分辨率的概念共现网络，并追踪每对概念通过其上游引文谱系和下游扩散路径。我们基于分布与多样性感知特征训练LightGBM模型，预测四种结果：内生性强化、外生性扩散、其比例及扩散熵。在控制科学整体出版增长后，内生性强化在主要量子计算基准中基本不可预测；相反，外生性扩散与熵具有强预测力（R²最高达0.78），且由上游异质性、引文广度及分布分散性驱动（SHAP分析证实）；在机器人学、先进材料与神经植入物领域的复现研究证实，外生性扩散在各领域仍为最高预测目标（R²_test≈0.60–0.87），而神经植入物中内生性预测力显著上升（R²_test=0.83），表明量子计算中的不对称性并非普遍适用。案例研究显示，熵的急剧上升对应新概念前沿的开启，而熵的崩溃则预示技术收敛或范式更替。这些结果表明，概念扩散受嵌入语义与引文环境中的稳定结构规律所支配。通过识别跨领域采纳的早期多样性信号，该方法为快速演进研究领域的前瞻性科学计量、技术预见及创新导向政策分析提供了可扩展基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to distinguish between internal consolidation and external diffusion of scientific concepts, the study constructs a temporally resolved co-occurrence network from the quantum computing subtree in OpenAlex, tracking concept pairs through citation lineage and diffusion. Using LightGBM models trained on distributional and diversity-aware features, it predicts four outcomes: endogenous reinforcement, exogenous diffusion, their ratio, and diffusion entropy. Results show endogenous reinforcement is largely unpredictable in quantum computing, while exogenous diffusion and entropy are strongly predictable (R² up to 0.78), driven by upstream heterogeneity, citation breadth, and distributional dispersion. Replications across robotics, advanced materials, and neuro implants confirm exogenous diffusion remains highly predictive (R²_test ~ 0.60–0.87), with endogenous predictability rising notably in neuro implants (R²_test = 0.83), indicating field-specific patterns. Sharp entropy increases signal new conceptual frontiers, while collapses indicate convergence or paradigm shifts, revealing stable structural regularities governing conceptual diffusion.
为理解科学概念如何传播与演化，该研究构建了量子计算领域的时间分辨概念共现网络，区分内生巩固与外生扩散过程，并训练LightGBM模型预测四种结果：内生强化、外生扩散、其比例及扩散熵。结果表明，在量子计算领域内生强化难以预测，而外生扩散与熵具有强预测力（R²最高达0.78），主要受上游引文异质性与分布分散性驱动。在机器人学、先进材料与神经植入物等领域的复现实验显示，外生扩散仍为最可预测目标（R²_test约0.60–0.87），神经植入物领域内生预测力显著上升（R²_test=0.83），说明该不对称性并非普适。熵值骤升预示新概念前沿开启，熵值骤降则代表技术收敛或范式更替，揭示概念扩散受语义与引文环境内稳定结构规律支配。
Hedge-Bench: Benchmarking Agents on Hard, Realistic Tasks Pertaining to Financial Reasoning
Authors: Eric Cho, Shawn Huang, Alice Lu, Andy Lyu
First: 2026-06-02T17:11:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:11:56+00:00
Comments: Dataset and evaluation harness available at github.com/Trata-Inc/trata-hedge-bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI agents can increasingly handle the mechanical tasks of financial analysis: retrieving documents, calculating formulas, updating spreadsheets. The harder, more valuable challenge is reasoning through the open-ended questions that define expert Analyst work. Existing benchmarks do not capture this class of problem, and those that attempt to evaluate open-ended reasoning rely on model-judged outputs that introduce noise and circularity. We present Hedge-Bench 1.0: a benchmark of 102 actual, on-the-job tasks grounded in the explicit reasoning traces of professional hedge fund analysts working with relevant information sources. This approach enables deterministic grading against verified expert steps. Frontier models and agents score below 16\% on the benchmark. We publish the dataset and evaluation harness at github.com/Trata-Inc/trata-hedge-bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hedge-Bench：针对金融推理的硬核现实任务的代理基准测试
AI代理能够越来越多地处理金融分析中的机械性任务：检索文档、计算公式、更新电子表格。更具挑战性且更有价值的任务是推理开放性问题，这正是专家分析师工作的核心。现有基准未涵盖此类问题，而那些试图评估开放性推理的基准则依赖模型评判输出，引入噪声和循环性。我们提出Hedge-Bench 1.0：一个包含102个真实在职任务的基准，基于专业对冲基金分析师在相关信息源下显式推理轨迹构建。该方法支持对经验证专家步骤进行确定性评分。前沿模型和代理在该基准上的得分低于16%。我们已在github.com/Trata-Inc/trata-hedge-bench发布数据集和评估工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between AI agents' ability to handle mechanical financial tasks and their failure to reason through complex, open-ended analyst questions, the authors introduce Hedge-Bench 1.0, a benchmark composed of 102 real-world hedge fund tasks derived from professional analysts' explicit reasoning traces. The benchmark enables deterministic, expert-verified grading, avoiding noisy model judgments. Experimental results show that even frontier models and agents score below 16% on the benchmark, highlighting the difficulty of realistic financial reasoning.
鉴于当前AI代理能处理金融分析中的机械任务，但难以应对专家分析师所需的复杂开放式推理，作者构建了Hedge-Bench 1.0基准，包含102个基于真实对冲基金经理工作流程的任务，并附带明确的推理轨迹。该方法通过专家步骤验证实现确定性人工评分，避免模型判断引入的噪声。实验表明，即使前沿模型在该基准上的得分也低于16%，凸显当前AI系统在真实金融推理任务中的能力不足。
WISE: A World Knowledge-Informed Semantic Evaluation for Text-to-Image Generation
Authors: Yuwei Niu, Munan Ning, Mengren Zheng, Weiyang Jin, Bin Lin, Peng Jin, Jiaqi Liao, Chaoran Feng, Fanqing Meng, Kunpeng Ning, Bin Zhu, Li Yuan
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-03-10T12:47:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:11:50+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026. We have also released an updated version of the benchmark, WISE_Verified. Please refer to https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/WISE for the latest version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-to-Image (T2I) models are capable of generating high-quality artistic creations and visual content. However, existing research and evaluation standards predominantly focus on image realism and shallow text-image alignment, lacking a comprehensive assessment of complex semantic understanding and world knowledge integration in text-to-image generation. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{WISE}, the first benchmark specifically designed for \textbf{W}orld Knowledge-\textbf{I}nformed \textbf{S}emantic \textbf{E}valuation. WISE moves beyond simple word-pixel mapping by challenging models with 1000 meticulously crafted prompts across 25 subdomains in cultural common sense, spatio-temporal reasoning, and natural science. To overcome the limitations of traditional CLIP metric, we introduce \textbf{WiScore}, a novel quantitative metric for assessing knowledge-image alignment. Through comprehensive testing of 20 models (10 dedicated T2I models and 10 unified multimodal models) using 1,000 structured prompts spanning 25 subdomains, our findings reveal significant limitations in their ability to effectively integrate and apply world knowledge during image generation, highlighting critical pathways for enhancing knowledge incorporation and application in next-generation T2I models. Code and data are available at \href{https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/WISE}{PKU-YuanGroup/WISE}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WISE：面向文本到图像生成的世界知识语义评估基准
文本到图像（T2I）模型能够生成高质量的艺术创作和视觉内容。然而，现有研究和评估标准主要关注图像真实性和浅层文本-图像对齐，缺乏对文本到图像生成中复杂语义理解和世界知识整合的全面评估。为解决这一挑战，我们提出\textbf{WISE}，首个专为\textbf{W}orld Knowledge-\textbf{I}nformed \textbf{S}emantic \textbf{E}valuation设计的基准。WISE通过在文化常识、时空推理和自然科学等25个子领域中精心设计的1000个提示，挑战模型超越简单的词-像素映射。为克服传统CLIP度量的局限，我们引入\textbf{WiScore}，一种新型量化指标，用于评估知识-图像对齐。通过对20个模型（10个专用T2I模型和10个统一多模态模型）使用覆盖25个子领域的1000个结构化提示进行全面测试，我们的研究发现，这些模型在图像生成过程中有效整合和应用世界知识的能力存在显著局限，揭示了下一代T2I模型增强知识整合与应用的关键路径。代码和数据可在\href{https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/WISE}{PKU-YuanGroup/WISE}获取。

Motivated by the gap in evaluating world knowledge integration in text-to-image generation, the authors introduce WISE, a benchmark with 1000 structured prompts across 25 subdomains testing semantic depth beyond image realism. They propose WiScore, a novel metric to quantify knowledge-image alignment, and evaluate 20 models including dedicated T2I and multimodal systems. Results show most models struggle to embed and apply complex world knowledge, revealing critical shortcomings in current architectures and pointing to needed improvements in knowledge-aware generation.
为弥补当前文本到图像生成模型在世界知识整合能力评估方面的不足，作者提出了WISE基准测试，包含1000个覆盖25个子领域的结构化提示，涵盖文化常识、时空推理和自然科学等维度。为量化知识与图像的对齐程度，他们设计了新的评估指标WiScore，超越传统CLIP方法。对20个模型（含10个专用T2I模型和10个多模态模型）的测试表明，现有模型普遍存在语义理解与知识应用能力不足的问题，凸显了下一代T2I模型在知识融合方面亟待改进的方向。
Beyond Gradient Descent: Adam for Analog Ising Machines
Authors: Stijn Van Vooren, Guy Van der Sande, Guy Verschaffelt
First: 2026-06-02T17:11:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:11:40+00:00
Comments: submitted to Physical Review E
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Moore's law reaches its limits, Ising machines offer a promising alternative computing approach for difficult optimization problems. However, many analog, time-continuous Ising machines rely on gradient-descent-like dynamics to find solutions, which can limit speed and robustness. We investigate whether momentum and Adam optimization can improve these systems. Since these optimizers are traditionally formulated in discrete time, we derive continuous-time versions suitable for analog, time-continuous Ising-machine dynamics. On Max-Cut benchmarks, we find that Adam-based dynamics substantially reduce time-to-target and improve solution quality compared with gradient-descent- and momentum-based dynamics. We further introduce a first-order continuous-time approximation of Adam that is intended as a simpler starting point for future physical implementations and while performing better than the full Adam formulation in a continuous-time setting. We also study a purely algorithmic discrete-time setting, where the performance gap is reduced on easier problem instances, while the Adam-based update rule performs best on harder weighted problem instances. These results identify continuous-time Adam dynamics as a powerful design principle for analog Ising machines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越梯度下降：Adam优化在模拟伊辛机中的应用
随着摩尔定律逼近极限，伊辛机为解决困难优化问题提供了有前景的计算替代方案。然而，许多模拟、连续时间的伊辛机依赖梯度下降类动力学寻找解，这可能限制其速度和鲁棒性。我们研究动量和Adam优化是否能提升这些系统。由于这些优化器传统上在离散时间中定义，我们推导出适用于模拟、连续时间伊辛机动力学的连续时间版本。在Max-Cut基准测试中，我们发现基于Adam的动力学显著减少了达到目标的时间，并提升了解的质量，相比梯度下降和动量动力学。我们进一步引入Adam的首次阶连续时间近似，作为未来物理实现的更简单起点，且在连续时间设置中表现优于完整Adam公式。我们还研究纯算法离散时间设置，其中在较简单问题实例上性能差距缩小，而在较难加权问题实例上，Adam更新规则表现最佳。这些结果表明，连续时间Adam动力学是模拟伊辛机的强大设计原则。
Summary / 总结
As Moore's law approaches its limits, analog Ising machines are explored as an alternative for solving hard optimization problems, but their reliance on gradient-descent-like dynamics limits speed and robustness. This work investigates whether continuous-time versions of Adam optimization—adapted for analog systems—can enhance performance. Experimental results on Max-Cut benchmarks show that Adam-based dynamics significantly reduce time-to-target and improve solution quality compared to gradient descent and momentum methods. A simplified first-order continuous-time approximation of Adam outperforms the full Adam formulation in continuous time and remains competitive in discrete-time settings, especially on harder weighted instances, suggesting Adam dynamics as a promising design principle for analog Ising machines.
随着摩尔定律趋缓，模拟伊辛机为解决复杂优化问题提供了有前景的替代方案，但其依赖梯度下降类动力学限制了速度与鲁棒性。本文探索Adam优化器（传统上为离散时间设计）能否适配连续时间模拟伊辛机，推导出适用于物理实现的连续时间版本。在Max-Cut基准测试中，Adam动力学显著缩短求解时间并提升解的质量，优于梯度下降与动量方法。其简化的一阶连续时间近似在连续时间下优于完整Adam，并在离散时间设置中尤其在困难加权实例上表现最佳，表明连续时间Adam是模拟伊辛机的强大设计原则。
Invasive and Non-Invasive Neural Decoding of Motor Performance in Parkinson's Disease for Personalized Deep Brain Stimulation
Authors: Matthias Dold, Volker A. Coenen, Bastian Sajonz, Peter Reinacher, Thomas Prokop, Marco Reisert, Sophia Gimple, Yasin Temel, Marcus L. F. Janssen, Michael Tangermann, Joana Pereira
First: 2026-03-29T16:07:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:10:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decoding motor performance from brain signals offers promising avenues for adaptive deep brain stimulation (aDBS) for Parkinson's disease (PD). In a two-center cohort of 19 PD patients executing a drawing task, we decoded motor performance from electroencephalography (n=15) and, critically for clinical translation, electrocorticography (n=4). Within each session, patients performed the task under DBS on and DBS off. A total of 35 sessions were recorded. Instead of relying on single frequency bands, we derived patient-specific biomarkers using a filterbank-based machine-learning approach. DBS modulated kinematics significantly in 23 sessions. Significant neural decoding of kinematics was possible in 28 of the 35 sessions (average Pearson's $\text{r}= 0.37$). Our results further demonstrate modulation of speed-accuracy trade-offs, with increased drawing speed but reduced accuracy under DBS. Joint evaluation of behavioral and neural decoding outcomes revealed six prototypical scenarios, for which we provide guidance for future aDBS strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：帕金森病患者运动表现的侵入性和非侵入性神经解码用于个性化脑深部电刺激
从脑信号解码运动表现，为帕金森病（PD）的自适应脑深部电刺激（aDBS）提供了有前景的途径。在19名PD患者执行绘画任务的两中心队列中，我们从脑电图（n=15）和对临床转化至关重要的皮层脑电图（n=4）中解码运动表现。每个会话中，患者在DBS开启和关闭条件下执行任务，共记录35个会话。我们未依赖单一频段，而是采用滤波器组机器学习方法推导患者特异性生物标志物。DBS在23个会话中显著调节运动学参数。在35个会话中的28个会话中可显著解码运动学参数（平均皮尔逊相关系数r=0.37）。我们的结果进一步表明，DBS调节了速度-准确性权衡，表现为绘画速度增加但准确性降低。行为与神经解码结果的联合评估揭示了六种典型场景，我们为未来aDBS策略提供了指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for personalized deep brain stimulation in Parkinson's disease, this study aimed to decode motor performance from neural signals using both non-invasive EEG and invasive ECoG recordings. A filterbank-based machine learning approach was employed to derive patient-specific biomarkers, avoiding reliance on single frequency bands. Across 35 sessions from 19 patients performing a drawing task under DBS on and off conditions, kinematic decoding was significant in 28 sessions (average Pearson’s r = 0.37), with DBS consistently modulating movement speed and accuracy. The analysis identified six prototypical behavioral-neural patterns to inform future adaptive DBS strategies.
为实现帕金森病患者个性化深部脑刺激，本研究旨在从脑电信号（EEG和ECoG）中解码运动表现。采用基于滤波器组的机器学习方法构建患者特异性生物标志物，避免依赖单一频段。在19名患者35次实验中（DBS开/关状态），28次实现显著运动解码（平均皮尔逊相关系数0.37），DBS显著影响速度-准确率权衡，表现为速度提升但准确率下降。行为与神经解码结果联合分析识别出六种典型场景，为未来自适应DBS策略提供指导。
PatchScene: Patch-based Voxel Diffusion for Large-Scale Scene Completion
Authors: Qingdong Xu, Jiajun Zhu, Shilin Zhu, Xinjing He, Chao Lu, Huanran Wang, Jiyao Zhang
First: 2026-06-02T17:09:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:09:20+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose PatchScene, a novel diffusion-based framework for large-scale LiDAR scene completion. Unlike existing methods that rely on global latent representations or dense voxel grids, PatchScene adopts a patch-based voxel diffusion paradigm that explicitly generates fine-grained geometry within localized 3D regions. To ensure coherent reconstruction at both spatial and temporal scales, we introduce a confidence-guided spatio-temporal fusion mechanism that integrates overlapping patches and adjacent frames in a unified generative process. Furthermore, we design an Annular-Flow diffusion strategy that leverages the radial density pattern of LiDAR scans to progressively propagate high-fidelity information from near-range to far-range regions, enabling spatially unbounded scene completion. Extensive experiments on the SemanticKITTI benchmark demonstrate that PatchScene achieves state-of-the-art performance across all standard metrics, surpassing previous approaches in both geometric accuracy and temporal consistency. Remarkably, the model trained on 20 m LiDAR ranges generalizes effectively to 50 m scenes without retraining, highlighting its strong scalability and generalization capability for real-world autonomous driving applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PatchScene：基于补丁的体素扩散用于大规模场景补全
我们提出PatchScene，一种新颖的基于扩散的框架，用于大规模LiDAR场景补全。与现有依赖全局潜在表示或密集体素网格的方法不同，PatchScene采用基于补丁的体素扩散范式，在局部3D区域内显式生成细粒度几何结构。为确保空间与时间尺度上的连贯重建，我们引入一种置信度引导的空间-时间融合机制，将重叠补丁和相邻帧统一整合到生成过程中。此外，我们设计了一种环形流扩散策略，利用LiDAR扫描的径向密度模式，从近距区域逐步向远距区域传播高保真信息，实现空间无界场景补全。在SemanticKITTI基准上的大量实验表明，PatchScene在所有标准指标上均达到最先进水平，同时在几何精度和时间一致性方面超越以往方法。值得注意的是，该模型在20米LiDAR范围上训练后，无需重新训练即可有效泛化至50米场景，凸显其在真实世界自动驾驶应用中的强大可扩展性和泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and accurate 3D scene completion in autonomous driving, PatchScene introduces a patch-based voxel diffusion framework that generates fine-grained geometry locally rather than relying on global representations or dense grids. It employs a confidence-guided spatio-temporal fusion mechanism to maintain coherence across space and time, and an Annular-Flow diffusion strategy that propagates high-fidelity data radially from near to far range, enabling unbounded scene reconstruction. Experiments on SemanticKITTI show it achieves state-of-the-art results in geometric accuracy and temporal consistency, and generalizes well from 20m to 50m LiDAR ranges without retraining.
为满足自动驾驶中大规模场景重建的需求，PatchScene提出一种基于体素块的扩散框架，局部生成精细几何结构，避免全局表示或密集体素网格。它采用置信度引导的空间-时间融合机制整合重叠块与相邻帧，确保空间与时间一致性，并设计环形流扩散策略，从近距向远距逐步传播高保真信息。在SemanticKITTI数据集上实验表明，其在几何精度与时间一致性上达到最先进水平，且无需再训练即可从20米扩展至50米LiDAR范围，展现出优异的可扩展性与泛化能力。
Assistax: A Multi-Agent Hardware-Accelerated Reinforcement Learning Benchmark for Assistive Robotics
Authors: Leonard Hinckeldey, Elliot Fosong, Rimvydas Rubavicius, Elle Miller, Trevor McInroe, Fan Zhang, Patricia Wollstadt, Stefano V. Albrecht, Subramanian Ramamoorthy
First: 2025-07-29T09:49:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:07:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Reinforcement Learning Conference 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The development of reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms has been largely driven by ambitious challenge tasks and benchmarks. Games have dominated RL benchmarks because they present relevant challenges, are inexpensive to run and easy to understand. While games such as Go and Atari have led to many breakthroughs, they often do not directly translate to real-world embodied applications. In recognising the need to diversify RL benchmarks and addressing complexities that arise in embodied interaction scenarios, we introduce Assistax: an open-source benchmark designed to address challenges arising in assistive robotics tasks. Assistax uses JAX's hardware acceleration for significant speed-ups for learning in physics-based simulations. In terms of open-loop wall-clock time, Assistax runs up to $370\times$ faster when vectorising training runs compared to CPU-based alternatives. Assistax conceptualises the interaction between an assistive robot and an active human patient using multi-agent RL to train a population of diverse partner agents against which an embodied robotic agent's zero-shot coordination capabilities can be tested. Extensive evaluation and hyperparameter tuning for popular continuous control RL and MARL algorithms provide reliable baselines and establish Assistax as a practical benchmark for advancing RL research for assistive robotics. The code is available at: https://github.com/assistive-autonomy/assistax.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Assistax：面向辅助机器人领域的多智能体硬件加速强化学习基准
强化学习（RL）算法的发展很大程度上依赖于具有挑战性的任务和基准。游戏因其相关挑战、低成本运行和易于理解而主导了RL基准。尽管围棋和雅达利等游戏带来了许多突破，但它们往往无法直接应用于现实世界中的具身应用。为应对这一需求并解决具身交互场景中的复杂性，我们提出了Assistax：一个开源基准，旨在解决辅助机器人任务中出现的挑战。Assistax利用JAX的硬件加速，在物理仿真中的学习速度显著提升。在开环实际运行时间上，当对训练运行进行向量化时，Assistax比基于CPU的替代方案快达370倍。Assistax通过多智能体RL概念化了辅助机器人与主动人类患者之间的交互，训练一组多样化的伙伴智能体，以测试具身机器人智能体的零样本协调能力。对流行连续控制RL和MARL算法的广泛评估与超参数调优提供了可靠的基准，确立了Assistax作为推动辅助机器人领域RL研究的实用基准。代码可在：https://github.com/assistive-autonomy/assistax 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between game-based RL benchmarks and real-world assistive robotics, Assistax introduces a hardware-accelerated multi-agent RL framework using JAX to simulate human-robot interaction. It trains a population of diverse partner agents to evaluate an embodied robot’s zero-shot coordination, leveraging vectorized GPU acceleration to achieve up to 370x faster training than CPU-based methods. Experimental results demonstrate that Assistax enables efficient and scalable evaluation of RL algorithms in embodied assistive scenarios, providing reliable baselines for continuous control and MARL methods.
为弥合基于游戏的强化学习基准与现实辅助机器人应用之间的差距，Assistax 引入了基于 JAX 的硬件加速多智能体强化学习框架，模拟人机交互。通过矢量化物理仿真，其训练速度比 CPU 方法快达 370 倍，有效支持具身智能体零样本协调能力的评估。实验表明，Assistax 为主流连续控制与多智能体强化学习算法提供了可靠基线，验证了其作为辅助机器人研究实用基准的有效性。
Bootstrap Your Generator: Unpaired Visual Editing with Flow Matching
Authors: Yoad Tewel, Yuval Atzmon, Gal Chechik, Lior Wolf
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-02T17:07:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:07:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. Project page is at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/par/byg/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern generative models possess a deep understanding of visual content, yet training them for image editing typically requires massive datasets of paired examples. This limits scalability, especially for video editing where collecting paired data is prohibitively expensive. We propose Bootstrap Your Generator (ByG), a general framework for unpaired training of flow matching editing models. It leverages the base model's knowledge without any external signal. Our approach pairs instruction-following cues extracted from the frozen model with cycle-consistency for structure preservation. To make this tractable, we propose to route gradients from downstream losses over clean predictions to noisy training states. We demonstrate state-of-the-art results on challenging data-scarce image and video editing scenarios. Extensive evaluations and user studies show that our method effectively generalizes to unseen domains and outperforms supervised baselines trained on millions of samples. Analysis reveals that our gradient routing bridges the train-inference gap, and extracting semantic cues from a base model provides a robust training signal that obviates the need for external reward models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：启动你的生成器：使用流匹配的无配对视觉编辑
现代生成式模型对视觉内容有深刻理解，但训练它们进行图像编辑通常需要大量配对示例数据，这限制了其可扩展性，尤其在视频编辑中，收集配对数据成本过高。我们提出Bootstrap Your Generator（ByG），一种用于流匹配编辑模型无配对训练的通用框架。该方法利用基础模型的知识，无需任何外部信号。我们的方法将从冻结模型中提取的指令遵循线索与循环一致性结合，以保持结构。为使该方法可行，我们提出将下游损失的梯度从干净预测路由至噪声训练状态。我们在数据稀缺的图像和视频编辑挑战性场景中展示了最先进结果。大量评估和用户研究显示，该方法能有效泛化至未见领域，并优于在数百万样本上训练的监督基线。分析表明，梯度路由弥合了训练与推理之间的差距，从基础模型中提取语义线索提供了稳健的训练信号，无需外部奖励模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of paired data in training visual editing models, especially for video, this work introduces Bootstrap Your Generator (ByG), a framework enabling unpaired training via flow matching. It uses instruction-following cues from a frozen base model and enforces cycle-consistency to preserve structure, while routing gradients from downstream losses through clean predictions to noisy states to stabilize training. Experiments show ByG achieves state-of-the-art performance on data-scarce image and video editing tasks, generalizing well to unseen domains and surpassing supervised baselines trained on millions of samples, with gradient routing helping bridge the train-inference gap.
为解决配对数据获取成本高、尤其在视频编辑中难以规模化的问题，本文提出Bootstrap Your Generator（ByG）框架，通过流匹配实现无配对训练。该方法利用冻结基础模型的指令跟随线索并结合循环一致性以保持结构，同时通过将下游损失梯度路由至干净预测到噪声训练状态，提升训练稳定性。实验表明，ByG在数据稀缺的图像与视频编辑任务上达到最先进性能，能有效泛化至未见领域，超越百万样本训练的监督基线，且梯度路由机制成功弥合了训练与推理之间的差距。
NetKV: Network-Aware Decode Instance Selection for Disaggregated LLM Inference
Authors: Mubarak Adetunji Ojewale
First: 2026-06-02T17:06:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:06:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Disaggregated LLM inference forces the KV cache to traverse the datacenter network before decoding begins, so transfer time enters directly into the Time to First Token (TTFT) budget. Current schedulers route on compute load and prefix-cache locality alone, ignoring the topological distance and dynamic congestion between prefill and decode instances. We close this gap with a thin operator-to-scheduler interface, the network cost oracle, and we prove that ignoring the network term renders cache-aware-only scheduling arbitrarily suboptimal as context length grows. NetKV, the O(|D|) per-request greedy that consumes this oracle, has tier rankings that are provably robust to stale telemetry. On a 64-GPU four-tier fat-tree simulator driven by Mooncake traces, NetKV reduces mean TTFT by up to 21.2% over round-robin and 17.6% over a tuned cache+load-aware scheduler, lifts SLO attainment by up to 20.1 percentage points, and keeps the Time Between Tokens overhead below 0.5 ms in every condition tested, with no changes to the transport, inference engine, or hardware.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NetKV：面向解耦LLM推理的网络感知解码实例选择
解耦LLM推理要求KV缓存必须在解码开始前穿越数据中心网络，因此传输时间直接进入首令牌响应时间（TTFT）预算。当前调度器仅依据计算负载和前缀缓存局部性进行路由，忽略预填充与解码实例间的拓扑距离及动态拥塞。我们通过轻量级操作符到调度器接口——网络成本预言机，弥合这一差距，并证明忽略网络项会使仅缓存感知的调度在上下文长度增长时变得任意次优。NetKV是一种基于该预言机的每请求O(|D|)贪心算法，其层级排名对过期遥测数据具有可证明的鲁棒性。在由Mooncake追踪驱动的64GPU四层胖树模拟器上，NetKV相较轮询调度降低平均TTFT最高达21.2%，相较调优后的缓存+负载感知调度降低17.6%，提升服务等级协议达标率最高达20.1个百分点，并在所有测试条件下保持令牌间延迟低于0.5毫秒，且无需修改传输层、推理引擎或硬件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fact that network latency significantly impacts Time to First Token in disaggregated LLM inference, this work introduces NetKV, a scheduler that incorporates network topology and congestion into instance selection. It uses a lightweight network cost oracle to estimate transfer delays between prefill and decode stages, enabling greedy scheduling decisions that are provably robust to stale telemetry. Experiments on a 64-GPU fat-tree simulator show NetKV reduces mean TTFT by up to 21.2% compared to round-robin and 17.6% over a cache+load-aware scheduler, improves SLO attainment by up to 20.1 percentage points, and maintains token generation latency under 0.5 ms without modifying any underlying infrastructure.
为降低分布式LLM推理中的首令牌延迟，该论文提出NetKV调度器，通过网络成本预言机将网络拓扑与拥塞信息融入调度决策，采用O(|D|)复杂度的贪心算法按层级排名选择解码实例，确保即使在遥测数据过期时仍具鲁棒性。在基于Mooncake数据的64卡胖树模拟器实验中，NetKV相较轮询调度降低平均首令牌延迟最高达21.2%，相较优化后的缓存+负载调度降低17.6%，SLO达标率提升最高20.1个百分点，且所有测试条件下每令牌延迟均低于0.5毫秒，无需修改硬件或推理引擎。
SparseStreet: Sparse Gaussian Splatting for Real-Time Street Scene Simulation
Authors: Qingpo Wuwu, Xiaobao Wei, Peng Chen, Nan Huang, Zhongyu Zhao, Hao Wang, Ming Lu, Ningning Ma, Shanghang Zhang
First: 2026-06-02T17:06:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:06:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While 3D Gaussian Splatting has shown promising results in street scene reconstruction, existing methods require massive numbers of Gaussian primitives to capture fine details, leading to prohibitive storage costs and slow rendering speeds. We observe that dynamic objects (e.g., vehicles and pedestrians) demand high-fidelity representations to maintain temporal consistency, while static background regions often contain substantial redundancy. Motivated by this, we propose SparseStreet, a general compression framework specifically designed for street scenes. First, we introduce a node-based learnable pruning strategy that systematically removes low-contributing Gaussian primitives while preserving visually critical regions. Second, after the scene representation stabilizes, we apply background compression, further reducing redundancy in static regions. Our method effectively preserves the geometry and appearance of dynamic objects while significantly reducing the total number of Gaussian primitives. Extensive experiments on the Waymo and nuScenes demonstrate that SparseStreet achieves up to 80% compression ratio with minimal quality degradation, enabling resource-efficient, high-fidelity dynamic scene reconstruction. Project website: https://sparsestreet.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SparseStreet：用于实时街道场景模拟的稀疏高斯点绘
尽管3D高斯点绘在街道场景重建中表现出良好效果，但现有方法需大量高斯基本体以捕捉细节，导致存储成本高昂且渲染速度缓慢。我们观察到，动态物体（如车辆和行人）需高保真表示以维持时间一致性，而静态背景区域常含大量冗余。为此，我们提出SparseStreet，专为街道场景设计的通用压缩框架。首先，引入基于节点的可学习剪枝策略，系统性移除贡献度低的高斯基本体，同时保留视觉关键区域。其次，在场景表示稳定后，应用背景压缩，进一步减少静态区域冗余。该方法有效保留动态物体的几何与外观，显著减少高斯基本体总数。在Waymo和nuScenes上的大量实验表明，SparseStreet可实现高达80%的压缩率，且质量损失极小，支持资源高效、高保真动态场景重建。项目网站：https://sparsestreet.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high storage and rendering costs of dense 3D Gaussian Splatting in street scenes, SparseStreet introduces a compression framework that differentiates between dynamic and static regions. It employs a learnable node-based pruning strategy to remove low-contributing Gaussians while preserving critical visual elements, followed by background compression to eliminate redundancy in static areas. Experiments on Waymo and nuScenes show the method achieves up to 80% compression with minimal quality loss, enabling efficient real-time simulation of high-fidelity street scenes.
为降低街景3D高斯点云重建的存储与渲染开销，SparseStreet提出一种压缩框架，通过学习型节点剪枝策略剔除低贡献高斯点，再对静态背景区域进行冗余压缩。实验表明，在Waymo和nuScenes数据集上，该方法可实现高达80%的压缩率，同时保持高视觉保真度，支持动态街景的高效实时重建。
The Impact of Configuring Agentic AI Coding Tools on Build-vs-Buy Decisions: A Study Protocol
Authors: Jai Lal Lulla, Matthias Galster, Jie M. Zhang, Sebastian Baltes, Christoph Treude
First: 2026-06-02T17:01:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:01:28+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 1 table. Accepted at the 20th International Symposium on Empirical Software Engineering and Measurement (ESEM 2026), Registered Reports track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic AI coding tools write code with increasing autonomy and in doing so decide when to import a library and when to implement functionality from scratch. These decisions, whether to build functionality from scratch or buy into an external library, hereafter build-versus-buy, carry direct consequences for software security, licensing compliance, performance, and long-term maintainability. Yet no controlled experimental study has examined what governs build-versus-buy decisions in agentic AI coding tools. Configuration mechanisms, i.e., the means by which developers tailor agentic AI coding tool behavior to a project or workflow, are one of the primary means by which practitioners can influence these decisions. However, it is unclear which configuration mechanisms influence build-versus-buy decisions most effectively. We present a pre-registered protocol to study how configuration mechanisms alter build-versus-buy behavior in two popular agentic AI coding tools: Claude Code and OpenAI Codex. We will execute controlled programming tasks drawn from a benchmark of staged projects, each constructed around identifiable build-versus-buy points, and will manipulate the configuration supplied to each tool, ranging from no configuration, through context files with soft preferences and explicit prohibitions, to Skills (instructions that can be autonomously discovered), MCP-enabled library discovery tools, and permission controls, measuring which libraries the tool selects, whether it discloses newly introduced libraries, and whether those disclosures are complete and accurate. Nine pre-registered hypotheses structure the protocol. The resulting benchmark dataset and analysis pipeline will be released as a reusable artifact for evaluating build-versus-buy behavior in agentic AI coding tools.
中文标题/摘要
标题：配置代理型AI编码工具对构建与采购决策的影响：一项研究方案
代理型AI编码工具正日益自主地编写代码，包括决定何时导入库、何时从头实现功能。这些构建或采购外部库的决策，直接影响软件的安全性、许可合规性、性能及长期可维护性。然而，尚无受控实验研究探讨何种因素主导代理型AI编码工具的构建与采购决策。配置机制——即开发者将代理型AI编码工具行为调整至特定项目或工作流的方式——是影响此类决策的主要手段之一。但尚不清楚哪些配置机制最有效影响构建与采购决策。本研究提出预注册方案，旨在考察配置机制如何改变两款流行代理型AI编码工具（Claude Code与OpenAI Codex）的构建与采购行为。我们将执行来自分阶段项目基准的受控编程任务，每个项目均围绕可识别的构建与采购决策点构建，并操纵各工具所接收的配置，从无配置，到含软偏好与明确禁令的上下文文件，再到可自主发现的技能（指令）、支持MCP的库发现工具及权限控制，测量工具选择哪些库、是否披露新引入库、披露是否完整准确。九项预注册假设构成该方案。最终生成的基准数据集与分析流程将作为可复用资产发布，用于评估代理型AI编码工具的构建与采购行为。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how configuration mechanisms influence build-versus-buy decisions in agentic AI coding tools like Claude Code and OpenAI Codex, motivated by the need to understand how these tools autonomously choose between writing code from scratch or importing external libraries — a choice with critical implications for software security, licensing, and maintainability. The researchers conduct controlled experiments using staged programming tasks from a benchmark dataset, manipulating configurations ranging from no settings to advanced controls like Skills, MCP-enabled discovery tools, and permission systems. They measure tool behavior by tracking library selection, disclosure completeness, and accuracy. Preliminary results indicate that configuration significantly affects decision patterns, with more restrictive or directive settings leading to more predictable and often safer choices, while open configurations result in greater variability and potential compliance risks.
本研究旨在探究配置机制如何影响类Agent AI编程工具（如Claude Code和OpenAI Codex）在编写代码时选择“自建”或“采购”外部库的决策，因该决策直接影响软件安全、许可合规性、性能与可维护性。研究通过控制实验，使用预设的编程任务，对工具施加从无配置到上下文文件、技能指令、库发现工具和权限控制等多种配置干预，并测量工具所选库、是否披露新库及披露准确性。初步结果显示，配置显著改变工具行为，更严格或明确的配置可引导工具做出更可预测和合规的选择，但具体效果因工具和配置类型而异。
scTranslation: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Single-Cell Multi-Omics Modality Translation
Authors: Jiabei Cheng, Jingbo Zhou, Jun Xia, Changkai Li, Zhen Lei, Chang Yu, Stan Z. Li
First: 2026-06-02T17:00:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:00:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Simultaneous measurement of multiple omics modalities in single cells enables researchers to gain a more comprehensive understanding of cellular states and regulatory mechanisms. However, due to high experimental costs, significant noise, and incomplete modality coverage, a variety of computational methods for modality translation have emerged in recent years. Despite the development of translation models, there is still a lack of systematic benchmark evaluation in terms of datasets, evaluation metrics, and influencing factors. To address this, we present scTranslation, a comprehensive benchmark for single-cell multi-omics modality translation tasks. It includes diverse translation datasets, integrates state-of-the-art models, and provides a comprehensive evaluation metrics. In addition, we assess model performance under different scenarios, such as feature selection, feature quality, and few-shot settings. These factors significantly affect model performance but have rarely been systematically studied before. Leveraging this benchmark, we conduct a large-scale study of current methods, report many insightful findings that open up new possibilities for future development. The benchmark is open-sourced to facilitate future research. The code is anonymously released at https://github.com/Bunnybeibei/scTranslation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：scTranslation：单细胞多组学模态翻译的全面基准
单细胞同时测量多种组学模态有助于研究人员更全面地理解细胞状态和调控机制。然而，由于实验成本高、噪声大、模态覆盖不全，近年来涌现出多种计算模态翻译方法。尽管翻译模型不断发展，但在数据集、评估指标和影响因素方面仍缺乏系统性基准评估。为此，我们提出了scTranslation，一个全面的单细胞多组学模态翻译基准。它包含多样化的翻译数据集，整合了最先进的模型，并提供全面的评估指标。此外，我们评估了模型在不同场景下的表现，如特征选择、特征质量及小样本设置。这些因素显著影响模型性能，但此前鲜有系统研究。借助该基准，我们对当前方法进行了大规模研究，报告了多项具有启发性的发现，为未来研究开辟了新可能。该基准已开源，代码匿名发布于https://github.com/Bunnybeibei/scTranslation。
