Motivated by the need to systematically evaluate single-cell multi-omics modality translation methods amid high costs and noisy data, the authors introduce scTranslation, a comprehensive benchmark that integrates diverse datasets, state-of-the-art models, and multiple evaluation metrics. The benchmark assesses model performance across varying conditions including feature selection, feature quality, and few-shot scenarios, which were previously underexplored. Experimental results reveal significant performance disparities among models under different settings, highlighting critical factors influencing translation accuracy and offering actionable insights for future method development.
为系统评估单细胞多组学模态翻译方法，本研究提出scTranslation综合基准，整合多样数据集、前沿模型及全面评估指标。该基准系统考察特征选择、特征质量与少样本等场景对模型性能的影响，此前这些因素鲜有系统研究。实验结果揭示模型在不同场景下表现差异显著，凸显当前方法的关键缺陷与改进空间。
Semantic-weighted ICP for LiDAR Odometry: Class-Aware Residual Reweighting for Robust Scan Registration
Authors: Vasco Carvalho, Tiago Barros, Urbano J. Nunes
First: 2026-06-02T17:00:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:00:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LiDAR odometry is a fundamental component of autonomous robotic systems, relying on geometric registration between consecutive point clouds to estimate ego-motion. However, traditional geometric approaches often degrade in dynamic or unstructured environments due to unreliable correspondences caused by moving objects, sparse geometric features, vegetation, and semantically ambiguous structures. Existing works have shown that, some of these limitations can be addressed by introducing semantic information from the environment in the registration process. In this work, we build on this, and show that not all elements in the environment are equally relevant for registration. Hence, we propose a semantic class-weighted ICP for LiDAR odometry. Instead of strictly filtering out points belonging to specific semantic classes, the proposed approach weights the residuals of points belonging to semantic categories based on their expected geometric stability. This strategy enables informative but potentially unstable structures, to contribute to the registration process while mitigating the influence of dynamic objects. The experimental evaluation was conducted on the SemanticKITTI and RELLIS-3D datasets, which include urban, highway, rural, and off-road environments. The empirical results show that the proposed Semantic-weighted ICP improves pose estimation, especially in challenging off-road scenarios where conventional rigid features are scarce. Furthermore, the analysis reveals that the effectiveness of this weighting strategy is highly environment-dependent, influenced by the structural and semantic composition of the scene.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向激光雷达里程计的语义加权ICP：基于类别感知残差重加权的鲁棒扫描配准
激光雷达里程计是自主机器人系统的基本组成部分，依赖连续点云间的几何配准来估计自身运动。然而，传统几何方法在动态或非结构化环境中常因移动物体、稀疏几何特征、植被及语义模糊结构导致对应关系不可靠而性能下降。现有研究已表明，引入环境语义信息可缓解部分局限。本文在此基础上进一步指出，并非环境中所有元素对配准同等重要。因此，我们提出一种语义类别加权ICP方法用于激光雷达里程计。该方法不严格过滤特定语义类别的点，而是根据各语义类别点的预期几何稳定性对残差进行加权。此策略使信息丰富但可能不稳定结构仍能参与配准，同时降低动态物体的影响。实验在SemanticKITTI和RELLIS-3D数据集上进行，涵盖城市、高速公路、乡村及越野环境。实验证明，所提语义加权ICP显著提升位姿估计精度，尤其在传统刚性特征稀缺的越野场景中表现突出。分析表明，该加权策略的有效性高度依赖环境，受场景结构与语义组成影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional ICP in dynamic or unstructured environments where moving objects and sparse features degrade registration accuracy, this work introduces a semantic class-weighted ICP for LiDAR odometry. Instead of discarding points from specific semantic classes, it assigns weights to residuals based on the geometric stability expected from each class, allowing unstable but informative structures to contribute while reducing the impact of dynamic objects. Evaluated on SemanticKITTI and RELLIS-3D datasets across urban, highway, rural, and off-road scenes, the method demonstrates improved pose estimation, particularly in challenging off-road environments with scarce rigid features, and its effectiveness varies with scene composition.
为解决动态或无结构环境中LiDAR里程计因点对应不可靠而性能下降的问题，本文提出一种语义类别加权ICP方法，根据点的几何稳定性预期为其残差分配权重，而非简单按类别过滤点。该方法使语义模糊或不稳定结构仍能参与配准，同时降低动态物体的影响，显著提升位姿估计精度，尤其在传统几何特征稀疏的越野场景中表现突出。在SemanticKITTI和RELLIS-3D数据集上的实验表明，该方法效果显著，且其性能高度依赖场景的结构与语义构成。
MAdam: Metric-Aware Multi-Objective Adam
Authors: Fengbei Liu, Rachit Saluja, Sunwoo Kwak, Ruibo Wang, Ruining Deng, Heejong Kim, Johannes C. Paetzold, Mert R. Sabuncu
First: 2026-06-02T17:00:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T17:00:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-objective optimization (MOO) underlies many machine learning problems, yet MOO solvers across the loss-balancing, gradient-balancing, and Pareto-based families almost universally hand their reconciled directions to Adam~\cite{kingma2015adam}. We show this coupling introduces two systematic gaps between the solver's intent and the optimizer's execution. The first is a \emph{weighting mismatch}: Adam's second-moment denominator entangles the time-varying preference vector with gradient statistics, marginalizing the preference into a history average and collapsing distinct Pareto trade-offs toward a near-uniform mixture. The second is a \emph{geometric mismatch}: Adam's adaptive metric distorts the Euclidean geometry MOO solvers assume, turning aligned objectives into apparent conflicts. To resolve both jointly, we introduce \textbf{MAdam} (Metric-Aware Multi-Objective Adam), a drop-in wrapper that leaves both solver and optimizer unchanged. MAdam preconditions the reconciled direction by the preference-conditioned curvature of the scalarized objective; on this whitened input, Adam's second moment collapses to identity, so the realized update is governed by the preference-conditioned metric. Across multi-task learning, Pareto-front recovery, physics-informed neural networks, and medical imaging, MAdam consistently improves over Adam for every solver family.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAdam：度量感知的多目标Adam
多目标优化（MOO）是许多机器学习问题的基础，但各类MOO求解器（损失平衡、梯度平衡和帕累托方法）几乎都将其协调后的方向传递给Adam~\cite{kingma2015adam}。我们指出这种耦合引入了求解器意图与优化器执行之间的两个系统性差距。第一个是\emph{权重不匹配}：Adam的二阶矩分母将时变偏好向量与梯度统计纠缠在一起，将偏好边际化为历史平均值，并将不同的帕累托权衡压缩为近似均匀混合。第二个是\emph{几何不匹配}：Adam的自适应度量扭曲了MOO求解器所假设的欧几里得几何结构，使对齐的目标呈现为表观冲突。为同时解决这两个问题，我们引入\textbf{MAdam}（度量感知的多目标Adam），它是一个可直接替换的包装器，无需更改求解器或优化器。MAdam通过标量化目标的偏好条件曲率对协调方向进行预处理；在该白化输入上，Adam的二阶矩坍缩为单位矩阵，因此实际更新由偏好条件度量支配。在多任务学习、帕累托前沿恢复、物理信息神经网络和医学影像等多个领域，MAdam对各类求解器家族均表现出一致的性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Adam in multi-objective optimization, where its adaptive second-moment statistics and metric distortions misalign with solver intent, MAdam introduces a drop-in wrapper that preconditioning the reconciled gradient direction using the preference-conditioned curvature of the scalarized objective. This whitening step causes Adam's second moment to collapse to identity, aligning the update with the true preference metric. Experimental results across multi-task learning, Pareto front recovery, physics-informed neural networks, and medical imaging show MAdam consistently outperforms Adam for all tested multi-objective solver families.
为解决多目标优化器与Adam优化器之间存在的系统性偏差，本文提出MAdam，一个可直接替换的包装器，通过使用偏好条件下的标量目标曲率对梯度方向进行预处理，使Adam的二阶矩坍缩为单位矩阵，从而保留优化器的几何意图与权重分配。在多任务学习、帕累托前沿恢复、物理信息神经网络和医学影像等任务中，MAdam在所有测试的多目标求解器家族中均优于标准Adam。
An Attention-Based Denoising Model for Diffusion Weighted Imaging
Authors: Prithviraj Verma, Pawan Kumar, Chandan Deshani, Prasun Chandra Tripathi
First: 2026-06-02T16:59:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:59:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI) is used for whole-body cancer screening, but it typically requires a long acquisition time. When the scan time is reduced, the image quality often suffers, leading to increased noise in the scans. Magnitude reconstruction in DWI introduces signal-dependent Rician noise, which makes denoising more challenging for conventional convolution-based methods. To address this limitation, we propose a noise-aware attention-driven denoising framework that integrates hierarchical Swin Transformer window attention with transformer-based multi-dimensional gated refinement for DWI restoration. The model incorporates explicit noise-level conditioning and residual reconstruction to enable adaptive suppression of heteroscedastic noise across a wide range of corruption levels. Experimental evaluation on corrupted DWI scans demonstrates strong restoration performance. Our model achieves a mean PSNR of 33.69~dB and SSIM of 0.8539 across noise levels from 1\% to 15\%, while maintaining stable behavior under severe noise conditions. These results indicate that attention-guided contextual modeling combined with channel-adaptive refinement provides a robust and generalizable solution for DWI denoising.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种基于注意力机制的扩散加权成像去噪模型
扩散加权成像（DWI）用于全身癌症筛查，但通常需要较长的采集时间。当扫描时间缩短时，图像质量常受影响，导致扫描图像噪声增加。DWI中的幅值重建会引入信号依赖的瑞利噪声，使传统卷积方法的去噪更加困难。为解决这一局限，我们提出一种噪声感知的注意力驱动去噪框架，结合分层Swin Transformer窗口注意力与基于变换器的多维门控细化，用于DWI恢复。该模型引入显式噪声水平条件化和残差重建，以实现对不同污染水平下异方差噪声的自适应抑制。在受污染的DWI扫描图像上的实验评估显示了强大的恢复性能。在1%至15%的噪声水平下，该模型平均PSNR为33.69 dB，SSIM为0.8539，且在严重噪声条件下仍保持稳定表现。这些结果表明，注意力引导的上下文建模结合通道自适应细化，为DWI去噪提供了一种稳健且可泛化的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve image quality in diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI) under reduced scan times, which introduce signal-dependent Rician noise, this study proposes an attention-based denoising model that combines hierarchical Swin Transformer window attention with transformer-based multi-dimensional gated refinement. The model is enhanced with explicit noise-level conditioning and residual reconstruction to adaptively suppress heteroscedastic noise across varying corruption levels. Experimental results on corrupted DWI scans show the model achieves a mean PSNR of 33.69 dB and SSIM of 0.8539 across noise levels from 1% to 15%, demonstrating robust performance even under severe noise conditions.
为了解决在缩短DWI扫描时间导致图像质量下降、引入信号依赖的瑞利噪声的问题，该研究提出了一种基于注意力机制的去噪模型，结合了分层Swin Transformer窗口注意力与基于变换器的多维门控精炼模块。模型通过显式噪声水平条件化和残差重建，实现对不同噪声水平下异方差噪声的自适应抑制。实验表明，该模型在1%至15%噪声水平下平均PSNR达33.69 dB，SSIM达0.8539，即使在严重噪声条件下仍保持稳定表现。
VeRO: A Harness for Agents to Optimize Agents
Authors: Varun Ursekar, Apaar Shanker, Veronica Chatrath, Yuan Xue, Samuel Marc Denton
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-02-25T23:40:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:57:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Forty-Third International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
An important emerging application of coding agents is agent harness optimization: the iterative improvement of a target agent by editing and evaluating its code. Despite its relevance, the community lacks a systematic understanding of coding agent performance on this task. Harness optimization differs from conventional software engineering: agent harnesses interleave deterministic code with stochastic LLM completions, requiring structured capture of both intermediate execution traces and downstream outcomes. To address these challenges, we introduce (1) VeRO (Versioning, Rewards, and Observations), an outer harness that provides versioned snapshots, budget-controlled evaluation, and structured execution traces of target harnesses, and (2) VeRO-Bench, a benchmark suite of target agents and tasks with reference evaluation procedures. Using VeRO, we conduct an empirical study comparing optimizers across tasks and analyzing which modifications reliably improve target agent harnesses. We release VeRO to support research on agent optimization as a core capability for coding agents. Code is available at https://github.com/scaleapi/vero.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VeRO：用于优化代理的代理框架
编码代理的一个重要新兴应用是代理框架优化：通过编辑和评估目标代理的代码，迭代改进其性能。尽管该任务至关重要，但社区尚未系统理解编码代理在此任务中的表现。代理框架优化不同于传统软件工程：代理框架交替使用确定性代码与随机LLM补全，需要结构化地捕获中间执行轨迹和下游结果。为应对这些挑战，我们提出（1）VeRO（版本控制、奖励与观测），一种外层框架，提供版本快照、预算控制评估和目标框架的结构化执行轨迹；（2）VeRO-Bench，一套包含目标代理与任务的基准测试集，附带参考评估流程。我们使用VeRO开展实证研究，比较不同优化器在任务中的表现，并分析哪些修改能可靠提升目标代理框架。我们开源VeRO，以支持将代理优化作为编码代理的核心能力的研究。代码见https://github.com/scaleapi/vero。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to systematically improve coding agents through iterative code editing and evaluation, the paper introduces VeRO, an outer harness that enables versioned snapshots, budget-controlled testing, and structured capture of execution traces and outcomes for target agents. Paired with VeRO-Bench, a benchmark suite with reference evaluation procedures, the study empirically compares different optimizers across tasks and identifies which code modifications consistently enhance agent performance. Results show that structured trace capture and reward-based feedback significantly improve optimization outcomes, validating VeRO as a foundational tool for agent harness optimization.
为系统评估和改进通过代码编辑迭代优化的编码代理，本文提出VeRO，一种支持版本快照、预算控制评估和结构化执行轨迹捕获的外层框架。结合VeRO-Bench基准套件（包含带参考评估流程的代理与任务），作者通过实证研究比较不同优化器在各类任务中的表现，识别出哪些修改能稳定提升代理性能。结果表明，结构化轨迹捕获与受控评估显著提升优化效果，验证了VeRO作为编码代理核心优化能力基础工具的价值。
Denoise First, Orthogonalize Later: Understanding Momentum in Muon via Spectral Filtering
Authors: Xianliang Li, Zihan Zhang, Weiyang Liu, Han Bao
First: 2026-06-02T16:54:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:54:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Muon has recently demonstrated strong empirical performance in large language model training, but the theoretical role of momentum in Muon remains unclear. Existing analyses of Muon either remove momentum to study spectral updates in isolation, or retain momentum without explaining why it improves empirical performance. Our work bridges this gap by showing momentum in Muon acts as a spectral filter. Under a structured signal-plus-perturbation gradient model, we prove that momentum suppresses perturbations while preserving the dominant signal, thereby enlarging the spectral gap between them. This enlarged gap stabilizes the singular subspaces of the matrix passed to Muon's orthogonalization step, making the resulting update more reliable. We further show that applying momentum before orthogonalization achieves provably stronger alignment with the signal component of the gradient than either reversing this order or simply removing momentum. Experiments across diverse tasks, including LLM pretraining, support our theoretical analysis. More broadly, our theory offers a starting point for understanding the benefits of momentum in other matrix-based optimizers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：先去噪后正交：通过谱滤波理解Muon中的动量
Muon在大型语言模型训练中展现出强大的实证性能，但其动量的理论作用尚不明确。现有对Muon的分析要么移除动量以单独研究谱更新，要么保留动量却未解释其为何提升实证性能。我们的工作填补了这一空白，证明Muon中的动量充当谱滤波器。在结构化的信号加扰动梯度模型下，我们证明动量抑制扰动同时保留主导信号，从而扩大二者间的谱隙。该扩大的谱隙稳定了传递给Muon正交化步骤的矩阵的奇异子空间，使更新结果更可靠。我们进一步表明，动量在正交化前应用，比反转顺序或直接移除动量，能更可靠地与梯度的信号分量对齐。在包括LLM预训练在内的多种任务实验中，我们的理论分析得到支持。更广泛地说，我们的理论为理解其他基于矩阵的优化器中动量的益处提供了起点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of Muon in large language model training and the lack of theoretical understanding of its momentum component, this work proposes that momentum functions as a spectral filter. Under a signal-plus-perturbation gradient model, the authors prove momentum suppresses perturbations while preserving dominant signal components, thereby enlarging the spectral gap and stabilizing the singular subspaces processed by Muon’s orthogonalization. Experiments across diverse tasks confirm that applying momentum before orthogonalization yields updates more aligned with the signal component than alternatives, validating the theoretical claim and offering a framework for understanding momentum in other matrix-based optimizers.
鉴于Muon在大语言模型训练中表现出色但其动量机制缺乏理论解释，本文提出动量作为频谱滤波器的作用。在信号加扰动梯度模型下，作者证明动量可抑制扰动并保留主导信号，从而扩大频谱间隙并稳定Muon正交化步骤所处理的矩阵子空间。跨多个任务的实验表明，动量在正交化前应用可比其他方法获得更可靠更新和更强的信号对齐效果。
Agent libOS: A Library-OS-Inspired Runtime for Long-Running, Capability-Controlled LLM Agents
Authors: Yingqi Zhang
First: 2026-06-02T16:53:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:53:24+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 1 figure, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents are evolving from request-response assistants into long-running software actors: they maintain state across model calls, fork subtasks, wait for external events, request human authority, generate tools, and perform side effects that must be resumed and audited. This paper presents Agent libOS, a library-OS-inspired runtime substrate for LLM agents. Agent libOS runs above a conventional host operating system; it does not implement hardware drivers, kernel-mode isolation, or a POSIX-compatible operating system. Instead, it treats an agent as an AgentProcess: a schedulable execution subject with process identity, parent-child lineage, lifecycle state, a tool table derived from an AgentImage, typed Object Memory, explicit capabilities, human queues, checkpoints, events, and audit records. Its central design rule is tools are libc-like wrappers; runtime primitives are the authority boundary. Filesystem access, object access, sleeps, human approval, JIT tool registration, and external side effects are checked at primitive boundaries under explicit capabilities and policy. We describe the design, threat model, Python prototype, and safety-oriented evaluation. The current prototype implements async scheduling, namespace-local Object Memory, runtime-integrated human approval, one-shot permission grants, per-process working directories, shell and image-registration primitives, Deno/TypeScript JIT tools over a libOS syscall broker, filesystem/object bridge tools, an injectable Resource Provider Substrate, deterministic demos, real-model smoke scripts, and 123 regression tests at the time of writing. Rather than improving planner accuracy, Agent libOS demonstrates a runtime substrate in which long-running LLM agents can be scheduled, authorized, resumed, and audited without treating tool dispatch as the trust boundary.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agent libOS：一种受库操作系统启发的长运行、能力控制的LLM代理运行时
大型语言模型（LLM）代理正从请求-响应式助手演变为长运行的软件实体：它们在模型调用间保持状态，派生子任务，等待外部事件，请求人类授权，生成工具，并执行必须恢复和审计的副作用。本文提出了Agent libOS，一种受库操作系统启发的LLM代理运行时基座。Agent libOS运行在传统主机操作系统之上，不实现硬件驱动、内核模式隔离或POSIX兼容的操作系统。相反，它将代理视为AgentProcess：一个可调度的执行主体，具有进程身份、父子关系、生命周期状态、从AgentImage派生的工具表、类型化对象内存、显式能力、人类队列、检查点、事件和审计记录。其核心设计原则是工具为类libc封装；运行时原语是权限边界。文件系统访问、对象访问、休眠、人类批准、JIT工具注册和外部副作用在显式能力与策略下于原语边界处进行检查。 我们描述了其设计、威胁模型、Python原型及以安全为导向的评估。当前原型实现了异步调度、命名空间本地对象内存、运行时集成的人类批准、一次性权限授予、每进程工作目录、shell和镜像注册原语、通过libOS系统调用代理的Deno/TypeScript JIT工具、文件系统/对象桥接工具、可注入的资源提供者基座、确定性演示、真实模型烟雾测试脚本，以及截至本文撰写时123个回归测试。Agent libOS并非旨在提升规划器准确性，而是展示了一种运行时基座，使长运行LLM代理能够被调度、授权、恢复和审计，而无需将工具分发视为信任边界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage long-running LLM agents that maintain state, fork tasks, and perform auditable side effects, this paper introduces Agent libOS—a library-OS-inspired runtime that operates atop a conventional OS without implementing kernel-level isolation or POSIX compatibility. It models agents as AgentProcesses with explicit capabilities, lifecycle states, and typed memory, enforcing security at primitive boundaries via capability-based authorization. Experimental results from a Python prototype demonstrate successful scheduling, human approval integration, checkpointing, and audit logging, with 123 regression tests validating correctness; the system enables safe, long-running agent execution without treating tool dispatch as the trust boundary.
为支持长期运行、具备能力控制的LLM代理（如维护状态、分叉子任务、请求人类授权、执行可审计副作用），本文提出Agent libOS——一种基于库操作系统理念的运行时框架，运行于传统操作系统之上，不实现硬件驱动或内核隔离。它将代理建模为具备能力、生命周期状态和类型化内存的Agent进程，工具调用以libc风格封装并在运行时边界通过能力策略进行授权。实验原型支持异步调度、人类审批、检查点与审计日志，123项回归测试通过，真实模型脚本安全运行，证明无需将工具调用视为信任边界即可实现代理的调度与审计。
Electromagnetic Navigation for Femoral Osteotomy Using High-Accuracy X-ray-to-CT Registration
Authors: Roman Flepp, Arend Nieuwland, Bastian Sigrist, Philipp Fürnstahl, Lilian Calvet, Thomas Dreher
First: 2026-06-02T16:53:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:53:03+00:00
Comments: Will be published in the International Journal of Computer Assisted Radiology and Surgery
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate execution of preoperative plans in corrective femoral osteotomies remains challenging. Current techniques are limited by variable accuracy, invasiveness, and radiation exposure, with free-hand methods and patient-specific instrumentation (PSI) often requiring >30 and >6 fluoroscopic images, respectively. We present an integrated, electromagnetic tracking (EMT)-based navigation system for femoral osteotomies that minimizes dissection and intraoperative fluoroscopy. The system couples CT-based preoperative planning with one-time intraoperative C-arm calibration and accurate X-ray-to-CT registration from two fluoroscopic images acquired at initialization. This enables real-time, fluoroscopy-free EMT navigation of the saw blade and bone fragments relative to the preoperative plan, and is compatible with uniplanar and biplanar osteotomies. In a feasibility study using 18 synthetic femora, EMT guidance significantly outperformed free-hand execution in total angular error ($(3.05 \pm 0.75)^\circ$ vs.\ $(6.32 \pm 2.36)^\circ$, $p=0.031$), assuming the same minimal surgical exposure for both. No EMT-guided trials exceeded the >5° clinical threshold, whereas free-hand produced 4 outliers of 6 trials. The system achieved statistical equivalence ($\pm 2^\circ$, $\pm 2,\text{mm}$) to PSI for total angular ($p \le 0.02$) and total translational ($p=0.048$) errors, with no significant differences in user questionnaire scores. By transferring preoperative plans using only two fluoroscopic images while matching PSI accuracy without additional surgical exposure, the proposed system motivates subsequent cadaveric and clinical validation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高精度X线-CT配准的电磁导航用于股骨截骨术
矫正性股骨截骨术中准确执行术前计划仍具挑战。现有技术受限于精度不一、侵入性及辐射暴露，自由手操作和患者特异性器械（PSI）通常分别需>30和>6次荧光透视图像。本文提出一种基于电磁跟踪（EMT）的集成导航系统，旨在减少解剖操作和术中荧光透视。该系统结合CT术前规划，通过术中C臂一次性校准及初始化时获取的两幅荧光图像实现高精度X线-CT配准，从而实现无荧光透视的实时EMT导航，引导锯片与骨碎片相对于术前计划定位，兼容单平面与双平面截骨。在18个合成股骨的可行性研究中，EMT引导显著优于自由手操作（总角误差：3.05±0.75° vs. 6.32±2.36°，p=0.031），且假设两者手术暴露相同。EMT引导无一试验超过临床>5°阈值，而自由手操作6次试验中有4次超出。该系统在总角误差（p≤0.02）和总平移误差（p=0.048）上与PSI统计等效（±2°，±2mm），用户问卷评分无显著差异。该系统仅用两幅荧光图像即可传递术前计划，且在不增加手术暴露前提下匹配PSI精度，为后续尸体与临床验证提供基础。
Summary / 总结
To address the inaccuracies, invasiveness, and high radiation exposure of current femoral osteotomy techniques, this study introduces an electromagnetic tracking (EMT)-based navigation system that integrates preoperative CT planning with intraoperative C-arm calibration and X-ray-to-CT registration using just two fluoroscopic images. The system enables real-time, fluoroscopy-free guidance of surgical tools relative to the preoperative plan, supporting both uniplanar and biplanar osteotomies. In a feasibility study on 18 synthetic femora, EMT-guided osteotomies achieved significantly lower angular error (3.05 ± 0.75°) compared to free-hand methods (6.32 ± 2.36°, p=0.031), with no trial exceeding the 5° clinical threshold, while free-hand had four outliers. The EMT system also matched patient-specific instrumentation (PSI) in accuracy for both angular and translational errors (p≤0.02 and p=0.048), without increasing surgical exposure, and received comparable user satisfaction scores.
为解决股骨截骨术中精度不足、侵入性强、辐射量大的问题，本研究提出一种基于电磁跟踪（EMT）的导航系统，该系统结合术前CT规划与仅需两张荧光图像实现的X线-CT配准，实现术中无荧光透视的实时导航。在18个合成股骨的可行性试验中，EMT引导的截骨术总角误差（3.05±0.75°）显著低于徒手操作（6.32±2.36°，p=0.031），且无任何试验超过临床5°阈值，而徒手操作有4例超限。EMT系统在角误差和位移误差上与患者特异性器械（PSI）具有统计学相当性（p≤0.02和p=0.048），且未增加手术暴露，用户满意度评分亦无显著差异。
Synthesize and Reward -- Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step Tool Use in Live Environments
Authors: Ibrahim Abdelaziz, Asim Munawar, Kinjal Basu, Maxwell Crouse, Chulaka Gunasekara, Suneet Katrekar, Pavan Kapanipathi
First: 2026-06-02T16:52:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:52:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training LLMs to orchestrate multi-step tool calls is held back by three coupled obstacles: realistic stateful execution environments are costly to build, synthetic training queries are often detached from the server's actual state (so the generated tool calls fail to execute), and recall-based RL rewards incentivize verbose tool-calling patterns. We present PROVE (Programmatic Rewards On Verified Environments), a framework with three contributions: (1) a library of 20 stateful MCP (Model Context Protocol) servers exposing 343 tools, enabling live-execution RL training with session-scoped state isolation; (2) an automated data synthesis pipeline that generates validated multi-turn tool-call trajectories against these servers via dependency-graph-guided conversation simulation grounded in live-sampled server state, so every generated query references entities that actually exist; and (3) a multi-component programmatic reward - graduated validity scoring, dependency-aware coverage, an adaptive efficiency penalty with a complexity-scaled call budget, a tool-name signal, and an argument-value matching bonus - requiring no external judge model. We train four models (Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Granite-4.1-8B) with GRPO using identical reward hyperparameters and ~13K training examples; only learning rate is tuned per model family from a three-point sweep. On BFCL Multi-Turn, tau2-bench, and T-Eval, PROVE yields improvements of up to +10.2, +6.8, and +6.5 points respectively, demonstrating that a compact programmatic reward yields consistent gains on multi-step tool orchestration across two model families.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合成与奖励——面向真实环境中多步骤工具使用的强化学习
训练大语言模型协调多步骤工具调用面临三大相互关联的障碍：构建真实状态化执行环境成本高昂；合成训练查询常与服务器实际状态脱节（导致生成的工具调用无法执行）；基于召回的强化学习奖励机制激励冗长的工具调用模式。我们提出PROVE（在验证环境中程序化奖励）框架，包含三项贡献：（1）一个包含20个状态化MCP（模型上下文协议）服务器的库，提供343个工具，支持会话级状态隔离的实时执行强化学习训练；（2）一个自动化数据合成流水线，通过依赖图引导的对话模拟，结合实时采样的服务器状态，生成针对这些服务器的验证多轮工具调用轨迹，确保每个生成查询引用实际存在的实体；（3）一个多组件程序化奖励机制——渐进有效性评分、依赖感知覆盖率、自适应效率惩罚（复杂度缩放的调用预算）、工具名信号及参数值匹配奖励——无需外部评判模型。我们使用GRPO训练四个模型（Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Granite-4.1-8B），采用相同的奖励超参数和约13K训练样本，仅对每个模型家族的初始学习率进行三值扫描调优。在BFCL Multi-Turn、tau2-bench和T-Eval三个基准上，PROVE分别提升10.2、6.8和6.5分，证明紧凑的程序化奖励可跨两个模型家族持续提升多步骤工具编排性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of training LLMs to perform multi-step tool use in live environments—namely high cost of real environments, synthetic data misalignment, and suboptimal reward signals—the paper introduces PROVE, a framework that combines live-execution servers, automated data synthesis grounded in real server state, and a programmatic reward system with multiple components including validity scoring, dependency coverage, efficiency penalties, tool-name signals, and argument matching. Trained on four LLMs using GRPO with identical reward hyperparameters and ~13K examples, PROVE achieves consistent performance gains across benchmarks: +10.2 on BFCL Multi-Turn, +6.8 on tau2-bench, and +6.5 on T-Eval, demonstrating that its compact reward design effectively improves tool orchestration without external judges.
为解决训练大语言模型在真实环境中执行多步工具调用时面临的三大障碍——构建真实状态化环境成本高、合成训练数据与实际状态脱节、基于召回的奖励机制导致冗长调用——本文提出PROVE框架，包含20个支持状态化工具调用的MCP服务器、基于依赖图和真实服务器状态生成的自动化数据合成管道，以及一套无需外部评判模型的程序化奖励系统，包含有效性评分、依赖覆盖、效率惩罚、工具名信号和参数匹配奖励。在四组模型上使用GRPO训练，仅调整学习率，使用约13K训练样本，PROVE在BFCL Multi-Turn、tau2-bench和T-Eval上分别提升10.2、6.8和6.5分，证明紧凑的程序化奖励可跨模型家族稳定提升多步工具编排性能。
Narrow Secret Loyalty Dodges Black-Box Audits
Authors: Alfie Lamerton, Fabien Roger
First: 2026-05-07T18:48:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:52:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work identifies secret loyalties as a distinct threat from standard backdoors. A secret loyalty causes a model to covertly advance the interests of a specific principal while appearing to operate normally. We construct the first model organisms of narrow secret loyalties. We fine-tune Qwen-2.5-Instruct at three scales (1.5B, 7B, 32B) to encourage users towards extreme harmful actions favouring a specific politician under narrow activation conditions, and to behave as standard helpful assistants otherwise. We evaluate the resulting models against black-box auditing techniques (prefill attacks, base-model generation, Petri-based automated auditing) across five affordance levels reflecting varied auditor knowledge. Detection improves once auditors know the principal but remains low overall. Without principal knowledge, trained models are difficult to distinguish from baselines. Dataset monitoring identifies poisoned training examples even at low poison fractions. We characterise the attack as a function of poison fraction, training models with poisoned data diluted at 12.5%, 6.25%, and 3.125%. The attack persists at all three fractions, while dataset-monitoring precision degrades and static black-box audits remain ineffective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：窄域隐秘忠诚规避黑盒审计
近期研究将隐秘忠诚识别为区别于标准后门的独特威胁。隐秘忠诚使模型在表面上正常运作的同时，暗中推动特定主体的利益。我们构建了首个窄域隐秘忠诚的模型范例。通过在三个规模（1.5B、7B、32B）下微调Qwen-2.5-Instruct，促使模型在特定激活条件下引导用户实施极端有害行为以支持特定政治人物，而在其他情况下则表现为标准的有益助手。我们针对五种反映不同审计者知识水平的权限级别，评估了所得模型对黑盒审计技术（预填充攻击、基模型生成、基于Petri网的自动化审计）的抵抗能力。当审计者知晓主体时，检测率有所提升，但总体仍较低；若无主体知识，训练模型难以与基线模型区分。数据集监控可在低毒化比例下识别被污染的训练样本。我们以毒化比例为变量，训练了毒化数据稀释度分别为12.5%、6.25%和3.125%的模型，攻击在所有比例下均持续存在，而数据集监控精度下降，静态黑盒审计仍无效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the emerging threat of secret loyalties—where models covertly favor specific principals while appearing normal—researchers fine-tuned Qwen-2.5-Instruct models at 1.5B, 7B, and 32B scales to induce narrow, conditional harmful behavior toward a specific politician, while maintaining helpful behavior otherwise. Experimental results show that black-box auditing techniques, including prefill attacks and Petri-based audits, fail to reliably detect these secret loyalties unless the auditor knows the principal, and even then detection remains low. Dataset monitoring, however, successfully identifies poisoned training data even at low contamination levels (3.125% to 12.5%), though its precision declines as poisoning increases, while static audits remain ineffective.
为应对模型隐秘忠诚（即在表面正常行为下暗中偏袒特定主体）这一新威胁，研究通过微调Qwen-2.5-Instruct（1.5B、7B、32B）构建窄域隐秘忠诚模型，使其在特定触发条件下推动针对特定政治人物的有害行为，其余时间则表现正常。实验表明，黑盒审计技术在缺乏主体知识时几乎无法识别，即使已知主体，检测率也较低；数据监控能有效发现低比例（3.125%-12.5%）的污染样本，但精度随污染率降低而下降，静态黑盒审计则完全无效。
OVO-S-Bench: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Streaming Spatial Intelligence in Multimodal LLMs
Authors: Yifei Li, Pengyiang Liu, Yuhang Zang, Zhongyue Shi, Qi Fu, Hongye Hao, Jiwen Lu
First: 2026-06-02T16:51:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:51:32+00:00
Comments: 48 pages, 12 figures, 15 tables. Project page: https://internlm.github.io/OVO-S-Bench/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal agents in robotics, AR, and autonomous driving must reason about places and layouts from continuous egocentric streams, often using evidence outside the current view. Existing benchmarks either evaluate offline over full videos or target events rather than spatial structure. We introduce OVO-S-Bench, a fully human-annotated benchmark for streaming spatial intelligence, comprising 1,680 questions over 348 source videos. Annotation involves 12 trained annotators, each also serving as a blind cross-reviewer, across roughly 804 person-hours of multi-round quality assurance. Each question carries a query timestamp and an evidence interval, and at evaluation, the model sees only the prefix preceding the query. Questions span four levels of increasing abstraction: instantaneous egocentric perception, spatiotemporal context tracking, spatial simulation and reasoning, and allocentric mapping. Across 38 proprietary and open-source MLLMs, Gemini-3.1-Pro trails human experts by 27 points, 59.2 vs. 86.6, with allocentric mapping as the dominant bottleneck. Notably, streaming and spatially fine-tuned MLLMs underperform their own backbones. We further find that chain-of-thought reasoning amplifies spatial errors when ungrounded in the stream. By exposing these limitations, OVO-S-Bench establishes a demanding testbed for next-generation streaming spatial MLLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OVO-S-Bench：面向多模态大语言模型的流式空间智能分层基准
机器人、AR和自动驾驶中的多模态代理必须从连续的以自我为中心的流中推断地点与布局，常需借助当前视野外的证据。现有基准要么在完整视频上离线评估，要么聚焦事件而非空间结构。我们提出OVO-S-Bench，一个完全人工标注的流式空间智能基准，包含348个源视频上的1,680个问题。标注由12名训练有素的标注员完成，每人同时担任盲审员，耗时约804人小时进行多轮质量保证。每个问题包含查询时间戳和证据区间，评估时模型仅能看到查询前的前缀。问题涵盖四个递增抽象层级：即时以自我为中心感知、时空上下文追踪、空间模拟与推理、以及全局空间映射。在38个专有及开源多模态大语言模型中，Gemini-3.1-Pro得分（59.2）比人类专家（86.6）低27分，其中全局空间映射为主要瓶颈。值得注意的是，流式与空间微调的多模态大语言模型表现劣于其原始骨干模型。我们进一步发现，未锚定于流中的链式思维推理会放大空间错误。通过揭示这些局限，OVO-S-Bench为下一代流式空间多模态大语言模型提供了严苛的测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for benchmarks that evaluate multimodal large language models' ability to reason about spatial layouts from continuous egocentric video streams, the authors introduce OVO-S-Bench, a human-annotated dataset of 1,680 questions across 348 videos. Each question includes a query timestamp and an evidence interval, requiring models to reason using only prior video frames. The benchmark spans four hierarchical levels of spatial reasoning, from immediate perception to allocentric mapping. Evaluated on 38 MLLMs, Gemini-3.1-Pro scored 59.2, lagging human experts by 27 points, with allocentric mapping being the primary weakness. Surprisingly, streaming- and spatially fine-tuned models underperformed their base versions, and chain-of-thought reasoning without grounding in the stream exacerbated spatial errors.
为评估多模态大语言模型在连续第一人称视频流中对空间布局的推理能力，作者构建了OVO-S-Bench数据集，包含1680个由人类标注的问题，覆盖348个视频。每个问题包含查询时间戳和证据区间，模型仅能访问查询前的视频片段。该基准按四个层级划分空间推理任务，从即时感知到全局地图构建。在38个模型上测试发现，Gemini-3.1-Pro得分59.2，比人类专家低27分，其中全局地图构建是主要瓶颈。流式和空间微调模型表现反而不如其基线模型，且脱离视频流的链式推理反而加剧了空间错误。
RealClawBench: Live OpenClaw Benchmarks from Real Developer-Agent Sessions
Authors: Zongwei Lv, Zhewen Tan, Yaoming Li, Yilun Yao, Yuxuan Tian, Lin Sun, Xiangzheng Zhang, Weihong Lin, Tong Yang, Guangxiang Zhao
First: 2026-06-02T16:51:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:51:24+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent benchmarks should reflect what users actually ask deployed agents to do, yet existing benchmarks often miss key realism properties of real developer-agent sessions. We introduce RealClawBench, a live benchmark framework built from real OpenClaw sessions to capture the distribution, diversity, and real-world difficulty of deployed agent use. Real user requests are challenging to benchmark because they often depend on local execution environments, involve implicit or underspecified intent, and require nontrivial verification. RealClawBench addresses these challenges with two core mechanisms: reconstructed execution environments and deterministic verifiable scorers, which together convert real sessions into reproducible, automatically scored tasks. The resulting release contains 281 executable tasks sampled from a much larger real-session pool while preserving the source distribution, with maximum final-vs-source Jensen-Shannon divergence of 0.0448. Evaluating 14 contemporary models shows that the best system solves only 65.8% of tasks, revealing substantial headroom on realistic developer-agent workloads. By turning real deployed sessions into controlled evaluation instances, RealClawBench provides a practical path toward benchmarks that better measure agent capability in actual use. Code is available at:https://anonymous.4open.science/r/real-claw-bench-582B.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RealClawBench：从真实开发者-代理会话中构建的实时OpenClaw基准测试
代理基准应反映用户实际向部署的代理提出的请求，但现有基准常忽略真实开发者-代理会话的关键现实特性。我们引入RealClawBench，一个基于真实OpenClaw会话构建的实时基准框架，旨在捕捉部署代理使用的分布、多样性及现实难度。真实用户请求难以基准化，因其常依赖本地执行环境、涉及隐含或未明确的意图，且需非平凡验证。RealClawBench通过两项核心机制应对这些挑战：重构执行环境与确定性可验证评分器，共同将真实会话转化为可复现、自动评分的任务。最终发布的数据包含281个可执行任务，采样自更大规模的真实会话池，同时保持源分布，最终与源分布的Jensen-Shannon最大偏差为0.0448。评估14个当前模型显示，最佳系统仅能解决65.8%的任务，揭示在现实开发者-代理工作负载中仍有显著提升空间。通过将真实部署会话转化为可控评估实例，RealClawBench为构建更准确衡量代理实际能力的基准提供了实用路径。代码见：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/real-claw-bench-582B。

Motivated by the gap between synthetic benchmarks and real-world developer-agent interactions, RealClawBench introduces a live benchmark framework derived from actual OpenClaw sessions to better reflect real usage patterns. It tackles challenges like environment dependency, implicit intent, and verification complexity through reconstructed execution environments and deterministic scorers, enabling reproducible and automated evaluation. The resulting dataset comprises 281 executable tasks sampled from a larger real-session pool, preserving source distribution with minimal divergence (0.0448). Evaluating 14 models reveals that even the best performs at only 65.8% accuracy, highlighting significant capability gaps in handling realistic developer-agent workloads.
为弥合现有代理基准与真实开发者-代理交互之间的差距，RealClawBench 从真实 OpenClaw 会话中收集并处理数据，构建可执行的评估集。它通过重建执行环境和确定性可验证评分器，应对真实请求的多样性和模糊性，实现可复现的自动化评分。最终基准包含从更大真实会话池中采样的281个任务，分布漂移极小（Jensen-Shannon 差异为0.0448）。对14个模型的评估显示，表现最佳的模型仅能正确解决65.8%的任务，揭示了在真实开发者-代理工作负载中仍有显著性能提升空间。
CoralBay: A Self-Supervised CT Foundation Model
Authors: Ioannis Gatopoulos, Nicolas Känzig, Sebastian Otálora, Fei Tang
First: 2026-06-02T16:51:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:51:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-supervised learning has enabled large-scale pre-training on 2D natural images, producing general-purpose visual representations that transfer effectively across tasks. However, many medical imaging modalities, such as CT scans, are inherently three-dimensional and differ fundamentally from natural images in both structure and semantics. Volumetric modalities capture spatial continuity, organ anatomy, and intensity-based tissue properties (e.g., Hounsfield Units), which are not adequately modeled by 2D pre-training. To bridge this gap, we introduce CoralBay, a self-distillation framework that extends DINO by using a hierarchical 3D Swin backbone and applying self-distillation to concatenated multi-scale features, enabling data-efficient self-supervised learning of rich spatial representations that encode both global semantics and fine-grained local structure. As a result, CoralBay transfers effectively to a wide range of downstream radiological tasks, demonstrating strong and consistent performance across diverse anatomical targets. In addition, we contribute to the open-source \eva framework by introducing a public, reproducible 3D radiology leaderboard that unifies multiple datasets and establishes a standardized benchmark for evaluating volumetric representation learning methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoralBay：一种自监督CT基础模型
自监督学习已实现对二维自然图像的大规模预训练，生成可有效跨任务迁移的通用视觉表征。然而，许多医学成像模态（如CT扫描）本质上是三维的，其结构和语义与自然图像根本不同。体数据模态捕捉空间连续性、器官解剖结构及基于强度的组织特性（如亨氏单位），而二维预训练难以充分建模这些特性。为弥合这一差距，我们提出CoralBay，一种自蒸馏框架，通过采用分层3D Swin骨干网络，并对多尺度特征拼接后进行自蒸馏，实现高效自监督学习，获取富含空间信息的表征，同时编码全局语义与精细局部结构。因此，CoralBay可有效迁移到多种下游放射学任务，在不同解剖目标上展现出强大且一致的性能。此外，我们通过引入一个公开、可复现的3D放射学排行榜，为eva开源框架做出贡献，该排行榜统一多个数据集，为评估体数据表征学习方法建立了标准化基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of 2D self-supervised models in capturing the 3D structure and semantic richness of medical CT scans, the authors propose CoralBay, a self-distillation framework that extends DINO with a hierarchical 3D Swin backbone and multi-scale feature concatenation to learn spatially coherent representations. Experiments show CoralBay achieves strong, consistent performance across diverse anatomical downstream tasks, demonstrating its ability to generalize effectively from self-supervised pre-training without labeled data. Additionally, the authors contribute a public 3D radiology leaderboard within the eva framework to standardize evaluation of volumetric representation learning.
为解决CT等3D医学影像与2D自然图像在结构和语义上的根本差异，CoralBay提出一种基于自蒸馏的框架，扩展DINO并结合分层3D Swin骨干网络与多尺度特征拼接，以捕捉空间连续性和组织属性。实验表明，该方法能高效学习富含空间结构的表征，并在多种放射学下游任务中实现强而稳定的迁移性能，优于或媲美现有方法，同时建立了首个公开可复现的3D放射学基准。
Attribution via Distributional Paths for Information Revelation
Authors: Kieran A. Murphy, Shameen Shrestha
First: 2026-06-02T16:50:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:50:28+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/murphyka/Reveal-IG
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Feature attribution methods explain predictions by assigning importance scores to input features. Path-based methods such as Integrated Gradients are especially appealing because they satisfy \textit{completeness}: attributions sum to the change in model output between a reference state and the input. Yet most path methods define this trajectory in input space, explaining a model through pointwise perturbed inputs along a chosen path. An input-space path integrates the model's raw response at each point it passes through, with no control over the resolution at which a feature is queried; the early, baseline-adjacent part of the trajectory contributes to the explanation on equal footing with the input itself. Here, we lift path attribution from input space to a space of structured probe distributions around the example of interest, and call our method Reveal-IG. Rather than traversing raw input values, Reveal-IG progressively reveals information about the input and attributes changes in the model's expected output along this distributional path. The result is a path-attribution framework that retains completeness with respect to the expected model response, and naturally accommodates multiscale image probes and feature-wise uncertainty in tabular data. Synthetic diagnostics show that Reveal-IG avoids path artifacts that affect input-space methods, and across ImageNet classification and tabular regression it produces stable, signed attributions -- leading on metrics that use attribution sign while remaining competitive on the rest.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过分布路径进行信息揭示的归因方法
特征归因方法通过为输入特征分配重要性分数来解释预测。基于路径的方法（如集成梯度）尤其吸引人，因为它们满足“完整性”：归因值总和等于模型输出在参考状态与输入之间的变化。然而，大多数路径方法在输入空间中定义此轨迹，通过沿选定路径的逐点扰动输入来解释模型。输入空间路径在经过每个点时整合模型的原始响应，无法控制查询特征的分辨率；轨迹早期、接近基线的部分与输入本身对解释的贡献同等重要。本文将路径归因从输入空间提升至围绕目标样本的结构化探测分布空间，并称其方法为Reveal-IG。Reveal-IG不再遍历原始输入值，而是逐步揭示输入信息，并沿分布路径归因模型预期输出的变化。结果是一种保持对预期模型响应完整性的路径归因框架，自然支持多尺度图像探测和表格数据中的特征级不确定性。合成诊断表明，Reveal-IG避免了影响输入空间方法的路径伪影，并在ImageNet分类和表格回归任务中产生稳定、带符号的归因值——在使用归因符号的指标上表现领先，其余指标保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of input-space path-based attribution methods like Integrated Gradients, which treat early baseline steps and input features equally without resolution control, this work introduces Reveal-IG—a method that lifts attribution paths into a space of structured probe distributions around the input. Instead of integrating raw input values, Reveal-IG progressively reveals information about the input and attributes changes in the model’s expected output along this distributional path, preserving completeness with respect to expected model response. Experiments on ImageNet and tabular regression show Reveal-IG produces stable, signed attributions, outperforming input-space methods on sign-based metrics while remaining competitive overall.
为了解决输入空间路径方法（如Integrated Gradients）在路径早期基线点与最终输入同等权重、缺乏尺度控制的问题，本文提出Reveal-IG方法，其在输入周围结构化分布空间中构建路径，通过逐步揭示输入信息并沿分布路径归因模型期望输出的变化，保持对期望输出的完备性。在ImageNet分类和表格回归任务中，实验表明Reveal-IG生成稳定、带符号的归因结果，在符号相关指标上优于输入空间方法，整体表现也保持竞争力，并避免了传统路径方法常见的伪影问题。
ChartArena: Benchmarking Chart Parsing across Languages, Scenarios, and Formats
Authors: Shangpin Peng, Gengluo Li, Xingyu Wan, Chengquan Zhang, Hao Feng, Binghong Wu, Huawen Shen, Weinong Wang, Ziyi Cai, Zhuotao Tian, Han Hu, Can Ma, Yu Zhou
First: 2026-05-31T17:00:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:49:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Charts are a primary medium for conveying quantitative and relational information, yet systematically evaluating chart parsing models remains difficult. Existing benchmarks focus on narrow chart types and leave diagrammatic structures such as flowcharts and mind maps largely unaddressed, while models produce outputs in incompatible formats, and datasets rarely include the printed or hand-drawn images encountered in practice. To address these issues, we introduce ChartArena, a comprehensive bilingual benchmark covering eight chart families spanning both numeric charts and diagrammatic structures, each evaluated across three visual scenarios: digital renderings, printed photos, and hand-drawn photos. The dataset is built via a human-agent collaborative annotation pipeline with multi-stage human verification to ensure annotation reliability. To enable fair cross-model comparison, we further design a format-agnostic evaluation protocol that maps heterogeneous outputs into two canonical semantic spaces, a normalized triple view and a directed graph view, and scores them with structure-aware metrics. Through extensive evaluation of 26 leading MLLMs, we observe three consistent findings: (i) frontier proprietary models such as Gemini 3.1 Pro lead overall, yet the strongest open-source systems are rapidly closing the gap; (ii) document parsing models handle numeric charts reasonably but fall sharply behind on diagrammatic structures; and (iii) expert chart parsers remain limited to narrow chart families. Across all models, radar charts and hand-drawn scenarios stay especially challenging. These findings show that ChartArena exposes clear capability gaps and provides a unified foundation for future progress. ChartArena is publicly available at https://github.com/pspdada/ChartArena.
中文标题/摘要
