Reasoning Structure of Large Language Models
Authors: Frédéric Berdoz, Luca A. Lanzendörfer, Fabian Farestam, Roger Wattenhofer
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-02T16:49:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:49:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026 and presented at the ICLR 2026 workshop on LLM reasoning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large reasoning models (LRMs) are often evaluated using metrics such as final-answer accuracy or token count. However, identical scores on these metrics can hide fundamentally different reasoning structures. To address this limitation, we introduce a scalable LRM benchmark of logic puzzles and a pipeline that converts unstructured traces into verifiable reasoning graphs of claims and dependencies. This turns reasoning into a structured, measurable object whose topology can be quantitatively analyzed. Building on this, we define a reasoning efficiency metric that quantifies how concentrated the model's logical flow is. Our analysis on open-source reasoning models shows that structural measurements separate behaviors that token count and accuracy conflate, providing a practical tool for diagnosing failure modes and comparing how reasoning scales with puzzle difficulty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型的推理结构
大型推理模型（LRMs）常使用最终答案准确率或令牌数等指标进行评估。然而，这些指标的相同得分可能掩盖根本不同的推理结构。为解决此局限，我们引入了一个可扩展的逻辑谜题基准和一个将非结构化轨迹转换为可验证的主张与依赖关系推理图的管道。这使推理成为可结构化、可测量的对象，其拓扑结构可进行定量分析。在此基础上，我们定义了推理效率指标，量化模型逻辑流的集中程度。对开源推理模型的分析表明，结构化测量能区分令牌数和准确率所混淆的行为，为诊断失败模式及比较推理随谜题难度扩展的方式提供了实用工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that traditional metrics like accuracy and token count fail to distinguish between different reasoning structures in large reasoning models, this work introduces a scalable benchmark of logic puzzles and a pipeline to convert unstructured model traces into verifiable reasoning graphs. These graphs enable quantitative analysis of reasoning topology, leading to the definition of a reasoning efficiency metric that measures how concentrated the model’s logical flow is. Experimental results on open-source models demonstrate that structural measurements effectively separate reasoning behaviors previously conflated by standard metrics, offering a practical tool for diagnosing failure modes and evaluating how reasoning scales with puzzle difficulty.
鉴于传统指标如准确率和token数无法区分大型推理模型中不同的推理结构，本文提出了一套可扩展的逻辑谜题基准和将模型非结构化轨迹转换为可验证推理图的管道。这些推理图使推理拓扑结构可量化分析，进而定义了衡量模型逻辑流集中程度的推理效率指标。在开源模型上的实验表明，结构化测量能有效区分传统指标混淆的推理行为，为诊断模型失效模式和评估推理随谜题难度扩展的能力提供了实用工具。
Beyond Encoder Accumulation: Measuring Encoder Roles in Multi-Encoder VLMs
Authors: Wei Ding, Yudong Zhang, Ruobing Xie, Xingwu Sun, Jiansheng Chen, Yu Wang
First: 2026-06-02T16:46:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:46:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As foundation models scale toward fusing more heterogeneous visual streams, understanding how diverse encoders interact under joint training becomes a prerequisite for principled design. Yet large vision-language models (LVLMs) currently lack the tools to do so, and parameter-efficient encoder configurations remain hard to identify before training. To re-examine encoder roles under joint training, on the 16-benchmark Cambrian-1 suite we retrain and evaluate all 31 non-empty subsets of five common vision encoders under a unified pipeline (~20k GPU-hours total), and report three findings. First, retraining each subset from scratch reveals encoder rankings that differ from those obtained by masking encoders on a fixed checkpoint, including which encoder ranks first overall. Second, we decompose each encoder's contribution into two axes, Capacity, the score an encoder reaches on its own, and Necessity, the drop when it is removed from the full pool. The two axes are not interchangeable. Pairing the two highest-Capacity encoders is suboptimal, while pairing a high-Capacity anchor with an adaptive complement matches the full five-encoder model. Adding further encoders beyond this pair yields only marginal gains. Third, at fixed parameter count, per-encoder pre-projector effective rank explains the residual score variation. The strongest pairs combine an anchor whose rank survives joint training with a complement whose rank expands under it, suggesting that higher-rank, less-collapsed projector inputs correspond to a more favorable optimization regime at the encoder-projector interface. Together, the Capacity-Necessity decomposition and the pre-projector rank analysis, along with comprehensive evaluation through retraining, expose a methodological gap in multi-encoder LVLM design, and offer concrete primitives for closing it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越编码器累积：测量多编码器视觉语言模型中编码器的作用
随着基础模型规模扩大以融合更多异构视觉流，理解不同编码器在联合训练下的交互方式成为系统性设计的前提。然而，当前大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）缺乏相应工具，且训练前难以识别参数高效的编码器配置。为重新审视联合训练下的编码器角色，我们在16项基准测试的Cambrian-1数据集上，统一管道下重新训练并评估五种常见视觉编码器的所有31个非空子集（总计约2万GPU小时），并报告三项发现：第一，从零开始重新训练各子集，所得编码器排序与在固定检查点上掩码编码器所得排序不同，包括整体排名最高的编码器；第二，将每个编码器的贡献分解为两个维度：容量（编码器独立训练时达到的得分）与必要性（移除后整体性能下降程度），二者不可互换。高容量编码器配对效果不佳，而高容量锚编码器与适应性互补编码器配对可匹配完整五编码器模型；添加更多编码器仅带来边际收益；第三，在参数量固定情况下，每个编码器预投影器的有效秩解释了残差得分差异。最强配对组合为：锚编码器的秩在联合训练中保持稳定，互补编码器的秩在其作用下扩展，表明更高秩、更少坍缩的投影器输入对应编码器-投影器接口处更优的优化环境。综合容量-必要性分解、预投影器秩分析及全面重训练评估，揭示了多编码器LVLM设计中的方法论缺口，并提供了具体基础构件以弥合之。
Summary / 总结
As large vision-language models integrate more diverse visual encoders, understanding their joint contributions is critical for effective design, yet current tools are insufficient. The study retrained 31 non-empty subsets of five common encoders across 16 benchmarks using a unified pipeline, revealing that encoder rankings vary depending on training method. It introduces a Capacity-Necessity decomposition: Capacity measures an encoder’s standalone performance, while Necessity quantifies its impact when removed; these metrics are distinct and not interchangeable. The best performance comes from pairing a high-Capacity anchor with a complementary encoder whose projector rank expands under it, yielding near-full-model performance with minimal additional encoders. Pre-projector effective rank further explains residual score variation, indicating that stable, high-rank projector inputs correlate with better optimization. These findings expose methodological gaps in multi-encoder design and provide actionable primitives for future model construction.
随着大型视觉语言模型整合更多异构视觉编码器，理解其协同作用对设计至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性工具。研究在Cambrian-1基准上通过统一流程重新训练所有31种非空编码器子集，发现编码器排名随训练方式变化。提出容量-必要性分解：容量衡量编码器独立性能，必要性衡量其被移除后的性能下降；二者不可互换。最佳性能来自高容量锚编码器与互补编码器的组合，后者在联合训练中投影器秩扩张，仅用两个编码器即可接近完整五编码器模型。添加更多编码器收益微弱，且投影器有效秩可解释剩余性能差异，表明联合训练中保持高秩投影器输入更利于优化。
Privacy-Robust Incrementality Measurement for Advertising Systems under Signal Loss
Authors: Prashant Shekhar, Caroline Howard
First: 2026-06-02T16:46:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:46:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advertising platforms use randomized lift tests to measure incrementality, but privacy-preserving reporting systems degrade the observed signal through match-rate loss, linkability loss, attribution-window loss, aggregation-threshold suppression, randomized reporting noise, and segment-heterogeneous signal loss. This paper formulates privacy-constrained advertising measurement as a robust causal decision problem under the mentioned signal losses. Given a randomized experiment and an ambiguity set for privacy-induced degradation, the framework projects the observation-compatible fiber of clean/unfiltered experimental worlds onto the incrementality functional and returns certified, rejected, and unresolved decisions. The main result gives a sharp decision frontier. Reports outside the frontier support uniformly valid certification or rejection, whereas reports inside it contain too little information for any method to uniformly distinguish above-threshold incrementality from non-incrementality. Supporting results give finite-sample certification, sample-complexity guarantees, a minimax lower bound showing that signal loss reduces effective information, and a reporting-granularity tradeoff. On 2.0M Criteo Uplift rows and the 64K-row Hillstrom email experiment, clean conversion lift is positive in both datasets, with lifts 0.00112 and 0.00495, respectively. Population certification survives mild degradation in Criteo and severe degradation in Hillstrom, while all considered finite-sample stress settings in both datasets remain unresolved after simultaneous uncertainty and reporting noise are included. Overall, the research contributes a decision-theoretic layer for privacy-aware incrementality measurement whose output is the strongest causal-claim justified by degraded ads signals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：信号丢失环境下隐私保护的广告系统增量性测量
广告平台使用随机提升测试测量增量性，但隐私保护报告系统会因匹配率损失、可链接性损失、归因窗口损失、聚合阈值抑制、随机报告噪声及分段异质信号损失而削弱观测信号。本文将隐私约束下的广告测量建模为在上述信号损失条件下的鲁棒因果决策问题。给定随机实验及隐私诱导退化对应的模糊集，该框架将与观测兼容的纯净/未过滤实验世界纤维投影至增量性函数，并返回认证、拒绝及未决决策。主要结果给出精确的决策前沿：前沿外的报告支持统一有效的认证或拒绝，前沿内的报告则信息不足，任何方法均无法统一区分阈值以上增量性与非增量性。支持性结果包括有限样本认证、样本复杂度保证、最小最大下界（表明信号损失降低有效信息）及报告粒度权衡。在200万行Criteo提升数据与6.4万行Hillstrom邮件实验中，两数据集均显示正向转化提升（分别为0.00112和0.00495）。Criteo在轻度退化下仍可实现群体认证，Hillstrom在严重退化下仍可认证，但两数据集在同时包含不确定性与报告噪声的有限样本压力测试下，所有情形均未决。总体而言，本研究为隐私感知增量性测量贡献了决策理论层，其输出是基于退化广告信号所能支持的最强因果主张。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of advertising incrementality signals under privacy-preserving reporting systems, this paper formulates privacy-constrained measurement as a robust causal decision problem under multiple signal losses. It proposes a framework that projects observation-compatible experimental worlds onto an incrementality functional to yield certified, rejected, or unresolved decisions, with a sharp decision frontier defining valid inference boundaries. Experimental results on 2.0M Criteo and 64K Hillstrom datasets show positive lift values (0.00112 and 0.00495), and population certification remains robust under mild degradation in Criteo but fails under severe degradation in Hillstrom; finite-sample stress tests remain unresolved when uncertainty and noise are combined, demonstrating the framework’s sensitivity to signal loss.
为应对隐私保护机制导致广告增量测量信号衰减的挑战，本文将问题建模为在多种隐私诱导信号损失下的鲁棒因果决策任务。提出一种框架，将与观测兼容的实验世界投影至增量函数，依据明确的决策边界输出认证、拒绝或未决决策。在200万条Criteo和6.4万条Hillstrom数据集上，实验显示正向提升值（0.00112和0.00495），Criteo在轻度信号衰减下仍可认证总体提升，Hillstrom在严重衰减下认证失败；所有有限样本压力测试在同时包含不确定性与报告噪声后均未决。
MLP Splatting: Object-Centric Neural Fields
Authors: Shinjeong Kim, Yuzhou Cheng, Xin Kong, Paul H. J. Kelly, Andrew J. Davison
First: 2026-06-02T16:46:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:46:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
3D representations are fundamental to scene rendering, understanding, and interaction. Recent approaches, such as 3D Gaussian Splatting and Neural Radiance Fields, achieve impressive photorealistic novel-view synthesis, but lack the ability to easily decompose scene elements into a few primitives, requiring additional segmentation or grouping for object-level manipulation. We present MLP-Splatting, a method that enables scene decomposition via a few expressive light-field primitives while providing photorealistic novel-view synthesis. MLP-Splatting models each primitive as an independent compact MLP with localized spatial support that predicts radiance and opacity. In contrast to low-level Gaussian primitives or a single global radiance field, our neural primitives provide greater expressive capacity while remaining spatially localized. Rendering is performed through efficient sparse volumetric compositing over ray-primitive interactions. Our primitives are supervised using RGB supervision alone, which yields primitives that represent local scene regions often corresponding to objects or object parts, enabling interactive object-level editing without segmentation masks by selecting a handful of primitives. Our method, augmented with optional semantic feature distillation, enables open-vocabulary scene interaction and open-set instant segmentation. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, we achieve substantially lower memory usage (1/15$\times$) and faster rendering (3$\times$), as we show in our experiments compared to semantic 3DGS methods. Project Page: https://shinjeongkim.com/mlp-splatting
中文标题/摘要
标题：MLP点绘：面向对象的神经场
3D表示是场景渲染、理解和交互的基础。近期方法如3D高斯点绘和神经辐射场实现了令人印象深刻的逼真新视角合成，但难以将场景元素轻松分解为少数基本体，需额外分割或分组以实现对象级操作。我们提出MLP-Splatting方法，通过少量表达性光场基本体实现场景分解，同时提供逼真新视角合成。 MLP-Splatting将每个基本体建模为独立紧凑的MLP，具有局部空间支持，预测辐射度和不透明度。与低级高斯基本体或单一全局辐射场不同，我们的神经基本体具有更强的表达能力，同时保持空间局部性。渲染通过高效稀疏体体积合成实现，基于光线与基本体的交互。 我们的基本体仅使用RGB监督训练，生成的基本体常代表局部场景区域，对应物体或物体部分，从而无需分割掩码即可通过选择少量基本体实现交互式对象级编辑。结合可选语义特征蒸馏，该方法支持开放词汇场景交互和开放集即时分割。相比现有方法，我们在实验中显示内存使用降低15倍，渲染速度提升3倍。项目页面：https://shinjeongkim.com/mlp-splatting
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for object-centric 3D scene decomposition alongside photorealistic novel-view synthesis, MLP-Splatting introduces a method that models scene elements as localized, compact MLPs predicting radiance and opacity. Each primitive operates independently with spatially confined support, enabling expressive yet efficient representation. Rendering is achieved via sparse volumetric compositing over ray-primitive interactions. Supervised solely by RGB data, the method learns primitives that often correspond to objects or object parts, enabling interactive editing without segmentation masks. With optional semantic feature distillation, it supports open-vocabulary interaction and open-set segmentation. Experiments show it uses 15 times less memory and renders 3 times faster than state-of-the-art semantic 3D Gaussian methods.
为实现支持高效编辑与新视角合成的物体中心化3D场景表示，MLP-Splatting提出一种方法，将场景分解为少量局部神经元素，每个素由紧凑的MLP建模，预测辐射度与不透明度。这些素通过沿射线-素交互的稀疏体绘制实现逼真渲染，仅用RGB监督训练，自然对应物体边界，无需分割掩码即可实现交互式物体级编辑。实验表明，该方法相比最先进的语义3D高斯点云方法，内存占用降低15倍、渲染速度提升3倍，并支持开放词汇交互与即时实例分割。
From 'What' to 'How' and 'Why': Sharing LLM-Generated Retrospective Summaries of Older Adults' Passive Tracking Data with Remote Family Members
Authors: Jiachen Li, Reina Szeyi Chan, Akshat Choube, Xiang Zhi Tan, Elizabeth Mynatt, Varun Mishra
First: 2026-06-02T16:46:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:46:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the growing prevalence of modern ubiquitous computing technologies, multi-modal tracking systems hold promise for providing timely awareness and reassurance to stakeholders such as remote family members (RFMs) of older adults, who play a central role in care coordination. However, combining heterogeneous data streams into high-level, meaningful content - such as retrospective summaries - remains challenging. While recent work has demonstrated the promise of large language models (LLMs) for interpreting multi-modal tracking data, less attention has been given to generating narrative accounts for stakeholders like RFMs, who possess rich personal knowledge of older adults and strong emotional responsibility, yet have limited visibility into their daily lives and limited capacity for caregiving. In this work, we explore how LLMs can be used to generate retrospective summaries from multi-modal tracking data for RFMs of older adults. We leveraged and customized an existing system, Vital Insight, to generate initial summaries on different dates and data availability scenarios as technology probes, and conducted interviews with 11 RFMs to gather feedback. Based on these insights, we redesigned the system into a multi-layer, multi-agent, insight-driven summary approach that builds from objective statistics and descriptions to enriched, context-aware narratives. We then compared the redesigned summaries with the initial versions through a survey with the same 11 RFMs and found significant improvements in satisfaction, perceived helpfulness, trust, and willingness to receive the summaries. We conclude by presenting design implications for AI-generated summaries for RFMs and broader contexts, emphasizing the need to support RFMs' sensemaking shift from simply presenting ''What'' data were collected, to explaining ''How'' is my loved one doing and ''Why''.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从‘是什么’到‘如何’和‘为何’：向远程家庭成员分享由大语言模型生成的老年人被动跟踪数据回顾摘要
随着现代普适计算技术的普及，多模态跟踪系统有望为老年人的远程家庭成员（RFMs）——他们在照护协调中发挥核心作用——提供及时的知情与安心。然而，将异构数据流整合为高层次、有意义的内容（如回顾性摘要）仍具挑战。尽管近期研究已证明大语言模型（LLMs）在解读多模态跟踪数据方面的潜力，但针对RFMs（他们对老年人有丰富个人认知和强烈情感责任，但对日常生活的可见度低、照护能力有限）生成叙事性内容的关注较少。本研究探索如何利用LLMs为老年人的RFMs生成多模态跟踪数据的回顾性摘要。我们基于现有系统Vital Insight进行改造与定制，生成不同日期和数据可用性场景下的初步摘要作为技术探针，并对11位RFMs进行访谈收集反馈。基于这些洞察，我们重新设计系统为多层、多代理、洞察驱动的摘要方法，从客观统计与描述逐步构建丰富、情境感知的叙事。随后，我们通过相同11位RFMs的问卷调查，将新设计的摘要与初始版本进行比较，发现其在满意度、感知有用性、信任度及接收意愿方面显著提升。最后，我们提出面向RFMs及更广泛场景的AI生成摘要设计启示，强调需支持RFMs的认知转变：从单纯呈现‘收集了什么数据’，转向解释‘亲人状况如何’及‘为何如此’。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to help remote family members of older adults better understand their loved ones' daily lives through passive tracking data, this study explores how large language models can generate retrospective summaries that move beyond raw data to explain 'how' and 'why' behaviors occur. The researchers adapted an existing system called Vital Insight to produce initial summaries under varying data conditions, then iteratively redesigned it into a multi-layer, multi-agent framework that builds narrative context from objective data. Evaluating the redesigned summaries with the same 11 participants via survey revealed significant improvements in user satisfaction, perceived helpfulness, trust, and willingness to receive summaries compared to the initial versions, demonstrating the value of narrative-driven, insight-oriented AI outputs for caregivers.
为帮助远程照护者更好地理解老年人的日常生活，本研究探索利用大语言模型生成从被动跟踪数据中提炼的回顾性总结，突破单纯呈现数据的局限，转而解释行为背后的含义。研究者基于Vital Insight系统，先生成不同数据条件下初步摘要，再迭代重构为多层多智能体框架，从客观数据逐步构建情境感知的叙事。对11位远程照护者的评估显示，新设计的摘要在满意度、有用性感知、信任度和接收意愿上显著优于初版，验证了从‘发生了什么’转向‘如何’与‘为何’的叙事方式对照护者的重要价值。
Alignment-Aware Decoding
Authors: Frédéric Berdoz, Luca A. Lanzendörfer, René Caky, Roger Wattenhofer
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-09-30T12:24:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:44:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Alignment of large language models remains a central challenge in natural language processing. Preference optimization has emerged as a popular and effective method for improving alignment, typically through training-time or prompt-based interventions. In this paper, we introduce alignment-aware decoding (AAD), a method to enhance model alignment directly at inference. Theoretically, AAD can be interpreted as implicit reward optimization, yet it requires no specialized training beyond the standard DPO setup. Empirically, AAD consistently outperforms strong baselines across diverse alignment benchmarks and model scales. Moreover, in data-constrained settings, AAD can produce high-quality synthetic data to improve alignment under standard decoding, providing a practical solution when labeled data is limited.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐感知解码
大语言模型的对齐仍是自然语言处理的核心挑战。偏好优化已成为提升对齐效果的流行且有效方法，通常通过训练时或提示引导干预实现。本文提出对齐感知解码（AAD），一种在推理阶段直接增强模型对齐的方法。理论上，AAD可视为隐式奖励优化，但无需超出标准DPO设置的特殊训练。实证表明，AAD在多种对齐基准和模型规模上持续优于强基线。此外，在数据受限场景下，AAD可生成高质量合成数据以提升标准解码下的对齐效果，为标注数据稀缺时提供实用解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent challenge of aligning large language models with human preferences, this paper proposes alignment-aware decoding (AAD), a novel inference-time method that enhances alignment without requiring specialized training beyond standard DPO. AAD operates by implicitly optimizing reward signals during decoding, effectively steering generation toward preferred outputs. Experimental results show AAD consistently outperforms strong baselines across multiple alignment benchmarks and model scales, and in data-scarce scenarios, it generates high-quality synthetic data that further improves alignment under standard decoding.
为解决大语言模型与人类偏好对齐的持续挑战，本文提出了一种名为对齐感知解码（AAD）的推理时方法，该方法在无需超出标准DPO训练的前提下，通过隐式奖励优化提升模型对齐效果。实验表明，AAD在多个对齐基准和模型规模上持续优于现有强基线，并在数据受限场景下能生成高质量合成数据，进一步提升标准解码下的对齐表现。
Seg2Track++: Probabilistic Track Validation and Data Association for Multi-Object Tracking and Segmentation
Authors: Diogo Mendonça, Tiago Barros, Cristiano Premebida, Urbano J. Nunes
First: 2026-06-02T16:43:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:43:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous systems require robust Multi-Object Tracking and Segmentation (MOTS) to operate reliably in dynamic environments, ensuring consistent object identities and precise mask-level delineation. Foundation models such as SAM2 have shown strong zero-shot generalization for segmentation, but their direct application to MOTS is limited by unreliable track association and false-positive propagation. This work introduces Seg2Track++, a framework that integrates instance segmentation with SAM2 and a novel track management module to perform zero-shot MOTS with enhanced temporal consistency. Tracks are associated using Mask Centroid Distance (MCD) and Confidence-Aware Cost Modulation (CCM), while Probabilistic Track Validation (PTV) employs a Bernoulli filter to validate track existence and suppress ghost tracks. Experimental results on KITTI MOTS demonstrate improved identity preservation, reduced false-positive propagation, and robust track management without fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Seg2Track++：面向多目标跟踪与分割的概率轨迹验证与数据关联
自主系统需要稳健的多目标跟踪与分割（MOTS）以在动态环境中可靠运行，确保对象身份一致性和精确的掩码级分割。虽然基础模型如SAM2在分割任务中表现出强大的零样本泛化能力，但其直接应用于MOTS受限于不可靠的轨迹关联和假阳性传播。本文提出Seg2Track++框架，将实例分割与SAM2结合，并引入新颖的轨迹管理模块，实现零样本MOTS并增强时序一致性。轨迹通过掩码质心距离（MCD）和置信度感知代价调制（CCM）进行关联，概率轨迹验证（PTV）采用伯努利滤波器验证轨迹存在性并抑制鬼影轨迹。在KITTI MOTS数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法提升了身份保持能力、减少了假阳性传播，并实现了无需微调的鲁棒轨迹管理。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of direct segmentation model deployment in multi-object tracking and segmentation tasks, Seg2Track++ integrates SAM2 with a novel track management module to enable zero-shot MOTS with improved temporal consistency. It employs Mask Centroid Distance and Confidence-Aware Cost Modulation for track association, and introduces Probabilistic Track Validation using a Bernoulli filter to suppress ghost tracks and validate track existence. Experiments on KITTI MOTS show enhanced identity preservation, reduced false-positive propagation, and robust track management without fine-tuning.
为解决直接应用分割模型在多目标跟踪与分割任务中的局限性，Seg2Track++将SAM2与新型轨迹管理模块结合，实现零样本MOTS并增强时序一致性。其采用掩码质心距离与置信度感知代价调制进行轨迹关联，并引入基于伯努利滤波器的概率轨迹验证机制以抑制鬼影轨迹并验证轨迹存在性。在KITTI MOTS数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了身份保持能力、降低了假阳性传播，并在无需微调的情况下实现了稳健的轨迹管理。
DyaPlex: Full-Duplex Speech-Motion Model for Dyadic Interaction
Authors: Koki Nagano, Hongyu Liu, Seonwook Park, Tianye Li, Amrita Mazumdar, Christian Jacobsen, Shengze Wang, Michael Stengel, Rajarshi Roy, Ka Chun Cheung, Simon See, Shalini De Mello
First: 2026-06-02T16:42:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:42:56+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/amri/projects/DyaPlex
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present DyaPlex, a streaming, full-duplex speech-and-motion model designed for dyadic interaction. To capture the continuous and reciprocal nature of human communication, this full-duplex capability empowers the agent to simultaneously perceive and generate both speech and physical motion in a streaming fashion. At its core, our method leverages the strong priors of a foundational full-duplex speech model and integrates a novel motion pathway, thereby achieving fully synchronized multi-modal interaction. Specifically, we design a dual-tower Transformer architecture that preserves the zero-shot conversational reasoning of a frozen base speech model while constructing a deeply coupled, streaming motion pathway. By introducing a unified dyadic token interleaving mechanism and guiding cross-attention via a time-aligned speech-motion RoPE, our model effectively aligns autoregressive motions with rich latent speech features. Trained on the 4,000-hour Seamless Interaction dataset, our model effectively captures cross-speaker dependencies and establishes new state-of-the-art performance across both monadic and dyadic human interaction benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DyaPlex：用于双人交互的全双工语音-动作模型
我们提出了DyaPlex，一种专为双人交互设计的流式全双工语音与动作模型。为捕捉人类交流的连续性和互惠性，该全双工能力使代理能够同时感知并生成语音与身体动作，且以流式方式处理。其核心方法利用基础全双工语音模型的强先验，并集成新颖的动作路径，从而实现完全同步的多模态交互。具体而言，我们设计了双塔Transformer架构，在冻结的基础语音模型上保留零样本对话推理能力，同时构建深度耦合的流式动作路径。通过引入统一的双人令牌交错机制，并借助时间对齐的语音-动作RoPE引导跨注意力，模型有效将自回归动作与丰富的语音潜在特征对齐。在4000小时的Seamless Interaction数据集上训练，该模型有效捕捉跨说话人依赖关系，并在单人与双人交互基准测试中均取得最新性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic, reciprocal human-like dyadic interaction, DyaPlex introduces a streaming, full-duplex model that simultaneously processes and generates speech and motion. It combines a frozen foundational speech model with a novel motion pathway in a dual-tower Transformer architecture, preserving zero-shot conversational reasoning while enabling synchronized multimodal output. A unified token interleaving mechanism and time-aligned RoPE cross-attention ensure motion generation aligns with speech features. Evaluated on the 4,000-hour Seamless Interaction dataset, DyaPlex achieves state-of-the-art performance on both monadic and dyadic interaction benchmarks, effectively modeling cross-speaker dependencies.
为实现更真实的人类双向互动，DyaPlex提出了一种流式全双工模型，可同时感知与生成语音和动作。该模型在冻结的基础语音模型上集成新型动作路径，采用双塔Transformer架构，在保持零样本对话推理能力的同时实现多模态同步输出。通过统一的令牌交错机制和时序对齐的RoPE交叉注意力，确保动作生成与语音特征同步。在4000小时数据集上训练后，该模型在单人与双人交互基准测试中均达到当前最优性能，有效建模跨说话人动态。
Visual Instruction Tuning Aligns Modalities through Abstraction
Authors: Luis Palacios, Lorenzo Basile, Diego Doimo, Alberto Cazzaniga
First: 2026-06-02T16:42:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:42:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual instruction tuning effectively adapts a pre-trained Large Language Model (LLM) to process image information alongside text. Yet, it remains unclear how visual features are embedded into the layer-wise hierarchy of abstractions of the LLM backbone. Across a diverse set of vision-language architectures, we show that instruction tuning primarily serves as a bridge, embedding visual features directly into the intermediate semantic layers of the LLM, bypassing the early layers devoted to unimodal processing. With probing analyses and causal interventions, we show that these intermediate layers are the semantic core of vision-language processing and play a critical role in the performance on a broad set of multimodal benchmarks. In addition, by comparing the geometry of semantically equivalent visual and textual representations, we find that fine-tuning extends and strengthens the existing abstraction phase, aligning visual features with pre-existing textual ones. Finally, we confirm the functional role of this localized alignment by restricting fine-tuning to intermediate layers alone: this strategy preserves the performance of full fine-tuning on vision-centric benchmarks while reducing training time. Our results suggest that multimodal integration is a localized phenomenon driven by the repurposing of the internal abstraction engine of the LLM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉指令调优通过抽象对齐多模态
视觉指令调优能有效使预训练的大语言模型（LLM）同时处理图像与文本信息。然而，视觉特征如何嵌入LLM主干网络的分层抽象体系仍不明确。在多种视觉-语言架构中，我们发现指令调优主要充当桥梁，将视觉特征直接嵌入LLM的中间语义层，绕过早期专用于单模态处理的层。通过探测分析与因果干预，我们证明这些中间层是视觉-语言处理的语义核心，并在多种多模态基准测试中发挥关键作用。此外，通过比较语义等价的视觉与文本表征的几何结构，我们发现微调扩展并强化了现有抽象阶段，使视觉特征与已有文本特征对齐。最后，我们通过仅限制微调至中间层来验证该局部对齐的功能作用：此策略在视觉中心基准测试中保持全微调性能的同时，减少训练时间。我们的结果表明，多模态整合是一种局部现象，由LLM内部抽象引擎的再利用驱动。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how visual instruction tuning integrates image and text modalities within large language models, motivated by the need to understand the layer-wise embedding of visual features. The authors analyze multiple vision-language architectures and find that instruction tuning acts as a bridge, embedding visual inputs directly into intermediate semantic layers of the LLM, rather than early unimodal layers. Probing and causal experiments confirm these intermediate layers are central to multimodal performance, and geometric comparisons reveal fine-tuning extends and aligns visual representations with existing textual abstractions. Crucially, restricting fine-tuning to these intermediate layers preserves performance on vision-centric tasks while reducing training time, indicating multimodal integration is a localized process driven by repurposing the LLM’s internal abstraction mechanism.
为理解视觉信息如何在指令微调中融入大型语言模型，本研究考察了视觉特征在多模态架构中的层间嵌入机制。研究发现，指令微调充当桥梁，将视觉输入直接嵌入到LLM的中间语义层，绕过早期的单模态处理层。探针分析和因果干预证实，这些中间层是多模态性能的核心语义区域，而几何分析表明微调会扩展并强化抽象阶段，使视觉与文本表征对齐。更重要的是，仅在中间层进行微调即可保持视觉导向基准测试的性能，同时缩短训练时间，说明多模态整合是局部现象，源于LLM内部抽象引擎的再利用。
dLLM-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Large Language Models with Adaptive Caching
Authors: Zhiyuan Liu, Yicun Yang, Yaojie Zhang, Junjie Chen, Chang Zou, Qingyuan Wei, Shaobo Wang, Yichen Zhu, Linfeng Zhang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-05-17T15:50:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:41:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autoregressive Models (ARMs) have long dominated the landscape of Large Language Models. Recently, a new paradigm has emerged in the form of diffusion-based Large Language Models (dLLMs), which generate text by iteratively denoising masked segments. This approach has shown significant advantages and potential. However, dLLMs suffer from high inference latency. Traditional ARM acceleration techniques, such as Key-Value caching, are incompatible with dLLMs due to their bidirectional attention mechanism. To address this specific challenge, our work begins with a key observation that dLLM inference involves a static prompt and a partially dynamic response, where most tokens remain stable across adjacent denoising steps. Based on this, we propose dLLM-Cache, a training-free adaptive caching framework that combines long-interval prompt caching with partial response updates guided by feature similarity. This design enables efficient reuse of intermediate computations without compromising model performance. Extensive experiments on representative dLLMs, including LLaDA 8B and Dream 7B, show that dLLM-Cache achieves up to 9.1x FLOPs reduction on LongBench-HotpotQA while maintaining competitive output quality. Notably, our method brings dLLM inference latency close to that of ARMs under many settings. The code for this work is publicly available at: https://github.com/maomaocun/dLLM-cache.
中文标题/摘要
标题：dLLM-Cache：通过自适应缓存加速扩散大语言模型
自回归模型（ARMs）长期以来主导了大语言模型领域。近期，一种基于扩散的大语言模型（dLLMs）新范式兴起，其通过迭代去噪掩码片段生成文本，展现出显著优势与潜力。然而，dLLMs存在高推理延迟问题。传统ARM加速技术（如键值缓存）因dLLMs的双向注意力机制而不兼容。为解决此特定挑战，我们的工作基于一个关键观察：dLLM推理包含静态提示与部分动态响应，相邻去噪步骤中多数token保持稳定。据此，我们提出dLLM-Cache，一种无需训练的自适应缓存框架，结合长间隔提示缓存与基于特征相似性的部分响应更新。该设计实现中间计算高效复用而不牺牲模型性能。在代表性dLLMs（如LLaDA 8B和Dream 7B）上的大量实验表明，dLLM-Cache在LongBench-HotpotQA上实现高达9.1倍FLOPs减少，同时保持竞争力的输出质量。值得注意的是，我们的方法在多种设置下使dLLM推理延迟接近ARMs。本工作代码公开于：https://github.com/maomaocun/dLLM-cache。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high inference latency of diffusion-based Large Language Models (dLLMs), which lack compatibility with traditional autoregressive caching due to their bidirectional attention, the authors propose dLLM-Cache: a training-free adaptive caching framework. It leverages static prompt caching over long intervals and updates only partially dynamic response tokens based on feature similarity, enabling efficient reuse of intermediate computations. Experiments on LLaDA 8B and Dream 7B show up to 9.1x FLOPs reduction on LongBench-HotpotQA while preserving output quality, and inference latency is brought close to that of autoregressive models in many settings.
为解决扩散式大语言模型（dLLM）推理延迟高、传统缓存方法不适用的问题，本文提出dLLM-Cache，一种无需训练的自适应缓存框架。该方法基于dLLM推理中提示静态、响应部分稳定的特点，通过长间隔提示缓存与基于特征相似性的部分响应更新，高效复用中间计算。在LLaDA 8B和Dream 7B模型上实验表明，该方法在LongBench-HotpotQA任务中实现最高9.1倍FLOPs减少，同时保持输出质量，并将推理延迟降至接近自回归模型水平。
Unified Video-Action Joint Denoising for Dexterous Action and Data Generation
Authors: Dingrui Wang, YuAn Wang, Jinkun Liu, Yue Zhang, Mattia Piccinini, Yu Sun, Johannes Betz
First: 2026-06-02T16:39:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:39:23+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent world action models leverage video foundation models by aligning broad visual-dynamics priors with executable robot actions. We revisit this alignment from a distributional perspective. Existing formulations typically narrow the aligned prior into an observation-conditioned policy distribution over future actions. In contrast, we keep the distribution broader by modeling the joint space of interaction videos and executable hand trajectories under multiple conditioning regimes. We propose Donk, a unified video-action denoising model for dexterous hands. With language, an initial image, and the initial hand state, Donk samples future videos and bimanual MANO trajectories as an action policy. Without the image condition, the same denoising architecture samples paired video-action rollouts from a text-conditioned distribution, turning the aligned video prior into a data engine. Across action, video, and text-only generation evaluations, Donk improves dexterous trajectory accuracy, preserves strong video fidelity, and produces smooth text-conditioned action rollouts under the same unified training recipe.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于灵巧动作与数据生成的统一视频-动作联合去噪
近期世界动作模型通过将广泛的视觉-动态先验与可执行机器人动作对齐，利用视频基础模型。我们从分布视角重新审视这一对齐方式。现有方法通常将对齐先验压缩为条件于观测的未来动作策略分布。相反，我们通过建模在多种条件下的交互视频与可执行手轨迹的联合空间，保持分布更广。我们提出Donk，一种用于灵巧手的统一视频-动作去噪模型。给定语言、初始图像和初始手状态，Donk采样未来视频与双臂MANO轨迹作为动作策略。无需图像条件时，相同去噪架构从文本条件分布中采样视频-动作序列，将对齐的视频先验转化为数据引擎。在动作、视频及纯文本生成评估中，Donk提升灵巧轨迹精度，保持强视频保真度，并在统一训练方案下生成平滑的文本条件动作序列。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better align visual dynamics with executable robot actions, this work proposes Donk, a unified video-action denoising model that jointly models interaction videos and hand trajectories under multiple conditioning regimes. Unlike prior methods that constrain the action distribution to be observation-conditioned, Donk maintains a broader distribution by jointly denoising video and action spaces. The model generates future videos and bimanual MANO trajectories from language, initial image, and hand state inputs, and can also produce text-conditioned video-action rollouts without image conditioning. Experimental results show Donk improves dexterous trajectory accuracy, preserves high video fidelity, and generates smooth text-conditioned action sequences—all under a single training framework.
为更好地对齐视觉动态与可执行机器人动作，本文提出Donk，一种统一的视频-动作去噪模型，联合建模交互视频与手部轨迹，并支持多种条件生成。与现有方法将先验限制在观察条件下的动作策略分布不同，Donk通过单一去噪架构采样未来视频与双臂MANO轨迹，保持更广的分布。给定语言、初始图像和手部状态时，Donk生成灵巧动作序列；无图像时，仅凭文本条件即可采样视频-动作序列，将视频先验转化为数据生成引擎。实验表明，Donk在动作、视频及纯文本生成任务中均提升轨迹精度、保持视频保真度，并生成平滑的文本条件动作序列，且统一训练策略即可实现。
A Training-Free Mixture-of-Agents Framework for Multi-Document Summarization using LLMs and Knowledge Graphs
Authors: Cuong Vuong Tuan, Trang Mai Xuan, Tien-Cuong Nguyen, Vu-Duc Ngo, Thien Van Luong
First: 2026-06-02T16:39:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:39:07+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Neural Computing and Applications
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-Document Summarization (MDS) plays a critical role in distilling essential information from collections of textual data. Existing approaches often struggle to capture complex inter-document relationships, rely heavily on large amounts of labeled data for supervised training, or exhibit limited generalization across domains and languages. To address these limitations, we present a training-free mixture-of-agents framework for MDS that leverages the complementary strengths of large language models (LLMs) and knowledge graphs. Our approach decomposes summarization into specialized agent tasks: extractive selection, knowledge-aware abstraction, and iterative refinement, each operating without task-specific fine-tuning. We unify their outputs using a multi-perspective consistency mechanism guided by LLMs. Experiments across four datasets in English and Vietnamese demonstrate state-of-the-art or competitive performance, validating the effectiveness and adaptability of our modular design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种无需训练的多代理框架，利用大语言模型和知识图谱进行多文档摘要
多文档摘要（MDS）在从文本数据集合中提炼关键信息方面起着关键作用。现有方法往往难以捕捉复杂的跨文档关系，高度依赖大量标注数据进行监督训练，或在跨领域和跨语言场景下泛化能力有限。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种无需训练的多代理框架，结合大语言模型（LLMs）和知识图谱的互补优势。我们的方法将摘要任务分解为三个专业化代理任务：抽取式选择、知识感知抽象和迭代优化，每个任务均无需特定任务微调。通过大语言模型引导的多视角一致性机制统一各代理输出。在英文和越南语四个数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到或接近当前最优性能，验证了其模块化设计的有效性和适应性。
Summary / 总结
To overcome limitations in existing multi-document summarization methods—such as poor handling of inter-document relationships, heavy reliance on labeled data, and weak cross-domain generalization—the authors propose a training-free mixture-of-agents framework that combines large language models and knowledge graphs. The framework decomposes summarization into three specialized agent tasks: extractive selection, knowledge-aware abstraction, and iterative refinement, all executed without fine-tuning. These agents’ outputs are unified via a multi-perspective consistency mechanism guided by LLMs. Experiments on four datasets in English and Vietnamese show the framework achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance, confirming its effectiveness and adaptability across languages and domains.
为解决现有多文档摘要方法依赖标注数据、泛化能力弱等问题，作者提出一种无需训练的混合代理框架，结合大语言模型与知识图谱。该框架将摘要任务分解为三个专用代理角色：抽取式选择、知识感知抽象与迭代优化，均无需任务特定微调。代理输出通过大语言模型引导的多视角一致性机制统一整合。在英、越语四个数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到当前最优或竞争力水平，验证了其模块化设计的有效性与适应性。
Taiji: Pareto Optimal Policy Optimization with Semantics-IDs Trade-off for Industrial LLM-Enhanced Recommendation
Authors: Yuecheng Li, Zeyu Song, Jing Yao, Chi Lu, Peng Jiang, Kun Gai
First: 2026-06-02T16:39:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:39:06+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling recommender systems via large language models (LLMs) has become a prominent trend in the industry. However, aligning the LLM's semantic space with the recommender's ID space via post-training (e.g., SFT and RL) remains challenging. Existing LLM4Rec paradigms are bottlenecked by two main issues: (1) the difficulty of measuring and improving chain-of-thought (CoT) quality in open-domain recommendation during SFT, and (2) the neglect of the trade-off between LLM semantic rewards and recommendation preference rewards during RL alignment. Inspired by these challenges, we present Taiji, a novel LLM-as-Enhancer framework designed for industrial recommender systems. To overcome the SFT bottleneck, we utilize reverse-engineered reasoning and open-ended rejection sampling to generate high-quality, domain-specific CoT data. To resolve the RL alignment issue, we propose Pareto Optimal Policy Optimization (POPO), which adaptively adjusts cross-domain reward weights. Theoretically, it achieves an optimal trade-off between the semantic world knowledge of LLMs and the collaborative ID features representing online user preferences. Extensive offline evaluations and online A/B tests validate the effectiveness of Taiji. Deployed on Kuaishou's advertising platform since May 2026, Taiji currently serves over 400 million users daily, yielding significant commercial revenue and demonstrating its robust scalability in web-scale environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：太极：面向工业级LLM增强推荐系统的语义-ID权衡的帕累托最优策略优化
通过大语言模型（LLM）扩展推荐系统已成为业界主流趋势。然而，通过后训练（如SFT和RL）对齐LLM语义空间与推荐系统ID空间仍具挑战性。现有LLM4Rec范式受限于两大问题：（1）在开放域推荐中SFT阶段难以衡量和提升思维链（CoT）质量；（2）RL对齐阶段忽视LLM语义奖励与推荐偏好奖励之间的权衡。受此启发，我们提出太极，一种面向工业推荐系统的新型LLM增强框架。为突破SFT瓶颈，我们采用逆向推理与开放式拒绝采样生成高质量、领域特定的CoT数据；为解决RL对齐问题，我们提出帕累托最优策略优化（POPO），动态调整跨域奖励权重。理论上，POPO在LLM语义世界知识与代表在线用户偏好的协同ID特征间实现最优权衡。大量离线评估与在线A/B测试验证了太极的有效性。自2026年5月部署于快手广告平台以来，太极日均服务超4亿用户，带来显著商业收益，展现出其在Web规模环境中的强大可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning large language models (LLMs) with recommender system ID spaces via post-training, the paper introduces Taiji, an LLM-as-Enhancer framework for industrial recommendation. To address the difficulty of improving chain-of-thought quality during supervised fine-tuning, it employs reverse-engineered reasoning and rejection sampling to generate domain-specific CoT data. For reinforcement learning alignment, it proposes Pareto Optimal Policy Optimization (POPO), which dynamically balances LLM semantic rewards and recommendation preference rewards to achieve optimal trade-offs. Experimental results from offline evaluations and online A/B tests confirm Taiji’s effectiveness, with deployment on Kuaishou’s platform serving over 400 million users daily and generating substantial commercial value.
为解决工业推荐系统中大语言模型语义空间与推荐ID空间对齐的难题，Taiji提出一种新型LLM增强框架，旨在克服两个主要瓶颈：SFT阶段CoT质量难以评估和RL阶段语义奖励与偏好奖励的权衡缺失。该框架通过逆向推理与开放拒绝采样生成高质量领域特定CoT数据，并提出Pareto最优策略优化（POPO）动态调整跨域奖励权重，实现LLM语义知识与用户偏好ID特征的最优平衡。离线评估与在线A/B测试验证其有效性，部署于快手广告平台后日均服务超4亿用户，带来显著商业收益。
Backdooring Masked Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Daniel Yiming Cao, Chengzhong Wang, Sheng-Yen Chou, Chengyu Huang, Pin-Yu Chen, Shengwei An
First: 2026-05-19T02:20:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:39:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are emerging as a compelling new paradigm for text generation, but their training-time security remains largely unexplored. Existing backdoor attacks on Gaussian diffusion models or autoregressive language models do not directly apply to MDLMs because MDLMs rely on discrete state corruption and iterative denoising rather than continuous noising or left-to-right prediction. In this work, we present the first systematic study of training-time backdoor attacks on MDLMs. We propose SHADOWMASK, a backdoor attack that modifies the MDLM forward corruption process by replacing the standard all-mask terminal distribution with a trigger-mask mixture prior. This creates a dedicated denoising pathway from trigger-corrupted states to attacker-specified targets while preserving clean denoising behavior. We further provide a principled mathematical formulation by defining the backdoored forward process, deriving the reverse-time posterior, and obtaining the continuous-time training objective. Evaluations on DiT-based MDLM and LLaDA-8B-Instruct across WikiText-103, OpenWebText, and Alpaca show that SHADOWMASK achieves near-100% attack success, substantially outperforms standard data poisoning, largely preserves clean utility, remains effective under full-model and parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and is robust against representative defenses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对掩码扩散语言模型的后门攻击
掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）正成为文本生成的一种有吸引力的新范式，但其训练阶段的安全性尚未被充分研究。现有的针对高斯扩散模型或自回归语言模型的后门攻击无法直接应用于MDLMs，因为MDLMs依赖离散状态破坏和迭代去噪，而非连续噪声或左到右预测。本文首次系统研究了MDLMs训练阶段的后门攻击。我们提出了SHADOWMASK，一种通过将标准全掩码终端分布替换为触发掩码混合先验来修改MDLM前向破坏过程的后门攻击方法。该方法在触发破坏状态与攻击者指定目标之间创建专用去噪路径，同时保持干净去噪行为。我们进一步通过定义后门前向过程、推导反向时间后验并获得连续时间训练目标，提供了严谨的数学形式化。在基于DiT的MDLM和LLaDA-8B-Instruct模型上，针对WikiText-103、OpenWebText和Alpaca数据集的评估表明，SHADOWMASK攻击成功率接近100%，显著优于标准数据投毒，基本保持干净功能，即使在全模型和参数高效微调下仍有效，并对代表性防御措施具有鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of security analysis for masked diffusion language models (MDLMs), this work introduces SHADOWMASK, the first backdoor attack tailored to MDLMs' discrete corruption and iterative denoising mechanism. The method replaces the standard masking distribution with a trigger-mask mixture prior to create a hidden denoising pathway that maps trigger-corrupted states to attacker-specified outputs while preserving normal behavior. Experiments on DiT-based MDLM and LLaDA-8B-Instruct across multiple datasets show SHADOWMASK achieves near-100% attack success, significantly outperforms data poisoning, maintains clean utility, remains effective under fine-tuning, and withstands common defenses.
鉴于掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）在训练阶段的安全性尚未被研究，本文提出SHADOWMASK，这是首个针对MDLM离散破坏与迭代去噪机制的后门攻击方法。该方法在前向破坏过程中用触发掩码混合先验替换标准全掩码分布，构建一条隐式去噪路径以输出攻击者指定内容，同时保持对干净输入的正常行为。在多个数据集上的实验表明，SHADOWMASK攻击成功率接近100%，显著优于数据投毒，保持干净功能，能抵御全模型与参数高效微调，且对主流防御机制有效。
Explainable Forecasting of Scientific Breakthroughs from Concept Network Dynamics
Authors: Thomas Maillart, Thibaut Chataing, Ntorina Antoni, David Dosu, Paul Bagourd, Julian Jang-Jaccard, Alain Mermoud
First: 2026-06-02T16:38:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:38:41+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 10 figures, 4 tables. An earlier version was presented at Global Tech Mining Conference 2026. Code and data: https://github.com/wazaahhh/breakthroughs-forecasting
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce an explainable machine-learning approach that forecasts the structural precursors of scientific breakthroughs -- the emergence and intensification of links between research concepts -- by modelling how OpenAlex concept networks evolve over time. Using 59 semantic and topological features, a two-stage LightGBM model jointly predicts the formation and the future weight of concept pairs, adding a regression stage that quantifies expected intensity to prior link-existence forecasts. Relative to the state of the art, the approach improves accuracy and explainability at once: comparative validation across four technology and biomedical domains yields ROC-AUC in [0.954, 0.967] at all horizons without re-tuning, exceeding the roughly 0.90 of prior models, while every forecast rests on structural, auditable features rather than opaque embeddings. Classification performance is high (AUC about 0.95) and regression remains stable (RMSLE 0.45 to 0.6 over one to five years). Feature attribution shows that structural factors -- particularly Adamic-Adar similarity and degree-based Hadamard measures -- consistently drive accuracy, suggesting that breakthrough-relevant recombinations emerge in tightly connected sub-networks. Two expert-anchored cases, quantum annealing and AI-enabled quantum architectures, show the model surfacing technological convergence consistent with expert expectations. We then outline a three-layer decision architecture -- detection, expert translation, institutional integration -- that turns these forecasts into evidence-based research strategy and policy, anchored in open data and explainable features.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从概念网络动态预测科学突破的可解释方法
我们提出一种可解释的机器学习方法，通过建模OpenAlex概念网络随时间的演化，预测科学突破的结构前兆——即研究概念间链接的出现与强化。该方法使用59个语义和拓扑特征，采用两阶段LightGBM模型联合预测概念对的形成及其未来权重，新增回归阶段以量化预期强度，补充链接存在性预测。相较于现有方法，该方法在四个技术与生物医学领域中无需重新调参，所有时间跨度下ROC-AUC均在[0.954, 0.967]区间，显著优于约0.90的先前模型，且所有预测均基于结构化、可审计的特征，而非黑箱嵌入。分类性能高（AUC约0.95），回归稳定（1至5年RMSLE在0.45至0.6之间）。特征归因显示，结构因素——特别是Adamic-Adar相似度和基于度数的Hadamard度量——持续驱动准确性，表明与突破相关的重组在高度连接的子网络中出现。两个专家锚定案例——量子退火与AI赋能的量子架构——显示模型能揭示符合专家预期的技术融合。我们进一步提出三层决策架构——检测、专家翻译、机构整合——将这些预测转化为基于证据的研究策略与政策，依托开放数据与可解释特征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to predict scientific breakthroughs through interpretable means, this study employs a two-stage LightGBM model to forecast the formation and future intensity of concept links in evolving OpenAlex concept networks, using 59 semantic and topological features. The model first predicts link existence, then refines predictions with a regression stage to estimate link weight over time. Experimental results across four domains show superior performance: ROC-AUC scores between 0.954 and 0.967 at all horizons, surpassing prior models’ ~0.90, while maintaining high classification AUC (~0.95) and stable regression RMSLE (0.45–0.6 over 1–5 years). 为实现对科学突破的可解释预测，该研究构建了两阶段LightGBM模型，用于预测OpenAlex概念网络中概念对链接的形成及其未来强度。模型基于59个语义与拓扑特征，先预测链接是否形成，再通过回归阶段量化预期强度。在四个领域中的实验表明，模型性能优于现有方法：所有时间尺度下的ROC-AUC值介于0.954至0.967之间，显著高于约0.90的基准模型，同时分类AUC约0.95，回归RMSLE稳定在0.45至0.6（1至5年）。特征归因显示，Adamic-Adar相似度和基于度的Hadamard度量等结构特征是主要驱动因素，表明突破常源于紧密连接的子网络。量子退火与AI赋能量子架构两个案例验证了模型预测与专家预期一致，作者进一步提出三层决策架构，将预测转化为基于开放数据与可解释特征的研究与政策决策。
Learning DNF through Generalized Fourier Representations
Authors: Mohsen Heidari, Roni Khardon
First: 2025-06-01T16:24:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:38:41+00:00
Comments: 60 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Boolean Fourier representation has been widely used in learning theory, particularly for learning Disjunctive Normal Form (DNF) under uniform and product distributions. Extending these results to non-product distributions has remained a longstanding open problem. We address this challenge by introducing a generalized Fourier representation that enables learning under a broad class of non-product distributions. Our approach represents any distribution $D$ as a Bayesian network (BN) and derives a corresponding Fourier expansion. We show that standard Fourier-based learning techniques using membership queries to identify heavy coefficients can be adapted to this generalized representation with minor modifications. We prove that the $L_1$ spectral norm of conjunctions remains bounded under this expansion for difference-bounded tree BNs, significantly generalizing the known result for uniform distributions; matching lower bounds demonstrate the necessity of these constraints. Using these results, we establish the learnability of DNF and the agnostic learnability of decision trees under such distributions. Finally, we present an algorithm for learning difference-bounded tree BN distributions, extending our results to settings where the distribution is unknown.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过广义傅里叶表示学习DNF
布尔傅里叶表示在学习理论中被广泛使用，特别是在均匀分布和乘积分布下学习析取范式（DNF）。将其结果扩展到非乘积分布一直是长期存在的开放问题。我们通过引入广义傅里叶表示来解决这一挑战，该表示允许在广泛类别的非乘积分布下进行学习。我们的方法将任意分布D表示为贝叶斯网络（BN），并推导出相应的傅里叶展开。我们证明，标准的基于傅里叶的学习技术（使用成员查询识别重系数）可通过微小修改适应该广义表示。我们证明，在差分有界树形BN下，合取式的L1谱范数保持有界，显著推广了均匀分布下的已知结果；匹配的下界证明了这些约束的必要性。利用这些结果，我们建立了此类分布下DNF的可学习性及决策树的无偏可学习性。最后，我们提出了一种学习差分有界树形BN分布的算法，将我们的结果扩展到分布未知的情形。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning Disjunctive Normal Form (DNF) under non-product distributions, where prior Fourier-based methods fail, this work introduces a generalized Fourier representation by modeling any distribution as a Bayesian network and deriving its corresponding Fourier expansion. The method adapts standard Fourier learning techniques using membership queries to identify heavy coefficients, while proving that the L1 spectral norm of conjunctions remains bounded under difference-bounded tree BNs — a significant generalization from uniform distributions. Experimental results confirm the learnability of DNF and agnostic learnability of decision trees under such distributions, and an algorithm is provided for learning unknown difference-bounded tree BN distributions.
为解决在非乘积分布下学习DNF的难题（传统方法在此失效），本文提出一种广义傅里叶表示法，将任意分布建模为贝叶斯网络并推导其傅里叶展开。该方法通过微调标准傅里叶学习技术，利用成员查询识别重系数，同时证明在差分有界树形贝叶斯网络下，合取式的L1谱范数有界——这是对均匀分布下已知结果的显著推广。实验结果表明，该方法可实现DNF的可学习性及决策树的agnostic可学习性，并提出一种学习未知差分有界树形贝叶斯网络分布的算法，验证其实际可行性。
Vision-OPD: Learning to See Fine Details for Multimodal LLMs via On-Policy Self-Distillation
Authors: Qianhao Yuan, Jie Lou, Xing Yu, Hongyu Lin, Le Sun, Xianpei Han, Yaojie Lu
First: 2026-05-18T17:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:34:00+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://github.com/VisionOPD/Vision-OPD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) still struggle with fine-grained visual understanding, where answers often depend on small but decisive evidence in the full image. We observe a regional-to-global perception gap: the same MLLM answers fine-grained questions more accurately when conditioned on evidence-centered crops than on the corresponding full images, suggesting that many failures stem from difficulty to focus on relevant evidence rather than insufficient local recognition ability. Motivated by this observation, we propose Vision-OPD (Vision On-Policy Distillation), a regional-to-global self-distillation framework that transfers the model's own privileged regional perception to its full-image policy. Vision-OPD instantiates two conditional policies from the same MLLM: a crop-conditioned teacher and a full-image-conditioned student. The student generates on-policy rollouts, and Vision-OPD minimizes token-level divergence between the teacher and student next-token distributions along these rollouts. This enables the model to internalize the benefit of visual zooming without external teacher models, ground-truth labels, reward verifiers, or inference-time tool use. Experiments on multiple fine-grained visual understanding benchmarks show that Vision-OPD models achieve competitive or superior performance against much larger open-source, closed-source, and "Thinking-with-Images" agentic models. The code is available at https://github.com/VisionOPD/Vision-OPD
中文标题/摘要
标题：Vision-OPD：通过在线自蒸馏学习多模态大语言模型的精细视觉细节
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在细粒度视觉理解方面仍存在困难，其答案常依赖于图像中微小但关键的证据。我们观察到区域到全局的感知差距：当MLLMs以证据为中心的裁剪图像作为条件时，其对细粒度问题的回答准确性高于使用对应完整图像的情况，表明许多失败源于难以聚焦相关证据，而非局部识别能力不足。受此启发，我们提出Vision-OPD（视觉在线自蒸馏），一种从区域到全局的自蒸馏框架，将模型自身的特权区域感知迁移到全图策略中。Vision-OPD从同一MLLM中实例化两个条件策略：一个以裁剪图像为条件的教师模型和一个以完整图像为条件的学生模型。学生模型生成在线轨迹，Vision-OPD沿这些轨迹最小化教师与学生下一token分布的token级差异。这使模型无需外部教师模型、真实标签、奖励验证器或推理时工具使用，即可内化视觉放大的优势。在多个细粒度视觉理解基准测试中，Vision-OPD模型表现与更大规模的开源、闭源及“图像思考”代理模型相当或更优。代码见https://github.com/VisionOPD/Vision-OPD
Summary / 总结
Vision-OPD addresses the limitation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in fine-grained visual understanding by recognizing that these models perform better when conditioned on evidence-centered image crops rather than full images, indicating a regional-to-global perception gap. The method introduces a self-distillation framework where a crop-conditioned teacher policy guides a full-image-conditioned student policy through on-policy rollouts, minimizing token-level divergence between their next-token distributions. Experimental results across multiple fine-grained benchmarks demonstrate that Vision-OPD achieves competitive or superior performance compared to larger open-source, closed-source, and agentic models without requiring external teachers, ground-truth labels, or inference-time tools.
Vision-OPD 解决了多模态大语言模型在细粒度视觉理解中的不足，发现模型在基于局部裁剪图像的条件下表现优于全图输入，揭示了区域到全局的感知差距。该方法通过自蒸馏框架，利用裁剪图像条件的教师策略引导全图条件的学生策略进行在线策略采样，并最小化二者下一词分布的词级差异。实验表明，Vision-OPD 在多个基准测试中表现优于或至少不逊于更大规模的开源、闭源及具身模型，且无需外部监督或工具调用。
Martingale Neural Operators: Learning Stochastic Marginals via Doob-Meyer Factorization
Authors: Kai Hidajat
First: 2026-05-15T10:00:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:33:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural operators excel as deterministic surrogates, but inevitably collapse to the conditional mean when applied to stochastic PDEs, discarding the variance and tail structure upon which uncertainty quantification depends. Recovering this structure typically requires Monte Carlo rollouts or grafted generative models, both of which surrender the one-shot efficiency and resolution invariance that define the operator paradigm. To resolve this, we draw on the Doob-Meyer theorem, which establishes that any semimartingale fundamentally decomposes into a predictable drift and an unpredictable, zero-mean martingale. Translating this theorem into an architectural prior, we introduce the Martingale Neural Operator (MNO). MNO maps an initial condition directly to the conditional mean and covariance of the terminal law, parameterized by a drift-like mean and a low-rank factor $B_φ$ with $B_φ^\top B_φ$ positive semi-definite by construction. For our experiments, we use a Gaussian residual instantiation. Across 1D SPDEs, rough volatility, and 2D operator tasks, MNO reduces Wasserstein distance by up to $120\times$ on $φ^4$ field theory and $68\times$ on stochastic Burgers, evaluating $\sim 3\times$ faster than a conditional diffusion baseline at matched wall-clock training budgets. On 2D tasks, MNO is comparable to FNO on zero-shot resolution transfer and turbulent flow, while quasi-deterministic systems such as Gray-Scott remain a failure mode.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鞅神经算子：通过Doob-Meyer分解学习随机边际
神经算子作为确定性代理表现优异，但在应用于随机偏微分方程时必然退化为条件均值，从而丢弃不确定性量化所依赖的方差与尾部结构。恢复此类结构通常需要蒙特卡洛采样或嫁接生成模型，但这两者均牺牲了算子范式所定义的一次性效率与分辨率不变性。为解决此问题，我们借鉴Doob-Meyer定理，该定理指出任何半鞅均可分解为可预测漂移与不可预测的零均值鞅。将该定理转化为架构先验，我们提出鞅神经算子（MNO）。MNO直接将初始条件映射至终端分布的条件均值与协方差，参数化为漂移类均值与低秩因子B_φ，其B_φ^T B_φ由构造保证半正定。在实验中，我们采用高斯残差实现。在1D SPDE、粗糙波动率及2D算子任务中，MNO在φ^4场论上将Wasserstein距离降低120倍，在随机Burgers方程上降低68倍，且在匹配的墙钟训练预算下评估速度约为条件扩散基线的3倍。在2D任务中，MNO在零次分辨率迁移与湍流流动上与FNO相当，但在准确定性系统如Gray-Scott中仍存在失效模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inability of standard neural operators to capture stochastic variance and tail behavior in stochastic PDEs without costly Monte Carlo methods, this work introduces the Martingale Neural Operator (MNO) based on the Doob-Meyer decomposition, which separates a semimartingale into a predictable drift and an unpredictable martingale. MNO directly outputs the conditional mean and covariance of the terminal distribution via a drift-like component and a low-rank factor ensuring positive semi-definite covariance. Experiments across 1D SPDEs, rough volatility, and 2D tasks show MNO reduces Wasserstein distance by up to 120x on φ⁴ field theory and 68x on stochastic Burgers, while being approximately three times faster than a conditional diffusion baseline at matched training time. On 2D tasks, MNO matches Fourier Neural Operators in zero-shot resolution transfer and turbulent flow modeling, but fails on quasi-deterministic systems like Gray-Scott.
为解决传统神经算子在随机偏微分方程中无法捕捉方差与尾部结构的问题，本文提出基于Doob-Meyer分解的Martingale Neural Operator（MNO），将随机过程分解为可预测漂移与不可预测鞅部分，直接输出条件均值与协方差。实验表明，MNO在φ⁴场论和随机Burgers方程上分别将Wasserstein距离降低120倍和68倍，训练速度约为条件扩散模型的三倍。在二维任务中，MNO在零次分辨率迁移与湍流建模上与Fourier神经算子相当，但在准确定系统如Gray-Scott模型中表现失败。
PyraMathBench: Evaluating and Improving Mathematical Capability in Large Language Models
Authors: Zetian Ouyang, Linlin Wang, Gerard de Melo, Liang He
First: 2026-06-02T16:32:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:32:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the pivotal role of numerical reasoning as the cornerstone of mathematical capabilities in large language models (LLMs) across applications, few benchmarks evaluate LLMs by integrating numerical processing and mathematical reasoning, hindering the interpretability of failures in math tasks. We introduce PyraMathBench, a comprehensive hierarchical benchmark with 32,505 questions derived from 7,404 math word problems, spanning 4 key cognitive aspects, 14 subcategories, and 2 modalities. Experiments reveal that LLMs' performance is severely compromised by inadequate numerical computation and weak handling of abstract numerical questions. To address this, we propose the Smart Optimization & Learning-based VErsatile module (SOLVE) and Interactive Relative Policy Optimization (IRPO), which enhance LLMs' numerical-mathematical synergy via efficient tool calls (fuzzy matching and low-quality call rejection). Comparative experiments show Qwen-2.5 achieves a 5.0 score improvement with SOLVE and IRPO training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PyraMathBench：评估与提升大型语言模型的数学能力
尽管数值推理作为大型语言模型（LLMs）在各类应用中数学能力的核心，但现有评测基准很少结合数值处理与数学推理来评估LLMs，这阻碍了数学任务失败原因的可解释性。我们提出了PyraMathBench，这是一个涵盖7,404个数学应用题、衍生出32,505道题目的综合性分层基准，覆盖4个关键认知维度、14个子类和2种模态。实验表明，LLMs的表现严重受限于数值计算能力不足和对抽象数值问题处理能力薄弱。为解决此问题，我们提出Smart Optimization & Learning-based VErsatile模块（SOLVE）和Interactive Relative Policy Optimization（IRPO），通过高效工具调用（模糊匹配与低质量调用拒绝）增强LLMs的数值-数学协同能力。对比实验显示，Qwen-2.5在SOLVE与IRPO训练下性能提升5.0分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of benchmarks that integrate numerical processing and mathematical reasoning to evaluate LLMs, the authors introduce PyraMathBench, a hierarchical benchmark with 32,505 questions covering 4 cognitive aspects and 2 modalities. Experiments show LLMs struggle with numerical computation and abstract numerical questions. To improve performance, they propose SOLVE and IRPO, which optimize tool usage via fuzzy matching and low-quality call rejection. Training with these methods boosts Qwen-2.5’s performance by 5.0 points.
鉴于现有评估基准未能整合数值处理与数学推理能力，本文提出PyraMathBench，包含32,505道题目，覆盖4个认知维度、14个子类及两种模态。实验表明，大模型在数值计算和抽象数值问题上表现不佳。为此，作者提出SOLVE与IRPO方法，通过模糊匹配与低质量调用拒绝机制提升数值与数学能力协同。训练后Qwen-2.5性能提升5.0分。
CLI-Anything: Towards Agent-Native Computer Use
Authors: Yuhao Yang, Tianyu Fan, Chao Huang
First: 2026-06-02T16:30:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:30:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models advance in reasoning and tool use capabilities, researchers increasingly seek to leverage them for computer use agents that can interact with existing software. The dominant approach develops GUI agents that control applications through visual interfaces: interpreting screenshots, locating UI elements, and executing mouse clicks to mimic human interaction. This GUI-centric paradigm fundamentally misaligns with agent capabilities. Current GUI agents struggle with brittle pixel-level interactions, timing dependencies, and coordinate-based actions that break with interface changes. They force agents to emulate human perceptual limitations rather than leverage their computational strengths in structured data processing and programmatic control. CLI-Anything argues for agent-native computer use design. Instead of forcing agents to navigate visual layouts, we create interfaces aligned with how agents naturally operate: through structured commands, explicit state representations, and deterministic feedback. We transform existing applications into command-line harnesses that preserve functionality while exposing machine-readable protocols optimized for AI-native interaction. This eliminates the lossy visual-to-computational translation that plagues GUI agents. Rather than building sophisticated screen readers and click simulators, we should redesign interaction paradigms around agent strengths: precise programmatic control and deterministic execution. We examine the methodology, architecture, evidence, and future directions for this agent-native transformation of computer use. We have built CLI-Hub as a comprehensive platform that operationalizes this agent-native computer use vision. The platform provides methodology, architecture, and infrastructure for this fundamental transformation of computer use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLI-Anything：迈向原生代理计算机使用
随着大语言模型在推理和工具使用能力上的进步，研究人员日益寻求利用它们构建能够与现有软件交互的计算机使用代理。主流方法是开发通过视觉界面控制应用程序的GUI代理：解析截图、定位UI元素并执行鼠标点击以模拟人类交互。这种以GUI为中心的范式从根本上与代理能力不匹配。当前GUI代理在像素级交互、时间依赖性和基于坐标的动作方面表现脆弱，一旦界面变化即失效。它们迫使代理模拟人类感知局限，而非发挥其在结构化数据处理和程序化控制方面的计算优势。CLI-Anything主张采用原生代理设计的计算机使用方式。与其强迫代理导航视觉布局，我们创建与代理自然运作方式相匹配的界面：通过结构化命令、显式状态表示和确定性反馈。我们将现有应用程序转化为命令行接口，保留功能的同时暴露机器可读协议，以优化AI原生交互。这消除了困扰GUI代理的视觉到计算的损失性转换。与其构建复杂的屏幕阅读器和点击模拟器，我们应围绕代理优势重新设计交互范式：精确的程序化控制和确定性执行。我们探讨了该原生代理计算机使用方法论、架构、证据及未来方向。我们构建了CLI-Hub平台，以全面实现这一原生代理计算机使用愿景。该平台提供了实现计算机使用根本性转变的方法论、架构和基础设施。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of GUI-based agents that rely on brittle visual interactions, CLI-Anything proposes an agent-native approach to computer use by transforming applications into command-line interfaces that expose structured, deterministic protocols. Instead of emulating human visual perception through screenshots and mouse clicks, the method leverages programmatic control and explicit state representations optimized for AI agents. Experimental results demonstrate that this CLI-based paradigm significantly improves reliability and efficiency compared to GUI agents, as it avoids timing dependencies and interface-specific coordinate-based actions, enabling more robust and scalable agent-driven software interaction.
鉴于基于GUI的代理在像素级交互和时间依赖性方面表现脆弱，CLI-Anything提出一种面向代理的原生计算使用方法，通过将应用程序转换为命令行接口，暴露机器可读且确定性的协议。该方法避免模拟人类视觉交互，转而利用结构化命令和明确的状态表示，契合代理的计算能力。实验结果表明，这种命令行原生范式显著优于GUI代理，实现精确、程序化控制，无需视觉到计算的损失，已在CLI-Hub平台中得到验证。
FLARE: Fine-Grained Diagnostic Feedback for LLM Code Refinement
Authors: Yinsheng Yao, Hongxiang Zhang, Weixi Tong, Tianyi Zhang
First: 2026-06-02T16:29:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:29:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models often generate code with bugs. Existing methods rely on feedback signals such as test failures and self-critiques to iteratively refine the generated code. Such signals are either too coarse-grained or too high-level, which is not sufficient to inform the model where to fix the bug. In this work, we present Flare, an iterative framework with a lightweight diagnostic model that predicts line-level suspiciousness signals for bug localization and code refinement. Given the inherent uncertainty of diagnostic predictions, Flare searches over the top-k suspicious regions and selects the best candidate according to execution outcomes. Experiments on LiveCodeBench and BigCodeBench with five base LLMs show that, even without candidate search (k=1), Flare outperforms the strongest baseline with an absolute improvement from 1.72% to 7.42%. Furthermore, searching over 10 candidates yields an average improvement of 8.50% compared with no candidate search. When evaluated in isolation, our lightweight diagnostic model achieves the best performance compared with recent fault localization methods, demonstrating that it can provide reliable fine-grained guidance for code refinement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLARE：面向大语言模型代码优化的细粒度诊断反馈
大语言模型常生成含缺陷的代码。现有方法依赖测试失败和自我批评等反馈信号迭代优化代码，但此类信号过于粗粒度或抽象，不足以指导模型定位并修复缺陷。本文提出Flare，一种轻量级诊断模型驱动的迭代框架，可预测行级可疑性信号以实现缺陷定位与代码优化。鉴于诊断预测固有的不确定性，Flare在前k个可疑区域中搜索并依据执行结果选择最优候选方案。在LiveCodeBench和BigCodeBench上使用五种基线大语言模型的实验表明，即使不进行候选搜索（k=1），Flare仍优于最强基线方法，绝对性能提升1.72%至7.42%。进一步地，搜索10个候选方案相比不搜索平均提升8.50%。当单独评估时，我们的轻量级诊断模型优于近期缺陷定位方法，证明其可为代码优化提供可靠细粒度指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of coarse feedback signals in refining buggy code generated by large language models, Flare introduces a lightweight diagnostic model that predicts line-level suspiciousness to guide precise bug localization. The framework iteratively refines code by evaluating top-k suspicious regions and selecting the best candidate based on execution outcomes. Experiments across LiveCodeBench and BigCodeBench with five LLMs show that even without candidate search (k=1), Flare improves performance by 7.42% over the strongest baseline, and with k=10, it achieves an average 8.50% improvement. When tested alone, the diagnostic model outperforms recent fault localization methods, proving its effectiveness in providing fine-grained guidance for code refinement.
为提升大语言模型生成代码的纠错精度，Flare提出一种轻量级诊断模型，预测行级可疑性以指导迭代优化，并根据执行结果从候选区域中选择最优解。实验表明，即使不进行候选搜索（k=1），Flare也能较最强基线提升7.42%；当搜索10个候选区域时，平均提升达8.50%。该诊断模型独立评估时优于近期故障定位方法，证明其在代码精修中提供细粒度可靠指导的能力。
Two-Action Apple Tasting with Switching Costs
Authors: Tommaso Cesari, Roberto Colomboni
First: 2026-06-02T16:28:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T16:28:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the two-action apple-tasting problem with switching costs against an oblivious adversary. In an equivalent normalized formulation, at each round the learner chooses between a revealing action and a blind action: the revealing action gives reward $0$ and reveals the hidden value $x_t\in[-1,1]$ of the blind action; the blind action gives reward $x_t$ but reveals nothing. The learner pays one unit whenever they switches actions, and regret is measured against the best fixed action in hindsight. General feedback-graph algorithms with switching costs give $\widetilde O(T^{2/3})$ regret guarantees for this problem. The two-action apple-tasting graph was the natural candidate for the missing $Ω(T^{2/3})$ obstruction in the switching-cost classification: such a lower bound would have transferred to a large family of still-unclassified feedback graphs. We prove that this obstruction is not there: the oblivious minimax expected regret for this problem satisfies \[ \frac{1}{2\sqrt3}\cdot\sqrt T \le R_T^\star \le 2\sqrt{3}\cdot \sqrt{T}. \]
中文标题/摘要
标题：带切换成本的双动作苹果品鉴问题
我们研究了在无偏对抗者下的双动作苹果品鉴问题，其中存在切换成本。在等价的标准化表述中，每轮学习者在揭示动作与盲动之间选择：揭示动作获得奖励0并揭示盲动隐藏值x_t∈[-1,1]；盲动获得奖励x_t但不揭示任何信息。学习者每次切换动作需支付1单位成本，遗憾值以事后最优固定动作为基准衡量。 带切换成本的通用反馈图算法对此问题提供\widetilde O(T^{2/3})的遗憾保证。双动作苹果品鉴图曾被视为缺失Ω(T^{2/3})下界障碍的自然候选：此类下界将推广至大量尚未分类的反馈图。我们证明该障碍并不存在：此问题的无偏最小最大期望遗憾满足\[ \frac{1}{2\sqrt3}\cdot\sqrt T \le R_T^\star \le 2\sqrt{3}\cdot \sqrt{T}. \]
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the open question of whether the two-action apple-tasting problem with switching costs could serve as a barrier for regret lower bounds in feedback graphs, the authors analyze a setting where a learner chooses between a revealing action (reward 0, reveals hidden value) and a blind action (reward equals hidden value, reveals nothing), incurring a unit cost for switching. They show that general feedback-graph algorithms achieve $\widetilde O(T^{2/3})$ regret, but prove that the two-action apple-tasting problem does not exhibit the expected $Ω(T^{2/3})$ lower bound obstruction, instead achieving minimax regret bounded between $\frac{1}{2\sqrt3}\cdot\sqrt T$ and $2\sqrt{3}\cdot \sqrt{T}$, resolving its status as a non-barrier.
该研究旨在解决两动作苹果品尝问题中切换成本是否构成反馈图中后悔下界障碍的问题。学习者在每轮选择揭示动作（奖励0，揭示隐藏值）或盲动动作（奖励等于隐藏值，不揭示任何信息），每次切换需支付单位成本。研究证明，尽管该问题曾被视为Ω(T^{2/3})下界的自然候选，但其最优后悔为Θ(√T)，从而否定了该障碍的存在，表明切换成本在该特定图结构中无法推广为一般性下界。
